EXEMPTIONS / RATCHET: no drain, no ratchet edit. None of the six is in `EXEMPT_BASELINE_PAIRS` — reverb's linux face pairs were already drained in the P1-batch-3 wave (vrt-exemptions.ts:1053-1058) and its PF-8 re-capture is recorded at `:1033` ("delay/mixer/reverb 421→398"), so both platforms are current. `vrt-meta.test.ts`'s linux-deficit ceiling (`:434-458`) is UNTOUCHED — nothing becomes exempt, nothing stops being exempt.

PROCEDURE: regen darwin locally (`REPEAT=3 flox activate -- task vrt:one -- reverb`, then inspect the PNG diffs — a face change is look-affecting), commit, then dispatch `flox activate -- gh workflow run vrt-update.yml -f ref=<branch> -f platform=linux` **UNSCOPED** (`-f grep=…` dies as `startup_failure`). The bot's push lands the follow-on runs in `action_required`, not `queued` — approve them. And treat a green dispatch that committed nothing as a RED FLAG, not as "nothing to do".

STRUCTURAL PAGE COUNT: `e2e/vrt/workflow-shell-faces.spec.ts:63` `{ type: 'reverb', pages: 2 }` → `pages: 1`, in the same commit. That assert (`:222`) runs BEFORE the pixel pin, so forgetting it fails first with a readable message. This matches plan:1312's "reverb 2→**1**".

**CI delta (claimed):** ≈ +2.5 s total; well under the 2-minute sign-off threshold, but state the number in the PR body anyway and CONFIRM IT ON CI (faces-parity's ~0.8 s/cell is a CI measurement — plan:1319 — and CI runs SwiftShader at ~4× the local per-cell cost). Breakdown: faces-parity gains exactly ONE cell (3 params → 4, no families) at ~0.8 s/cell = +0.8 s — this matches plan:1325's "reverb +1" but note that row's "+2.4 s" is the sum for qbrt+delay+reverb, not reverb alone. ART gains two extra offline Faust renders (the not-inert negative control below) at ~0.6 s each ≈ +1.2 s in the `art` job. VRT scene COUNT is unchanged in all three lanes (vrt-strict still runs one `reverb` card scene; workflow-shell-faces still runs 2 reverb scenes) — only baseline BYTES move, which is free. Unit lane: +0 (contract-lock, module-face-lint, param-vocabulary and module-docs-lint are all pure registry reads that already enumerate reverb). No new e2e spec, no new browser boot.

**Risks (as authored):**
- **AUDIO RISK — this is the one thing in the whole face program that can move audio (plan:1233), so it is OWNER-PREVIEW, never auto-merge.** The byte-identity claim is a HYPOTHESIS, not a measurement, and the plan states it as if it were settled. The argument for it is strong (identity mapping; 0.5 is exactly representable; every constant fold Faust could do on the old literal is exact), but the gate that decides is empirical. VERIFY LIKE THIS, in this order: `rm -rf packages/dsp/dist && flox activate -- task dsp:build` (a REAL build — NOT `dsp:fetch-dist`, which would hand you the pre-change wasm; `renderFaustOffline` refuses a stale dist at faust-offline.ts:104-111 by comparing dist .sha to source .sha, so a fetched dist fails loudly rather than silently), then `flox activate -- task art:update`, then `git status`. **ONLY `art/baselines/reverb/audio.sha` may appear.** If `audio.f32` is also modified, the DSP edit changed the audio and you must STOP and get an owner audition — do not re-pin. ⚠ The ART gate cannot tell you this by itself: `assertBaseline` (art/setup/capture.ts:176-190) checks the source SHA FIRST and fails there, and `art:update` then rewrites BOTH files, so a real audio change is laundered into a green re-pin. `git diff --name-only` is the actual instrument. Corollary: if `.f32` is byte-identical, `fingerprints.generated.json` does not move at all (its `sourceSha256` for `reverb/audio` — currently `208fc84182ff845a…` at line 6434 — is the sha256 of the `.f32`), so a fingerprint diff is itself a signal that the audio moved.
- **A DEF↔DSP PARAM-NAME TYPO ON `diffusion` IS INVISIBLE TO EVERY GATE IN THE REPO.** The factory does `params.get(`${PARAM_PREFIX}/${def.id}`)?.setValueAtTime(...)` (reverb.ts:145,152) — an optional chain. Spell the hslider `diffuse` in the `.dsp` and the def keeps `diffusion` and the knob is DEAD, silently. faces-parity's knob drive (faces-parity.spec.ts:315-325) asserts only that the value changed in the GRAPH (`readParam` polls the store), never that the DSP heard it. There is NO test anywhere that reconciles a Faust def's `params[]` against the compiled `dist/<name>.json` addresses — I looked; this hole is shared by filter, mixer, vca, qbrt and mixmstrs. MITIGATION, cheap and in this PR: add a SECOND, UNPINNED ART test — `it('DIFFUSION is not inert — the allpass feedback changes the tail')` — that calls `renderFaustOffline` twice with `params: { …, diffusion: 0 }` and `{ …, diffusion: 0.9 }` and asserts (a) the two tails differ materially and (b) both are finite and bounded. It negative-controls the new knob AND proves the address resolves, because `renderFaustOffline` THROWS on an unknown shortname (faust-offline.ts:141-147). Keep it OUT of the pinned `renderProfile()` so the pin stays an honest statement about the DEFAULT render.
- **DOC DRIFT THE NEW PARAM SILENTLY FALSIFIES — three existing sentences become conditional and one of them is in two files.** `docs.explanation` (reverb.ts:117) and `docs.controls.damp` (`:130`) both assert that at DAMP 1 'all that is left is the ~0.14 s of allpass diffusion, whatever SIZE says' — that 0.14 s is a FUNCTION of the allpass feedback, which `diffusion` now controls, so the figure is now the diffusion-0.5 case only. The same claim is in `module-manifest.ts:227` DESCRIPTIONS ('only ~0.14 s of allpass diffusion is left'), which ALSO says 'three knobs, no CV'. Plus the def's own header (`:9` 'No CV; the three knobs are the entire user surface'), the Params block comment (`:33-36`), and the face comment (`:66-67` 'there are three knobs and no menu'). SIX places. Only `docs.controls.diffusion` is enforced (module-docs-lint.test.ts:171-173, STRICT_DOCS completeness — reverb is at strict-docs.ts:70); the other five are quiet rot, and this repo has already caught ~25 authored doc claims that were wrong against the real DSP. Fix all six in the same diff, and re-measure the DAMP-1 residual at diffusion 0 / 0.5 / 0.95 rather than guessing a new number.
- **A GREEN LOCAL BUILD DOES NOT PROVE A GREEN CI BUILD OF THE SAME BYTES.** The wasm compiler is pinned by `@grame/faustwasm ^0.7.0` (packages/dsp/package.json:18) — a CARET range — and every committed `dist/*.json` reports libfaust 2.78.3. package-lock.json holds it steady today, but this is worth naming because the def header (reverb.ts:14) documents its measured behaviour against 'Faust 2.85.5 C backend' while the shipping wasm is 2.78.3 — two different compilers already coexist in this module's story. If a lockfile bump lands concurrently, the `.f32` moves for a reason that has nothing to do with `diffusion` and the byte-identity check reads as a false alarm. Build from the locked deps and say so in the PR body.
- **THE FULL-TIER GLYPH LOSS HAS NO GATE.** module-face-lint.test.ts:677-694 compares only `cellCount` (4 selected == 4 rendered) and never inspects `laneBodyPlan(...).glyph`. So the meter silently vanishing from the in-lane plate is invisible to CI and will be rediscovered as a bug by whoever opens the lane at zoom 0.6 next month. It must be in the face comment AND the commit body. This is the same shape as delay's priced trade (plan:731-733) and the two land in the same batch — say it once, in both.
- **MERGE-ORDER HAZARD ON THE SHIMMERSHINE VOCABULARY TIE.** reverb.ts:84-87 justifies its band names by pointing at shimmershine's. Batch B (this) merges before Batch D (shimmershine), and Batch D is the PR that folds shimmershine's `mix` into ITS `tank` band (plan:857). If the rewritten reverb comment claims present-tense parity it is false for the whole window between the two merges. Write it as a forward reference ('shimmershine's Batch-D face folds its blend into `tank` the same way') or drop the claim to structure only ('same page id, same membership shape').
- **CONFLICT SURFACE.** Batch B ships five modules; `contract-lock.txt` and `e2e/vrt/workflow-shell-faces.spec.ts`'s FACES table are both edited by qbrt, delay and reverb in the same wave. After any Batch-B merge run `flox activate -- task pr:conflict-sweep`, and NEVER `gh pr update-branch` — it silently drops additions on shared registry files. On a contract-lock conflict take main and re-run `task docs:accept`; never hand-merge it.
- **PER-PR VERIFICATION (CLAUDE.md, non-negotiable).** `task test:one -- module-face-lint` · `task test:one -- param-vocabulary` (the landmarks) · `task test:one -- curated-face` · `task test:one -- module-docs-lint` · `task test:one -- rear-card-model` (expected: unchanged, but prove it) · `task docs:check` · `REPEAT=3 flox activate -- npx --workspace e2e playwright test faces-parity --grep reverb` · `REPEAT=3 flox activate -- task e2e:one -- workflow-shell-faces` · `REPEAT=3 flox activate -- task art:one -- reverb` (the new not-inert test is a NEW test: 3× in a row, no flakes) · `REPEAT=3 flox activate -- task vrt:one -- reverb` and INSPECT the PNG diffs · `flox activate -- task typecheck` (svelte-check is stricter than vitest, and this PR edits a .svelte card). Also run `flox activate -- npx --workspace e2e playwright test coverage-groups-6-7-8-9 --grep reverb` — it spawns reverb with named params (`:36`) and is safe by construction, but it is the only bespoke e2e that touches this module and it is cheap.

**Counts independently re-derived by the fact-checker:**

> Independently derived from packages/web/src/lib/audio/modules/reverb.ts — INPUTS 1: `audio` (type audio, :53). OUTPUTS 1: `audio` (type audio, :54). PARAMS 3 (:56-58): size 0..1 linear default 0.5 label 'Size'; damp 0..1 linear default 0.3 label 'Damp'; mix 0..1 linear default 0.3 label 'Mix'. controlFamilies 0. exposableControls 0. stereoPairs 0. face.order 3 ['mix','size','damp'] (:96). face.pages 2 — {id:'tank',label:'reverb tank',controls:[size,damp]} (:98), {id:'output',label:'output blend',controls:[mix]} (:99). face.glyph 'meter' (:101). face.rear.groups 1 — {id:'signal',label:'mono in',ports:['audio']} (:111). face.momentary/paramCells/clusters/audioRate: absent. contract-lock.txt reverb block = 6 lines (2659-2664). packages/dsp/src/reverb.dsp = 19 lines, 3 hslider (:6,:7,:8) and TWO hardcoded constants — fb2=0.5 (:15) and spread=0.5 (:18, 4th arg of re.mono_freeverb). ReverbCard.svelte = 39 lines, 3 Faders (:29-31), width 200px (:37). POST-PR the spec implies: 4 params, 7 contract lines, 4 ranks, 1 page. The spec's own counts (1 in / 1 out / 3→4 params / 6→7 contract lines / pages 2→1) all AGREE with mine; the defects below are elsewhere.

**DEFECTS — fix these before building:**

- **[BLOCKER]** The ONE concrete DSP edit instruction points at the wrong line, and a second citation points past end-of-file. The spec says: "replace `fb2 = 0.5;` (reverb.dsp:18) with `fb2 = hslider(\"diffusion[style:knob]\", 0.5, 0.0, 0.95, 0.001);`" and separately "the Faust tank sums eight combs with no output scaling (reverb.dsp:20 → `re.mono_freeverb`)".
  - *evidence:* packages/dsp/src/reverb.dsp is 19 lines total. Line 15 is `  fb2 = 0.5;                              // fixed allpass feedback`. Line 18 is `  wet = re.mono_freeverb(fb1, fb2, d, 0.5, audio);` — i.e. the line the spec names as the edit target is the mono_freeverb call, not the constant. Line 20 DOES NOT EXIST. The same wrong :18 is repeated in newParams item 1 ("the hardcoded literal `fb2 = 0.5` at packages/dsp/src/reverb.dsp:18") and item 2. An implementer following the line number edits the wrong statement; this is the only file in the PR whose edit can change audio.

- **[MAJOR]** "`reverb.dsp` has exactly three `hslider`s and one hidden constant; after this PR the def↔DSP surface is 1:1 with zero un-exposed capability" is FALSE — there are TWO hidden constants — and the spec omits the `room`/`spread` rejection the plan explicitly requires it to state.
  - *evidence:* packages/dsp/src/reverb.dsp:18 `wet = re.mono_freeverb(fb1, fb2, d, 0.5, audio);` passes a hardcoded `0.5` as the 4th argument. The stdlib signature (extracted from node_modules/@grame/faustwasm/libfaust-wasm/libfaust-wasm.data) is `mono_freeverb(fb1, fb2, damp, spread) = _ <: par(i,8,lbcf(combtuningL(i)+spread,fb1,damp)) :> seq(i,4,allpass_comb(1024, allpasstuningL(i)+spread, -fb2))` with `// spread = spatial spread in number of samples (for stereo)`. So `spread` remains un-exposed after the PR. The plan's reverb §4 bullet at .myrobots/plans/dx7-and-faces-design-program-2026-07-27.md:753 mandates the statement "**`room` (Faust `spread`) is REJECTED** — see §2's demotion table. Reverb's contract cost is **+1, not +2**" (argued at plan:120). The spec never mentions room/spread anywhere.

- **[MAJOR]** The spec calls the pinned ART render "the DEFAULT render" and builds its entire byte-identity verification protocol on that framing. The pinned render is not at defaults.

Def defaults (reverb.ts:56-58) are size 0.5 / damp 0.3 / mix 0.3, so TWO of three are off-default (size +0.35 → fb1 = 0.5+0.45*0.85 = 0.8825, not 0.725). The spec's risks item 2 says "Keep it OUT of the pinned `renderProfile()` so the pin stays an honest statement about the DEFAULT render" — the pin has never been a default-render statement.

- **[MAJOR]** The spec asserts coverage-groups-6-7-8-9 "is the only bespoke e2e that touches this module". There are at least two others, and one of them is HARD-COUPLED to the exact DOM the spec's mandatory ReverbCard edit rewrites — and would break outright under the `size`→`Decay` relabel the spec discusses at length.

The spec's mandatory remedy rewrites that row (4th Fader, `.fader-row` → `display:flex; justify-content:center; gap:12px`, card widened). The `aria-label="Size"` selector also dies the moment the label becomes 'Decay' (plan:1455 OPEN item 6) — the spec argues the relabel purely on contract/VRT grounds and never names this selector. Also e2e/tests/docs-virtual-module.spec.ts:177-184 carries a reverb row (`controlParam: 'size'`, `cvPort: ''`, comment "reverb has no CV inputs (three knobs only)").

So the spec's own `[{0:'flutter'},{0.5:'classic'},{0.95:'smear'}]` roster renders in the shell dock and is invisible on the legacy card that legacy-fallback.ts:106-108 (`laneRenderKind`: `if (!i.workflowMode || !i.shellPreview || !i.hasCard) return 'legacy'`) serves to every dawless user. [])].sort(byId))` is :108, `byId` (localeCompare) is :73. rear-card-model.ts:297 cited for the empty-band drop → `if (band.holes.length > 0) bands.push(band);` is :299. coverage-groups-6-7-8-9.spec.ts:36 cited for the named-param spawn → :37. module-face-lint.test.ts:677 cited for the cellCount gate → :677 is `const LANE_TIERS`, the `it(...)` is :678. legacy-fallback.ts:32-38 cited for the dawless-always-legacy rule → :32-38 is the `LaneRenderKind` type + snowflake header; the rule is the file header :20-22 and `laneRenderKind` at :106-108. "docs promise 'the tail always dies — 0.95 is the ceiling' (reverb.ts:20, docs.controls.size)" conflates two places — :20 is the file-header comment; the docs.controls.size sentence is reverb.ts:128.

- **[MINOR]** The allpass tap figures are quoted as absolute sample counts without stating the sample rate they belong to, while the rest of the spec (and the ART harness) runs at the module's live SR.

The stdlib (libfaust-wasm.data, mono_freeverb `with{}` block) defines `origSR = 44100; allpasstuningL(0) = 556*SR/origSR : int; (1) = 441…; (2) = 341…` — the taps RESCALE with SR (reverb.ts:16-17 and docs.explanation both say so). The millisecond figures survive the rescale; the sample counts are the 44.1 kHz values only (at 48 kHz they are 245/371/480/605).

## shimmershine

**Verdict: NEEDS_WORK** · archetype: Time-based stereo FX insert — the reverb/delay/cloudseed archetype (`category: 'effects'`, `palette: {top:'Audio modules', sub:'Effects'}`, `glyph: 'meter'`, `stereoPairs: [[in_l,in_r],[out_l,out_r]]`, shimmershine.ts:211-216). Structurally: a Schroeder tank you tune + ONE gain that regenerates it + ONE gain that blends it out. Zero sources, zero gates, zero discrete params, zero control families, zero card-only buttons — the simplest face in Batch D by a wide margin.

**Rationale (as authored):** WHAT IT IS FOR: you reach for SHIMMERSHINE over REVERB or CLOUDSEED for exactly one reason — the octave-up regeneration loop that turns a decaying room into a self-sustaining crystalline drone. The verb is RIDING that threshold. Every rank below descends from that sentence.

ORDER — and an honest statement of how little of it is load-bearing. Five controls with a glyph means `order` decides exactly TWO things: who is the mini hero (cap 1) and who joins at compact (`LANE_ROW_MAX_CELLS_WITH_GLYPH = 2`). At 'full' the cap is 6 and the face has 5, so all five render regardless of rank; ranks 3-5 differ only in PLATE POSITION (row 1 = ranks 1-3, row 2 = 4-5). At 'dock' the layout comes from `pages`, not `order`. So there is exactly ONE contested slot and I will not dress up the other four as decisions.
· Rank 1 `shimmer` — kept. `fbGain = shimmer * FB_CAP` (shimmershine.ts:194, dsp:250) IS the regeneration path; at 0 this module is literally the plain REVERB, which is the one thing that makes it a different module. It has to survive to the smallest tile.
· Rank 2 `damp` (from rank 5) — the one real call, and it is DSP-grounded twice over. (a) It is the ONLY param with no CV jack (shimmershine.ts:221-225 declares decay_cv/shimmer_cv/size_cv/mix_cv and nothing else), so every other knob can be driven from a cable and this one can only be reached by hand. (b) `_CombLP.tick` is `fbStore = fbStore*damp + y*(1-damp)` (:63, mirrored dsp:66), so at damp 1 the store never takes new signal, comb feedback stops outright, and the tail collapses to ~0.15 s RT60 whatever DECAY and SIZE are doing (:314). On a module deliberately parked ON a self-oscillation threshold, that is simultaneously the tone control and the panic button. SHIMMER + DAMP at compact is "make the drone / kill the drone" — the pair a player actually rides.
· Ranks 3-5 `mix, size, decay` — `decay` last because `effSize = size * (0.5 + 0.5*decay)` (:192, dsp:248): a dependent scaler cannot outrank the parameter it scales, and it does nothing at all at size 0. `mix` at 3 puts the three "ride" controls in plate row 1 and the two "set the room" controls in row 2.

PAGES — two bands, by ARCHITECTURE, and I INVERT the plan's split because the plan got both ambiguous members backwards.
The plan proposes `{loop: shimmer, damp}` + `{tank: size, decay, mix}`, arguing that "damp lives inside each comb's feedback loop, so filing it under 'reverb tank' actively misteaches" (plan:857-859). That conflates TWO different loops. The COMB loop is inside the tank — the combs ARE the tank (`SchroederTank.tick` runs 4 combs then 2 allpasses, :93-103). The SHIMMER loop is around the tank: `wet → shifter.tick(wet) → fb = tanh(shifted × shimmer×0.55) → tank input` (dsp:262-269) and contains NO filter whatsoever. `damp` is a one-pole LP inside each comb's feedback path (:63) — filing it under "reverb tank" is exactly right. Meanwhile `mix` in the tank band has no defence at all: it is the output crossfade applied after everything (`out = dry*(1-mix) + wet*mix`, dsp:271) and the def's own doc says "The tank and the shimmer loop run regardless of where MIX sits" (:316). So the plan misplaced BOTH, and simply moving `mix` out leaves `{loop: shimmer, damp, mix}` — three different ideas in one band, which §1 of the plan itself rejects.
My split, and the grouping argument that makes it ONE idea rather than a merge: `wet` is computed once (dsp:262) and tapped exactly TWICE — once back INTO the tank through the +12 shifter, once OUT through the crossfade. SHIMMER and MIX are those two taps. One variable, two destinations.
· `tank` = size, decay, damp — everything that shapes the coefficient `fb = 0.70 + 0.18 × (size × (0.5 + 0.5×decay))` (dsp:105) plus the LP inside it. Control order follows the composition: set it, scale it, filter it. (The CURRENT page has `['decay','size','damp']` — decay before the size it scales, which reads backwards.)
· `loop` = shimmer, mix — everything outside the tank.

BAND COUNT 3 → 2, JUSTIFIED FROM THE COMMITTED PNG, NOT FROM ARITHMETIC (plan anti-pattern #1). I opened `e2e/vrt/__screenshots__/workflow-shell-faces.spec.ts/darwin/face-shimmershine-dock.png`: REVERB TANK and SHIMMER LOOP render, and the third band OUTPUT BLEND is **clipped at the frame edge — its MIX knob half-drawn, its label entirely below the fold.** That corroborates the independently-measured "shimmershine 58" overflow recorded at vrt-exemptions.ts:1031. A band's pitch in that image is ~90 px (header y≈203 → 293 → 383), so dropping one band puts the whole face inside the cap with room to spare. The grouping argument stands on its own; this is the second, measured reason. It also kills two ONE-CONTROL bands, which the plan convicts everywhere else (delay's `output blend`, dx7's `cartridge`).

WHAT I VERIFIED THIS BUILDS AGAINST (every gate, by hand):
· face-lint consistency + completeness: 5 params ranked, 0 families, 0 legend statics (no shimmershine.legend.json exists — only adsr/lfo/sequencer do), every page control in `order`. ✓
· dockFacePlan parity: pages claim all 5 keys exactly once → no `__unpaged` tail, each param renders 1×, zero static cells. ✓
· lane cap ↔ fit plan: mini 1/1, compact 2/2, full 5/5 (plate, 2 rows, glyph dropped). No drift. ✓
· rear totality, derived by hand: curated `signal` claims in_l+in_r; page `tank` takes size_cv+decay_cv (page-control order → SIZE, DECAY); page `loop` takes shimmer_cv+mix_cv; rail takes out_l+out_r with the stereo tie on out_r. 8 holes = 8 declared ports, no duplicates, no orphan `cv` band. ✓
· faces-parity: 5 `control-*` testids, multiset-equal to the def; all five resolve to the `knob` drive branch (linear, non-switch, no options, no paramCells) — no new `driveCell` branch needed. ✓
· jack labels: `expandStem` already yields IN L / IN R / OUT L / OUT R (`DIRECTION_SUFFIX = /_(?:in|out)$/` doesn't match, `_`→space does, patch-panel-labels.ts:166-183) and the four CV holes label from their target params via `rearHoleLabel`. **PF-4 is not needed here.**

CORRECTIONS TO THE PLAN, worth more than the spec:
(a) plan:849 says "`damp` 4 → 2". It is rank **5** today — `order: ['shimmer','mix','decay','size','damp']`, shimmershine.ts:267. 5→2.
(b) plan:855-859's page split is DSP-wrong on both ambiguous members (above). This is the substantive correction.
(c) plan:866 prices `interval` and `damp_cv` with an "ART re-pin". There is NO shimmershine ART baseline and no source-SHA pin — the price is zero. Same for the `damp_cv` "⚠ verify ART, do not assume".
(d) plan:868 cites `vrt-exemptions.ts:1011-1012`; the pairs are at **:1012-1013**.
(e) plan:846 makes Batch D depend on PF-1/PF-4/PF-9. **shimmershine needs NONE of them** — no discrete param (so no `options`), no bad jack derivation (so no `PortDef.label`), no sub-grouping (so no page clusters). Its face is buildable on today's `main` with zero platform work and should LEAD Batch D, not trail it.
(f) THE DEF'S OWN COMMENT IS STALE: shimmershine.ts:252 says "full **(8)**". PF-7 landed — `faceTierCap('full')` is `PLATE_COLS * PLATE_MAX_ROWS = 6` (curated-face.ts:46,65). And :249-252 never mentions that the meter DROPS at 'full'. Both lines must be rewritten in this commit, plus the "Dock bands follow SIGNAL FLOW … Three small bands" paragraph (:254-258) which this change falsifies.

THE CARD-vs-DEF BUG I FOUND (report, not spec input). `ShimmershineCard.svelte:14-18` resolves defaults **POSITIONALLY** — `shimmershineDef.params[0]!.defaultValue` … `params[4]` — while :31-35 **re-types** `min={0} max={1} defaultValue={0.6} curve="linear"` as literals in the markup. The numbers happen to agree with the def today, so this is not a live divergence. But `contract-signature.ts:107` sorts params **by id** before projecting, so re-ordering the `params` array is CONTRACT-TRANSPARENT: it would silently rebind all five faders' defaults to the wrong knobs with contract-lock, module-docs-lint and the ART scenario all green, and nothing in the repo reads both sides. That is the backdraft class one gate short of firing. Remedy: look defaults up by id (`params.find(p => p.id === 'decay')`), and delete the hardcoded min/max/curve — the card already imports the def three lines above. Two smaller drifts in the same family, since prose contradicting the code beneath it is anti-pattern (6): `packages/dsp/src/shimmershine.ts:32` ("comb feedback self-caps at 0.92") and :248 ("size=1, decay=1 → comb fb ≈ 0.92") both contradict `SchroederTank.tick` at :100-105, where `fb = 0.70 + 0.18*size` maxes at **0.88** — the def's mirror (:94-97, "pulled back from 0.92") and the authored docs (:312) have it right.

VERIFICATION BEFORE THE MR (CLAUDE.md, non-negotiable): `flox activate -- task test:one -- module-face-lint`, `… test:one -- rear-card-model`, `… test:one -- contract-lock` (expect NO diff), `… test:one -- vrt-meta`; `REPEAT=3 flox activate -- task e2e:one -- "faces-parity"` grepped to shimmershine; `REPEAT=3 flox activate -- task vrt:one -- shimmershine` AFTER `git rm`-ing the two darwin baselines; `flox activate -- task typecheck`.

```ts
face: {
  order: ["shimmer", "damp", "mix", "size", "decay"],
  pages: [
    { id: "tank", label: "reverb tank \u00b7 damp is inside the combs", controls: ["size", "decay", "damp"] },
    { id: "loop", label: "the tank output \u00b7 fed back, then blended out", controls: ["shimmer", "mix"] },
  ],
  glyph: "meter \u2014 UNCHANGED. `glyphBinding` (shell-glyph-live.ts:83-105) resolves 'meter' \u2192 `{kind:'live-audio', portId:'out_l'}` because `primaryAudioOutPortId` returns the first `audio` output (:86) and shimmershine declares out_l first (shimmershine.ts:228). The `tail` glyph proposed for this module is DEMOTED in the plan (\u00a72, line 117) on three independent defects. \u26a0 TIER TRUTH THE DEF'S OWN COMMENT OMITS: with 5 controls + a glyph, `laneBodyPlan(5, true, 'full')` (module-shell-model.ts:328-342) goes to the plate, `rows = ceil(5/3) = 2`, and `glyph = hasGlyph && rows <= 1` \u2192 **the meter does NOT render at the full-in-lane tier today and will not after this edit**. It shows at mini, at compact (2 knobs + meter), and as the dock hero. That is pre-existing, not a regression, but shimmershine.ts:249-252 never says it \u2014 fix the comment in the same commit.",
  paramCells: none — `face.paramCells` is omitted. All five params are `curve: 'linear'` (shimmershine.ts:232-236); module-face-lint's clause (d) (:504-511) requires a DISCRETE param with 2..GRID_MAX_CELLS integer steps, so a declaration here would fail the gate. Nothing else is declarable: `'toggle'`/`'segmented'`/`'selector'` are DERIVED (from `looksLikeToggle` / a declared `options` roster), not declared. `face.momentary` is likewise omitted and must stay omitted — `looksLikeToggle` requires `curve === 'discrete'` (group-controls.ts), so no shimmershine param is switch-shaped and the STRICT_FACES unclassified-switch ratchet (module-face-lint.test.ts:418-438) has nothing to classify.,
}
```

**New params:**
- NONE. Diffed the def's params against the worklet's `parameterDescriptors` (packages/dsp/src/shimmershine.ts:193-201): decay/shimmer/size/damp/mix, 0..1, defaults 0.6/0.4/0.6/0.4/0.4 — EXACTLY 1:1 with shimmershine.ts:232-236. Zero un-exposed DSP capability. That is a finding, not a shortfall (plan §7 Step 8).
- DEFERRED, own PR — `interval` (the +12 shifter rate, today the literal `2.0` at shimmershine.ts:190 and dsp/shimmershine.ts:224). Would be +1 contract-lock line, +1 `docs.controls` entry, +1 face rank (6 controls still fits the plate cap of 6; the meter is already dropped at 'full' so nothing further is lost), +1 faces-parity cell ≈ +0.8 s. ⚠ THE PLAN IS WRONG ON ITS PRICE: it says 'default must be 2.0; ART re-pin' (plan:866). There is NO `art/baselines/shimmershine` — `ls art/baselines | grep shimmer` is empty and `art/scenarios/shimmershine/octave-up-tail.test.ts` is a pure-math Goertzel assertion over `shimmershineMath`, with no `.f32` and no `docsStrippedRepoSourceSha`. So: ZERO re-pin. The default must stay 2.0 for a different reason — that scenario asserts 880 Hz band energy from a 440 Hz burst and would go red at any other default.
- DEFERRED, own PR — `damp_cv` input (`{id:'damp_cv', type:'cv', paramTarget:'damp', cvScale:{mode:'linear'}}`). +1 contract-lock line (`shimmershine in damp_cv cv param=damp cvScale=linear`), rear hole count 8→9 (the DAMP hole lands in the `tank` band by derivation), and it FALSIFIES this spec's rear label 'tank cv · DAMP has no jack' — that label must change in the same PR. `damp` is already k-rate in the worklet so the jack behaves exactly like the other four. ZERO ART cost (same reason as above; the plan's '⚠ verify ART, do not assume' resolves to 'there is nothing to verify'). ⚠ It also removes the primary argument for DAMP at rank 2 — re-rank in that PR.
- DEFERRED, noted not proposed — two more hidden constants: `FB_CAP = 0.55` (shimmershine.ts:193 / dsp:217) and the 25 ms crossfade window (shimmershine.ts:190 / dsp:224).

**Control-loss ledger:**
- NONE. Verified line by line against the whole 48-line `ShimmershineCard.svelte`: 5 `<Fader>` (:31-35), `<PatchPanel>` (:29), `<ModuleTitle>` (:27). The plan §7 Step-7 grep (`<button|<select|<input|oncontextmenu|manualTrigger|Toggle|Selector|format`) returns ZERO hits. Matches the plan's own ledger row (§5, line 1197: 'mixer · delay · reverb · vca · adsr · shimmershine · tomtom — NONE, verified line by line').
- NOT A LOSS, stated because it looks like one: the card paints vertical <Fader>s, the shell paints <KnobConic>. Both are MIDI-assignable and BOTH are live-motorized — the card passes `readLive={live('<id>')}` (:31-35) and the shell passes `readLive={params.live(pd.id)}` on every param cell (ModuleShell.svelte:457/478/502/522/539). No readback is lost.
- NOT A LOSS: the card's `<PatchPanel>` yellow drill-down for all 8 ports is replaced in the dock by the RearCard on TAB (PF-8 removed the duplicate dock lane rail). All 8 holes are still reachable — I hand-derived the rear plan below and it is TOTAL.
- DELIBERATELY DROPPED, nothing: no control moves to a panel, nothing is exempted, no page is deleted without its controls being re-homed. All five params sit in a declared page; `dockFacePlan` therefore emits no `__unpaged` tail band.

**Contract delta (claimed):** EXACTLY ZERO LINES. `face` is UI metadata with NO branch in `contract-signature.ts` — the projection emits only `meta` / `in` / `out` / `param` / `stereo` / `exposableControls` / control families (:95-120), and it sorts params BY ID (:107), so neither the face nor the params array ordering reaches the golden. shimmershine's 16 contract-lock lines (`contract-lock.txt:2876-2891`) come back byte-identical. This edit adds no param, no port, no `ControlFamily`, no `options`/`format`/`landmarks` (all three are cosmetic and unprojected anyway). `task docs:check` stays green with no `docs:accept` run. ATTEST: NIL — shimmershine.ts is an AUDIO def (not in the WebGL basis, so no `docs-hash-ignore` markers are needed) and is absent from the collab-attest basis (`scripts/collab-attest-lib.ts:60-88`). ART: NIL — no `art/baselines/shimmershine`, and `art/scenarios/shimmershine/octave-up-tail.test.ts` carries no `docsStrippedRepoSourceSha`, so the PF-11 pattern-3 gate (`art/scenarios/pattern3-face-pin.test.ts`) does not apply and a face edit cannot move a `.sha`.

**VRT impact (claimed):** TWO scenes move, both darwin, both in the informational lane; the required lane is untouched. (1) `workflow-shell-faces.spec.ts/darwin/face-shimmershine-dock.png` (1220×425) — CERTAIN, enormous: 3 bands → 2, all band labels change, band membership changes. Far over `DOCK_MAX_DIFF = 1500`. (2) `.../darwin/face-shimmershine-compact.png` (88×82) — ⚠⚠ **THE SUB-TOLERANCE TRAP, and I measured it.** I opened the committed baseline: the compact tile renders SHIMMER + MIX + the meter. Promoting DAMP to rank 2 changes the second column's LABEL ONLY — because `mix` and `damp` share the identical default 0.4 (shimmershine.ts:235-236), the knob pointer angle is pixel-identical. That is ~50-70 changed ink pixels against a `COMPACT_MAX_DIFF = 150` budget on a 7,216-px image. **The gate will PASS and `--update-snapshots` will write NOTHING** — Playwright only rewrites a FAILING snapshot. This is the A2/#1213 hole verbatim. FIX: `git rm e2e/vrt/__screenshots__/workflow-shell-faces.spec.ts/darwin/face-shimmershine-compact.png` (and the dock one, for symmetry) BEFORE regenerating; Playwright always writes a MISSING snapshot. Treat "green regen, zero files committed" as a red flag, never as "nothing to do". LINUX: `linux/face-shimmershine-{compact,dock}` are in `EXEMPT_BASELINE_PAIRS` at **vrt-exemptions.ts:1012-1013** (the plan says `:1011-1012` — off by one). RECOMMENDED: DRAIN both in the SAME commit and lower the vrt-meta linux-deficit ceiling `119 → 117` (vrt-meta.test.ts:468; I counted the live set — `awk '/EXEMPT_BASELINE_PAIRS/,0' … | grep -c "'linux/"` = exactly **119**, so it is at its ceiling today). THEN dispatch `gh workflow run vrt-update.yml -f ref=<branch> -f platform=linux`, UNSCOPED (never `-f grep=…`), and approve the `action_required` follow-on run. ⚠ The ratchet only fails on GROWTH, so forgetting to lower it from 119 is INVISIBLE to CI — that is a manual discipline, not a gate. STRUCTURAL GATE: `e2e/vrt/workflow-shell-faces.spec.ts:56` `{ type: 'shimmershine', pages: 3 }` → `pages: 2`; the dock test asserts `[data-testid="face-page"]` `toHaveCount(pages)` (:227) and fails BEFORE the pixel pin, so it must move in the same commit. NO OTHER PIN MOVES: no `rear-shimmershine` VRT scene exists, `rear-card-model.test.ts` names no shimmershine case, `e2e/tests/workflow-shell-faces.spec.ts` and `p1-batch2-faces.spec.ts` are hand-written for other modules, and there is no `shimmershine.legend.json` (only adsr/lfo/sequencer exist).

**CI delta (claimed):** ≈ +18 s, ALL of it on the INFORMATIONAL `vrt` lane; +0 s on every REQUIRED lane. Breakdown: (a) unit lane ~0 — face-lint, contract-lock, rear-card-model, docs-lint are all pure and their test COUNT is unchanged; (b) faces-parity e2e ~0 — the cell count stays 5 (params untouched), and its budget is derived per-cell (`FACE_FIXED_MS + 5 × FACE_PER_CELL_MS`, faces-parity.spec.ts:96-97), so nothing moves; (c) `vrt-strict` (the REQUIRED gate) +0 — `VRT_STRICT=1` narrows testMatch to `vrt.spec.ts` alone (vrt.config.ts:32-37,95-98), and shimmershine's entry there is the LEGACY card (STRICT_VRT_MODULES, vrt-exemptions.ts:894), whose baselines `vrt.spec.ts/{darwin,linux}/shimmershine.png` do not move because the card is not edited; (d) the `vrt` informational lane +~18 s IF the two linux pairs are drained — `workflow-shell-faces.spec.ts` is FULL_MATCH-only (vrt.config.ts:59), runs `workers: 1`, and each of the two currently-`test.skip()`-ed scenes costs a full goto + networkidle + palette spawn + frame + screenshot (~8-10 s each on the runner). If you instead leave the pairs exempt and go darwin-only, the total delta is exactly 0. Either way this is nowhere near the ~2 min sign-off threshold.

**Risks (as authored):**
- ⚠ THE COMPACT BASELINE CANNOT FAIL ITS OWN GATE. Measured: the DAMP↔MIX swap at rank 2 changes only a 4-glyph label on an 88×82 tile, because the two params share the identical default 0.4 (shimmershine.ts:235-236) so the knob pointer angle is pixel-identical. ~60 changed px against `COMPACT_MAX_DIFF = 150`. `--update-snapshots` writes nothing for a PASSING comparison, so the pin silently stays stale. `git rm` both darwin baselines before regenerating. Highest-consequence item in this spec.
- ⚠ REAR GROUP ID ↔ PAGE ID COLLISION IS LIVE HERE. The rear already declares `{ id: 'signal' }` (shimmershine.ts:281). `rearFieldPlan` claims a 'voice'/'signal' group for the LEADING band and marks it used (rear-card-model.ts:261-268), but the page-slot loop re-claims a curated group matching a page id WITHOUT re-checking `usedGroupIds` (:287-290) — so a page named `signal` or `voice` renders that band TWICE and reddens the rear-totality gate (module-face-lint.test.ts:756-767). `tank`/`loop` are safe. Never rename a page toward the word its band already displays; use dx7's `{id:'patch', label:'voice'}` trick instead.
- The two `ports: []` curated rear groups are a LABEL-ONLY override and read as dead config; a future reader deletes them and the rear band labels silently revert to the front page labels with every gate green. The comment must cite the mechanism (rear-card-model.ts:285-296) explicitly.
- TASTE CALL with a one-line revert: DAMP over MIX at compact. The counter — you ride MIX on an insert — is real. Swap 'damp' and 'mix' in `order` and NOTHING else moves: pages are independent, the rear derives from pages, the dock lays out by pages. Only face-shimmershine-compact.png re-pins.
- DO NOT add `ParamDef.format` to `shimmer`. Its self-oscillation threshold is a function of four params, not one (~0.4 at tank defaults, ~0.2 with SIZE/DECAY up and DAMP 0, ~0.55 at SIZE 0 — shimmershine.ts:310), so any formatter reading only `shimmer` would be a control lying about itself. PF-3 is not a shimmershine dependency.
- The vrt-meta linux-deficit ratchet only fails on GROWTH (vrt-meta.test.ts:314-318). Draining the two pairs without lowering 119 → 117 in the SAME commit is INVISIBLE to CI and rots the ceiling. It is a manual discipline, not a gate.
- The `pages` collapse re-groups the rear, since rear bands derive from `face.pages`. I hand-derived the new plan and it is total (8 holes, 6 inputs + 2 outputs, no duplicates, no orphan band) — but a later `pages` edit without re-deriving has only the totality gate between it and a dropped jack.

> Independently derived from packages/web/src/lib/audio/modules/shimmershine.ts — PARAMS: 5 (decay 0.6 / shimmer 0.4 / size 0.6 / damp 0.4 / mix 0.4, ALL min 0 max 1 curve 'linear', :232-236). INPUTS: 6 (in_l audio, in_r audio, decay_cv/shimmer_cv/size_cv/mix_cv all cv+paramTarget+cvScale linear, :218-226) — no damp_cv, confirmed. OUTPUTS: 2 (out_l, out_r audio, :227-230). TOTAL PORTS: 8. controlFamilies: 0. exposableControls: 0. paramCells/momentary: absent. stereoPairs: 2. face.order: 5 keys ['shimmer','mix','decay','size','damp'] → damp IS rank 5 today (spec's correction (a) is right). face.pages: 3 (tank['decay','size','damp'] / shimmer['shimmer'] / output['mix'], :269-271). face.rear.groups: 1 ({id:'signal', label:'stereo in', ports:['in_l','in_r']}, :281). contract-lock lines: 16 (contract-lock.txt:2876-2891). Worklet parameterDescriptors: 5, byte-for-byte the same ids/ranges/defaults (packages/dsp/src/shimmershine.ts:193-201). Legacy card: 5 Faders + PatchPanel + ModuleTitle, 48 lines, zero buttons/selects. ALL of these agree with the spec's numbers — the spec's counts, ranges, defaults, collision analysis (pages 'tank'/'loop' vs rear group 'signal' — no collision), lane budget (ranks 1-5 ≤ 6), glyph analysis (laneBodyPlan(5,true,'full') → rows=ceil(5/3)=2 → glyph=false; PLATE_COLS=3, PLATE_MAX_ROWS=2, LANE_ROW_MAX_CELLS_WITH_GLYPH=2 at module-shell-model.ts:286-289), rear totality (8 holes = 8 ports; curatedByPort skip at rear-card-model.ts:226 prevents in_l/in_r double-counting), zero contract delta (no `face` branch in contract-signature.ts; params sorted by id at :108), zero ART cost (shimmershine is on ART_BACKLOG at art/setup/profile-coverage.ts:107, no art/baselines/shimmershine, no docsStrippedRepoSourceSha in art/scenarios/shimmershine/octave-up-tail.test.ts), and the linux-deficit count (exactly 119 'linux/…' entries inside the EXEMPT_BASELINE_PAIRS literal, ceiling 119 at vrt-meta.test.ts:468) all CHECK OUT.

**DEFECTS — fix these before building:**

- **[MAJOR]** The spec's `pages[].label` values are explanatory PROSE, not labels — and they render verbatim as the dock band header AND the rear band header. 'reverb tank · damp is inside the combs' (38 chars) and 'the tank output · fed back, then blended out' (44 chars) would replace the 2-word headers the committed baseline shows.
  - *evidence:* curated-face.ts:292-297 — `dockFacePlan` maps `pages` straight through as `{id: p.id, label: p.label, …}`; rear-card-model.ts:291 — a page with no matching curated group becomes `{ id: page.id, label: page.label, holes: [] }`. So the page label IS the rendered band caption on both faces. Every shipped face uses 1-3 lowercase words: reverb.ts:98 `{ id: 'tank', label: 'reverb tank' }`, delay.ts:133-134 `'delay line'` / `'output blend'`, dx7.ts:225 `label: 'voice'`, and shimmershine's own current pages at shimmershine.ts:269-271 — which is exactly what e2e/vrt/__screenshots__/workflow-shell-faces.spec.ts/darwin/face-shimmershine-dock.png renders as `REVERB TANK` / `SHIMMER LOOP` / `OUTPUT BLEND` at ~90 px band pitch. A 38/44-char uppercase header does not fit that band and contradicts the house style the spec claims to be following.

- **[MAJOR]** The spec omits the `face.rear.groups` label-override entries that its OWN risk section and newParams section presuppose, and that the plan explicitly demands — then mis-cites the plan line range so the requirement disappears.
  - *evidence:* The deliverable declares NO rear change (the `rationale` rear derivation names only the existing curated `signal`). Yet `risks[2]` says "The two `ports: []` curated rear groups are a LABEL-ONLY override…" and `newParams[2]` says damp_cv "FALSIFIES this spec's rear label 'tank cv · DAMP has no jack'" — two pieces of config that appear nowhere in the spec. The plan flags this exact item as its own bullet at .myrobots/plans/dx7-and-faces-design-program-2026-07-27.md:858-860: "⚠ **The rear band labels need explicit `rear.groups` entries** — with no curated group for id `loop`, the rear band inherits the **page** label… The draft assumed they'd appear." The spec's correction (b) cites "plan:855-859" as if that whole range were the page-split bullet; the page-split bullet is :855-857 and :858-860 is the separate rear-label warning it never engages. Result: the face is not buildable as specified without a decision the spec never makes (long page labels, or the missing rear groups).

- **[MINOR]** A claim the spec presents as directly MEASURED from the committed PNG is contradicted by that PNG: the OUTPUT BLEND band LABEL is fully rendered, not "entirely below the fold". Only the MIX knob is clipped.
  - *evidence:* e2e/vrt/__screenshots__/workflow-shell-faces.spec.ts/darwin/face-shimmershine-dock.png (1220×425). Cropping rows 370-425 shows the header text `OUTPUT BLEND` painted in full at y≈383, with the MIX knob half-drawn beneath it. The spec's rationale states: "the third band OUTPUT BLEND is **clipped at the frame edge — its MIX knob half-drawn, its label entirely below the fold.**" The knob clause is true; the label clause is false. This is the second of the two justifications the spec offers for the 3→2 band collapse, and it is the one presented as independent measured evidence.

- **[MINOR]** Wrong line for the structural gate the spec instructs the implementer to move in the same commit — the `face-page` count assertion is at :222, not :227.
  - *evidence:* e2e/vrt/workflow-shell-faces.spec.ts:222 — `await expect(faceplate.locator('[data-testid="face-page"]')).toHaveCount(pages);`. Line :227-228 is inside the subsequent `toHaveScreenshot({ … maxDiffPixels: DOCK_MAX_DIFF })` call. The spec's vrtImpact says "the dock test asserts `[data-testid="face-page"]` `toHaveCount(pages)` (:227)". (The substantive claim — that the count assert precedes and therefore fails before the pixel pin — is correct; only the anchor is wrong. `{ type: 'shimmershine', pages: 3 }` at :56 is correct.)

- **[MINOR]** The `tail`-glyph demotion is cited to plan line 117; the `tail` row is at line 116. Line 117 is the `response` glyph row — a different demoted item.
  - *evidence:* .myrobots/plans/dx7-and-faces-design-program-2026-07-27.md — :112 `### DEMOTED — explicitly, with the reason`, :113 blank, :114 table header, :115 separator, :116 the `**`tail` glyph kind**` row, :117 the `**`response` glyph kind**` row. The spec's `glyph` field says "The `tail` glyph proposed for this module is DEMOTED in the plan (§2, line 117) on three independent defects" — the three-defect reasoning belongs to :116.

- **[MINOR]** Correction (e) attacks a BATCH-level dependency line as though it were a shimmershine claim, so "the plan is wrong" overstates it.
  - *evidence:* Plan :845 `### BATCH D — shimmershine · adsr · mixer · filter`, :846 `Depends on: **PF-1, PF-4, PF-9**.` The dependency is declared for the four-module batch, and the plan itself assigns PF-9 clusters to *filter* at :1134 (`- **PF-9 clusters on the `envelopes` band**: `filter eg` … vs `amp eg``) and PF-1/PF-10 to qbrt/filter mode elsewhere. The spec's "plan:846 makes Batch D depend on PF-1/PF-4/PF-9. **shimmershine needs NONE of them**" is true of shimmershine but is not a plan error. (The underlying verification is sound: DIRECTION_SUFFIX = /_(?:in|out)$/ at patch-panel-labels.ts:166 does not match `in_l`/`out_r`, so PF-4 is genuinely unneeded.)

- **[MINOR]** Citation drift across ~9 code anchors, several landing on a different statement than the one quoted.
  - *evidence:* packages/dsp/src/shimmershine.ts — the "self-caps at 0.92" comment is at :33 (spec says :32); "size=1, decay=1 → comb fb ≈ 0.92" is at :247 (spec says :248, which is `const effSize = …` — a line the spec separately and correctly cites as :248); `outL[i] = dryL * (1 - mix) + wetL * mix` is at :273 (spec says :271, which is `this.fbR = …`); the `2.0` rate / 25 ms window literals are at :225-226 (spec says :224, the comment). shell-glyph-live.ts — `glyphBinding` starts at :102 and the `if (audioOut) return { kind: 'live-audio', portId: audioOut }` branch the spec depends on is at :147 (spec says ":83-105"; only its `primaryAudioOutPortId` :86 is right). contract-signature.ts:108 sorts params by id (spec says :107). e2e/tests/faces-parity.spec.ts:97-98 defines FACE_FIXED_MS/FACE_PER_CELL_MS, applied at :597 (spec says :96-97). e2e/vrt/vrt.config.ts:60 lists workflow-shell-faces.spec.ts in FULL_MATCH (spec says :59). None of these change a conclusion, but the spec's authority rests on the citations.

- **[MINOR]** "0 legend statics (no shimmershine.legend.json exists — only adsr/lfo/sequencer do)" conflates a VRT annotation overlay with the shell-cells STATIC cell registry. The conclusion is right; the cited evidence proves nothing about it.
  - *evidence:* The only `*.legend.json` files in the repo are e2e/vrt/__annotated__/{adsr,lfo,sequencer}.legend.json — VRT annotation overlays. The face-lint's family/static resolution reads `SHELL_CELLS` (packages/web/src/lib/ui/workflow/shell-cells.ts:161, `Record<moduleType, Record<faceKey, ShellCell>>`) and `def.controlFamilies` (module-face-lint.test.ts:125). shimmershine has no SHELL_CELLS entry and no controlFamilies, which is the actual reason it ranks no family/static keys.


---

## mixer

**Verdict: NEEDS_WORK** · archetype: SYMMETRIC-BANK UTILITY — N interchangeable channel controls plus one master. Every design tension in this face descends from that shape (an arbitrary rank 2, a plate order that fights the priority order, and no legal way to collapse the bank into one cell), and none of them is a mixer-specific accident: mixmstrs, attenumix and moog984 are the same archetype waiting behind it.

**Rationale (as authored):** THE PLAN IS RIGHT ABOUT WHAT TO DO AND WRONG ABOUT WHY, IN THREE PLACES. (1) It justifies pages 2→1 with "a 5-fader mixer that needs a scroll is absurd" (line 905-906) — a fold-budget argument its own §1 had already retired, having measured face-mixer-dock.png at 1220×421 with the jack rail and ~26px spare, and PF-8 has since taken the dock to 398 against a 425px cap (vrt-exemptions.ts:1033-1035). There is no scroll. The merge must stand on grouping semantics per §1 consequence 1, and it does: all five controls are the SAME idea — a level — and every console puts the master in the fader row. Five 46px knob columns + four 10px gaps = 270px in a 1220px band; it is one row, trivially. (2) It says "order unchanged" and stops, which leaves the def's shipped comment self-contradictory: mixer.ts:86-97 demotes the channels because "picking ch1 over ch2/3/4 is arbitrary", then ranks ch1 second and calls the compact tile "SOURCE · BUS · LEVEL". The rank-1 argument applies verbatim at rank 2. I keep master-first — on the headroom argument alone, which is real and verified (master maxes at 1.0 in BOTH mixer.ts:76 and mixer.dsp, contract-lock.txt:1807; the .dsp is a bare weighted sum with no clamp; 4× correlated unity = +12.04 dB) — and I rewrite rank 2 honestly: the ladder must emit SOME second control, the four are interchangeable, so it emits the first in jack order. That is the only defensible sentence, and per the controlLoss ledger no platform mechanism can make it non-arbitrary. (3) Its clip-lamp remedy is unbuildable as written; see newParams.

WHY pages STAYS DECLARED RATHER THAN DROPPED. Deleting face.pages is tempting for a 5-control module and is wrong: dockFacePlan falls back to a single unlabelled '__all' band whose controls are `dock.controls` — i.e. face.order — so the dock would render MASTER first. `pages` is the ONLY mechanism by which the dock order can differ from the priority order, which is the entire "order and pages are allowed to disagree" doctrine. One declared page is what buys signal order: sources left, sum right.

WHY NO CLUSTER. PF-9 landed and `{ label:'bus', controls:['master'] }` looks like the elegant answer at ~14px instead of ~81px. It is wrong here: ModuleShell.svelte:797-806 renders `.page-cluster` as a BLOCK below `.page-controls` (CSS at :1064-1067), so master would drop onto its own row under a caption — which destroys the single fader row that is the whole reason to merge. A cluster is for "the same idea, twice"; this is one idea, five times.

THE FINDING THE FACE CANNOT FIX, stated so the next author does not rediscover it. `face.order` is OVERLOADED: it is the priority ranking AND the full-tier plate layout order (ModuleShell.svelte:815 renders `controls.slice(0, cellCount)` in order into PLATE_COLS=3). At full, master-first lays out [MASTER][CH1][CH2] / [CH3][CH4] — the master in the channel row's FIRST slot with the bank split 2/2 across rows, which is not a mixer. For a symmetric-bank archetype the priority answer and the layout answer genuinely differ, and there is no third list to declare. I considered a `face.plateOrder` field and REJECT it: one live module, one transient zoom band, and the dock — the surface anyone actually reads — already gets the right order from `pages`. Revisit when a second symmetric-bank module is promoted (mixmstrs is the obvious next).

TASTE CALLS, EACH WITH ITS REVERT. Page label `channel levels → master` (the arrow states why MASTER is rightmost and teaches the row; revert to `channel levels`, one string). Output label `MIX` (revert to `OUT`, one string). Rear band label `channel inputs` (revert to today's long mapping sentence, one string). None of the three moves a gate.

WHAT I VERIFIED AND FOUND CORRECT, so it does not get re-litigated: zero un-exposed DSP capability — mixer.dsp declares exactly five hsliders and the def exposes exactly five, which per recipe Step 8 is a FINDING, not a shortfall. Master-above-unity stays rejected, and the plan's musical argument now has a cost behind it: it is a .dsp edit, so it moves `dspSourceSha('mixer.dsp')` and forces an ART re-pin, on top of worsening the module's one hazard by removing its only headroom control. The Step-7 adversarial grep over MixerCard.svelte for `<button|<select|<input|oncontextmenu|manualTrigger|Toggle|Selector|format|read(|engine.|onclick|Segmented|Knob|XyPad` returns ZERO hits — the card is ModuleTitle + PatchPanel + five Faders and nothing else, so the control ledger really is closed. The `levels` per-channel glyph stays demoted, now for a concrete reason rather than the plan's hand-wave: post-fader per-channel level is not observable from the main thread, because createShellGlyphTap resolves taps only through `getOutputNode(nodeId, portId)` (shell-glyph-live.ts:295) — there is no input-tap seam, the Faust node sums internally, and the ChannelMerger inputs are pre-fader. It needs a DSP `read('levels')` seam like mixmstrs has, which is a separate PR.

```ts
face: {
  order: ["master", "ch1", "ch2", "ch3", "ch4"],
  pages: [
    { id: "channels", label: "channel levels \u2192 master", controls: ["ch1", "ch2", "ch3", "ch4", "master"] },
  ],
  glyph: "meter (UNCHANGED). glyphBinding(mixerDef) \u2192 { kind:'live-audio', portId:'audio' } via shell-glyph-live.ts:150 (no 'envelope'/'algorithm'/'waveform' branch applies; primaryAudioOutPortId finds the single audio out), so the VuMeter reads the REAL bus RMS off a lazy analyser on the Faust node. \u26a0 MEASURED CORRECTION TO THE DEF'S OWN SAFETY CLAIM: mixer.ts:105-108 says the meter is \"load-bearing rather than decorative\" because the sum can clip \u2014 but laneBodyPlan(5, true, 'full') returns glyph:false (module-shell-model.ts:340-342: rows = ceil(5/3) = 2, and `glyph = hasGlyph && rows <= 1`), so the meter VANISHES at the full-in-lane tier \u2014 precisely the tier where all four channels are visible and you are most likely to be balancing into clipping. It is geometrically unfixable inside the face: the plate body is ~112px, two 42px rows + one 4px gap = 88px, and a glyph strip needs 42+4 = 46 more (134 > 112). So the meter is a mini/compact/dock affordance and the face comment must say so instead of claiming otherwise.",
  paramCells: none — face.paramCells requires a DISCRETE param (module-face-lint negative control (d) at module-face-lint.test.ts:618 fails a 'grid' on a continuous/unbounded param), and all five of mixer's params are `curve: 'linear'` over 0..1. Likewise no `momentary` (no param has the 0..1-discrete-resting-at-0 press shape, so looksLikeSwitch is false for all five and the switch-classification ratchet at :419 has nothing to classify) and no `options`/`landmarks` (a continuous amplitude has no named states or waypoints).,
}
```

**New params:**
- NONE. mixer.dsp declares exactly ch1..ch4 + master as hsliders; mixerDef.params declares exactly those five. 1:1 — zero un-exposed DSP capability.
- PF-18 (PLATFORM, its own PR — NOT part of this face): a CLIP indicator on the meter. ⚠ THE PLAN'S VERSION IS UNBUILDABLE AND WOULD SHIP DEAD. Plan line 912-914 says 'make VuMeter clip-aware (a red segment above 1.0)'. The value can never exceed 1.0 before it reaches VuMeter: ShellGlyphTap.getLevel() returns rmsUnit(buf), and rmsUnit CLAMPS to [0,1] at level-meter.ts:24; VuMeter.toUnit clamps AGAIN (`v > 1 ? 1 : v`); dbfsToUnit clamps `db >= 0 → 1` (vu-meter-model.ts:27). A lamp gated on >1.0 is a control invariant to the dimension it claims to measure — the exact failure CLAUDE.md's 'VALIDATE THE INSTRUMENT' section names. Second, independent defect: even unclamped, RMS over GLYPH_TAP_FFT_SIZE = 2048 samples (~43ms) is not a peak detector — program material clips on peaks at an RMS well under 1, so the reading under-reports the very event. BUILDABLE VERSION: (a) add `getPeak(): number` to ShellGlyphTap = max(|s|) over the same getFloatTimeDomainData buffer, UNCLAMPED — and it genuinely sees >1 here, because the analyser connects to the Faust node itself (mixer.ts:187 `outputs: new Map([['audio', { node: f, output: 0 }]])`), upstream of any destination clamp; (b) give VuMeter an `over?: () => boolean` and an OVER lamp that is RENDER-INERT when false, so no layout shifts; (c) ship the negative control described in risks. ~40 LOC + 2 unit tests. It must NOT ride the mixer face PR: VuMeter is shared by every glyph:'meter' face (vca, cloudseed, shimmershine, reverb, delay, mixer) plus the moog914 card family, so any visible change moves all of their baselines and needs its own VRT pass. It also lands the safety affordance where it always renders — the DOCK hero meter — which matters given the full-tier glyph drop above.
- PF-19 (PLATFORM, its own PR — the Fader loss remedy): widen ModuleFace.paramCells from `'grid'` to `'grid' | 'fader'` and add a 'fader' ParamCellKind. Feasibility is real: Fader.svelte already emits `data-testid="control-<paramId>"` on a `role="slider"` (:437-439) and is MIDI-assignable, so faces-parity's multiset and MIDI-learn hold unchanged. Full price, stated so it is not underestimated: a new `data-cell-control` value REQUIRES a driveCell branch — faces-parity.spec.ts:531 THROWS on an unrecognised kind and kills the whole spec, not one row — plus a module-face-lint rule (a fader in a 46px --kcol-max lane column needs a declared tier restriction the way panels have one), plus every adopting face's baselines. Size it M. Do NOT fold it into a face wave.

**Control-loss ledger:**
- Fader → KnobConic (the ONE real loss, and the plan misses it). MixerCard.svelte:33-37 paints five `<Fader>`; ModuleShell.svelte:529-546 paints five `KnobConic` for every non-toggle/non-options param. Plan line 1197 lists mixer under 'NONE — verified line by line', which is true at the CONTROL level and false at the PRIMITIVE level: a linear-amplitude 0..1 level is the textbook case for linear travel, and the mixer fader is the module's whole idiom. WHERE IT GOES: nowhere today — accepted for this face, named as PF-19 below. Precisely scoped, so nobody over-claims: dblclick→defaultValue reset SURVIVES (Fader.svelte:337 and KnobConic.svelte:205 both have it), oncontextmenu/ControlContextMenu survives, MIDI-learn survives, and the `fader-zero-hash` mark is NOT lost because it only renders `{#if isBipolar}` (Fader.svelte:453) and mixer's params are unipolar 0..1. The loss is exactly the travel geometry: 200px of linear throw vs a 270° arc.
- Jack labels INPUT 1..4 / OUT — TODAY a live loss on the migrated surface, RECOVERED by this PR. MixerCard.svelte:22-24 hands portsFromDef an explicit labels map; ModuleShell.svelte:379 calls `portsFromDef(def.inputs ?? [])` with NO map, so the shell's rail and the rear card fall back to id derivation and read IN1..IN4 / AUDIO (expandStem('in1') hits neither PREFIX_TO_VERBOSE nor ABBREV_TO_VERBOSE → 'IN1'; ABBREV_TO_VERBOSE['audio'] = 'AUDIO', patch-panel-labels.ts:104,168-182). WHERE IT GOES: onto the def as PortDef.label (PF-4 landed in #1188 and portsFromDef honours `p.label`, card-kit.ts:62) — in1..in4 = 'INPUT 1'..'INPUT 4', audio = 'MIX'. Then DELETE the card's labels map so the def is the one source; leaving it makes the def label dead code on the front surface (`labels[p.id] ?? p.label`) — exactly the card-disagrees-with-def class the standards doc was written for.
- 'MIX' over the plan's 'OUT' is a taste call — state it as one. FOR: rear-card-model.ts:26-29 says labels are BY FUNCTION, and the repo's own DIRECTION_SUFFIX rule (patch-panel-labels.ts:148-165) kills 'OUT' next to a '→' glyph as saying it twice; 'MIX' is also the sibling family word — attenumix declares its summing bus as a port literally named `mix` (attenumix.ts:137). AGAINST: the legacy card says 'OUT' and the plan (line 908-911) explicitly warns against inventing new vocabulary. ONE-LINE REVERT: `{ id: 'audio', type: 'audio', label: 'OUT' }`, nothing else moves.
- Rear band label SHRINKS: `channel inputs · in1→ch1 … in4→ch4` → `channel inputs`. Not a loss — a transfer. The def's own rear comment (mixer.ts:121-128) says the long label exists because 'the label has one job: say which hole feeds which fader'. Once PF-4 puts INPUT 1..4 on the holes themselves and the front cells read Ch1..Ch4, the number match carries the mapping at the hole and the band header stops restating it. KEEP the curated group — dropping face.rear entirely sends in1..in4 into the derived band, and since none is gate/pitch/poly the `isVoice` test (rear-card-model.ts:273-275) is false and the band is titled the useless 'signal'.
- NOT A LOSS, a structural impossibility worth recording: the four channel levels CANNOT be collapsed into one 'balance' cell. A `family` or `panel` cell rendering four faders would have to emit control-ch1..control-ch4, while STRICT_FACES completeness forces ch1..ch4 to also be ranked as params — faces-parity.spec.ts:552-563 asserts EXACT MULTISET EQUALITY between the dock's control-* testids and the def param ids, so the duplicates fail. A panel is additionally forbidden from emitting control-<paramId> at all (shell-cells.ts:96-103). This is why the compact tile is master + one arbitrary channel and why no amount of face authoring fixes it.

**Contract delta (claimed):** EXACTLY 0 lines. contract-lock.txt:1797-1807 is mixer's whole block (meta, 4 ins, 1 out, 5 params) and every field this face touches is outside the projection: `face.*` and `docs.*` have no branch in serializeModuleContract (contract-signature.ts:91-129), and `PortDef.label` has no branch in `portLine` (contract-signature.ts:76-88 — parts are id, type, paramTarget, cvScale, accepts, edge, adoptsUpstreamFrom; label is absent). CHECKABLE PREDICTION, and treat a miss as a finding: `flox activate -- task docs:accept` must produce a ZERO-line diff in contract-lock.txt. If it moves, something in the change is not contract-transparent — stop and find out what, do not accept the diff. ATTEST: nil — mixer is an audio def, not in the WebGL basis, so no docs-hash-ignore markers are needed. ART: nil — art/scenarios/mixer/profile.test.ts:87 pins `dspSourceSha('mixer.dsp')`, NOT `docsStrippedRepoSourceSha` on the def, so a face/docs/label edit cannot move the .f32 or the .sha (this is the delay-class trap PF-11 exists for; mixer is clean of it).

**VRT impact (claimed):** MOVES: face-mixer-dock.png on BOTH darwin and linux. Losing a band changes the faceplate HEIGHT (one ~81px rule+header+row; the exemptions ledger records mixer's dock at 421→398 after PF-8), so Playwright fails on a size mismatch rather than a pixel count — which means this is NOT the A2 'passing-but-stale' hole where a sub-DOCK_MAX_DIFF change comes back with zero files committed. Belt and braces anyway: `git rm` both dock PNGs before dispatching, because Playwright always writes a MISSING snapshot and only sometimes rewrites a failing one. UNCHANGED (assumption, must be verified — see risks): face-mixer-compact.png on both platforms. NOT AFFECTED: there is no mixer rear scene — workflow-rear-card.spec.ts's SCENES list is dx7, sixstrum, tidyVco, vca only (confirmed against e2e/vrt/__screenshots__/workflow-rear-card.spec.ts/darwin/, which holds exactly those four PNGs). EXEMPTION DRAINS: NONE, and the vrt-meta linux-deficit ratchet does NOT move. mixer has zero entries in EXEMPT_BASELINE_PAIRS — its P1 batch-3 pairs were deliberately drained and both platforms captured on 2026-07-26/28 (vrt-exemptions.ts:1050-1060), so both baselines exist and are live gates. The plan's Batch D drain row (line 1301) lists only shimmershine, which is correct for mixer. PROCEDURE: regenerate darwin locally; for linux dispatch `flox activate -- gh workflow run vrt-update.yml -f ref=<branch> -f platform=linux`, UNSCOPED (a `-f grep=…` dies as startup_failure), then approve the follow-on runs — the bot's push lands them in `action_required`, not `queued`. STRUCTURAL GATE, same commit: e2e/vrt/workflow-shell-faces.spec.ts:47 `{ type: 'mixer', pages: 2 }` → `pages: 1`. NO unit pin moves: mixer appears in none of rear-card-model.test.ts, curated-face.test.ts, shell-cells.test.ts or e2e/tests/workflow-shell-faces.spec.ts (grep-verified), and faces-parity's only mixer reference is a comment at :265 explaining why dragKnob derives its direction from the current value — mixer ships every level at max, so a fixed upward drag would read a perfectly operable control as LOST. That behaviour is unaffected here and must stay unaffected.

**CI delta (claimed):** ≈0 s, and the arithmetic is closed rather than asserted. faces-parity drives one cell per param + family: 5 params + 0 families before, 5 + 0 after → zero cell delta, zero drive-loop delta (the plan's Batch D row already says 0 for the batch; mixer contributes nothing to it). VRT runs the same two mixer scenes (compact + dock). workflow-shell-faces' dock test does the same work with one band instead of two. No new spec, no new module, no new registry row, so no auto-enrolled sweep grows. Nothing here approaches the >~2 min owner sign-off threshold — but state the figure in the PR body anyway, and confirm it ON CI rather than locally, since the shared runner is SwiftShader at roughly 4× the local per-cell cost.

**Risks (as authored):**
- THE COMPACT BASELINE ASSUMPTION — verify, do not assume. I claim face-mixer-compact.png is UNCHANGED on both platforms: pages never reach a lane tier, and PortDef.label does not reach the compact tile because the lane rail (PatchPanel.svelte:869-893) paints jack DOTS + a '···' overflow + a flow label, no port names. That is a read of the markup, not a measurement. Run `flox activate -- task vrt:one -- mixer` and confirm the compact scene passes untouched. If it moves, the rail does print names and the assumption was wrong.
- FORGETTING THE STRUCTURAL GATE. e2e/vrt/workflow-shell-faces.spec.ts:47 pins `{ type: 'mixer', pages: 2 }` and the dock test asserts `toHaveCount(pages)` BEFORE the screenshot (:227). It must become `pages: 1` in the SAME commit or the scene fails with a count error that reads like a render bug.
- SOMEONE 'FIXES' order TO MATCH pages. This is the program's named anti-pattern and this face is its most tempting target, because pages is now literally the reverse-ish of order. If order becomes ch1-first, the mini tile shows CH1 — one knob of four identical ones, with which you cannot balance — and the module's only headroom control leaves the tile. The face comment must forbid it in words, with the reason, not just declare the two lists.
- THE CLIP LAMP IS EASY TO SHIP DEAD (see newParams/PF-18). If it is built the way the plan words it, it can never light, and nothing in the suite would notice. Any implementation must carry a NEGATIVE CONTROL: drive the peak source to 1.5 and assert the lamp lights; drive it to 0.9 and assert it does not. A test that only asserts 'the meter renders' proves nothing about the dimension the lamp exists to measure.
- PAGE-ID / REAR-GROUP-ID COLLISION — mixer is SAFE, but only because of which id it uses, so do not rename casually. The hazard is narrower than 'any page id colliding with a rear group id': rear-card-model.ts:262-269 gives a curated group whose id is 'voice' or 'signal' the leading band slot WITHOUT adding it to the exclusion set the page loop honours, and then :287-291 claims it AGAIN by page id — that is dx7's documented double-band (dx7.ts:215-224). mixer's group id is 'channels', which is claimed once at the page slot (usedGroupIds.add at :290) and skipped by the extra-curated sweep at :303-304 → ONE band, 4 holes + 1 output = 5 = declared port count, so module-face-lint's rear totality gate stays green. Keeping `id: 'channels'` on the merged page is the zero-risk choice.
- contract-lock.txt is a top conflict surface even at +0 lines if a sibling batch-D PR lands first. Take main + re-run `task docs:accept`; never hand-merge, never `gh pr update-branch`.

**Counts independently re-derived by the fact-checker:**

> Derived independently from the def, not from the spec. PARAMS = 5: ch1/ch2/ch3/ch4/master, every one min 0, max 1, defaultValue 1, curve 'linear' (mixer.ts:72-76). INPUTS = 4: in1,in2,in3,in4, all type 'audio', no accepts/paramTarget/edge/cvScale (mixer.ts:65-68). OUTPUTS = 1: 'audio', type 'audio' (mixer.ts:70). PORTS total = 5. contract-lock.txt:1797-1807 = 11 lines (1 meta + 4 in + 1 out + 5 param) — matches. controlFamilies = 0 (none declared). face.order = 5 keys, all resolve to real params, ranks 1-5, inside the 6-rank lane budget (curated-face.ts:46,62-67). face.rear.groups = 1 group, id 'channels', 4 ports (mixer.ts:136-144). DSP = exactly 5 hsliders (packages/dsp/src/mixer.dsp), so the 1:1 claim holds. Cross-checks that came back CORRECT: the >=4-cell glyph drop (curatedFace(def,'full') caps at 6 -> 5 controls; ModuleShell.svelte:159 laneBodyPlan(5,true,'full') -> plate, cellCount 5, rows ceil(5/3)=2, glyph = hasGlyph && rows<=1 = false, module-shell-model.ts:339-342); compact = 2 cells + glyph (faceTierCap, curated-face.ts:76-79); the page-id/rear-group-id collision is genuinely SAFE for 'channels' (the double-band at rear-card-model.ts:262-269 fires only for a group id 'voice'/'signal'; the page loop at :287-291 claims 'channels' once and :303-304 skips it — 4 holes + 1 output = 5 = declared port count, totality gate green); contract delta EXACTLY 0 (ContractPortLike has no `label` field, contract-signature.ts:55-63; portLine parts = id,type,paramTarget,cvScale,accepts,edge,adopts, :76-88; serializeModuleContract has no face/docs branch, :90-129); the card does NOT hardcode a range the def does not declare (MixerCard.svelte:33-37 min=0/max=1/default=1/curve=linear == mixer.ts:72-76); no def param is silently dropped; no family/static key is ranked, so nothing needs a new shell-cells spec.

**DEFECTS — fix these before building:**

- **[MAJOR]** The VRT ledger is presented as exhaustive but omits mixer's THIRD baseline pair — the legacy-card scene, which is in the REQUIRED vrt-strict subset, and which is the one surface the spec proposes editing.
  - *evidence:* ciDelta says "VRT runs the same two mixer scenes (compact + dock)" and vrtImpact enumerates only face-mixer-dock.png (MOVES), face-mixer-compact.png (UNCHANGED) and "there is no mixer rear scene" (NOT AFFECTED). But e2e/vrt/__screenshots__/vrt.spec.ts/darwin/mixer.png AND .../linux/mixer.png both exist — the per-card scene auto-enrolled by e2e/vrt/vrt.spec.ts:55-58 — and mixer is listed in STRICT_VRT_MODULES at e2e/vrt/vrt-exemptions.ts:886 ('mixer', // 4-channel mixer fader card), i.e. the `vrt-strict (visual regression — strict subset)` REQUIRED context, not the informational lane. The spec's own controlLoss item edits that exact file ("DELETE the card's labels map") and changes the rendered output label from 'OUT' to 'MIX' (MixerCard.svelte:24 `portsFromDef(mixerDef.outputs, { audio: 'OUT' })` -> PortDef.label 'MIX', consumed verbatim-uppercased by patch-panel-labels.ts:189-191 at PatchPanel.svelte:1036). The scene is never named, so nobody is told to check it.

- **[MAJOR]** The structural-gate edit instruction — stated twice, as the one same-commit change that prevents a confusing red — points at the WRONG LINE, and the line it names is a DIFFERENT module's row.
  - *evidence:* risks and vrtImpact both say: "e2e/vrt/workflow-shell-faces.spec.ts:47 `{ type: 'mixer', pages: 2 }` -> `pages: 1`". e2e/vrt/workflow-shell-faces.spec.ts:47 is `{ type: 'adsr', pages: 1 }`. mixer's row is line 61. The count assertion cited as ":227" is at :222 (`await expect(faceplate.locator('[data-testid="face-page"]')).toHaveCount(pages);`).

- **[MAJOR]** The glyph field's headline — a "MEASURED CORRECTION TO THE DEF'S OWN SAFETY CLAIM" that the face comment "must say so instead of claiming otherwise" — contradicts the def, which already says the glyph steps aside at the full tier.
  - *evidence:* mixer.ts:100-102 already states: "full-in-lane (plate)  all five (5 cells <= the 3x2 whole-cell cap; the glyph steps aside for the ranked controls, per laneBodyPlan) — the complete mixer in the lane." The laneBodyPlan arithmetic the spec derives is correct (module-shell-model.ts:339-342), but there is nothing to correct: the def never claims the meter renders at 'full'. The claim it does cite, mixer.ts:104-108, is about the meter being load-bearing where it renders, and is scoped by :100-102.

- **[MAJOR]** The FOR-side evidence for renaming the output 'OUT' -> 'MIX' misreads the DIRECTION_SUFFIX rule; the file cited is evidence AGAINST the rename.
  - *evidence:* Spec: "the repo's own DIRECTION_SUFFIX rule (patch-panel-labels.ts:148-165) kills 'OUT' next to a '->' glyph as saying it twice". The rule is `const DIRECTION_SUFFIX = /_(?:in|out)$/` at patch-panel-labels.ts:166, applied in expandStem at :171 — it strips a trailing `_in`/`_out` from a COMPOUND id (`audio_out` -> 'AUDIO'); it can never match a standalone label or the bare id `audio`. The same file deliberately PRESERVES the word: ABBREV_TO_VERBOSE maps `out: 'OUT'` at :105, and PREFIX_TO_VERBOSE expands `^out([LR])$` -> 'OUT L'/'OUT R' at :142. The companion cite "ABBREV_TO_VERBOSE['audio'] = 'AUDIO', patch-panel-labels.ts:104" is at :106.

- **[MINOR]** The faces-parity failure mode is overstated: an unrecognised cell kind fails ONE module's test, not "the whole spec".
  - *evidence:* e2e/tests/faces-parity.spec.ts:533 `throw new Error(...unknown cell control kind...)` sits inside driveCell, which is called from the per-type test generated at :537-538 (`for (const type of [...STRICT_FACES].sort()) { test(...) }`). It aborts the remaining cells of that one module's test; the other STRICT_FACES tests are independent. The cite ":531" is :533.

- **[MINOR]** Systematic +/- few-line citation drift on the svelte/e2e/art anchors, so a reviewer cannot trust the spec's line numbers even where the substance is right.
  - *evidence:* ModuleShell.svelte ":379" -> :380 (`portsFromDef(def.inputs ?? [])`); ":815" -> :817 (`controls.slice(0, lanePlan.cellCount)`); shell-glyph-live.ts ":150" -> :147 (`if (audioOut) return { kind: 'live-audio', portId: audioOut };`); shell-glyph-live.ts ":295" -> :292 (`audio.getOutputNode(nodeId, portId)`); art/scenarios/mixer/profile.test.ts ":87" -> :83 (`const srcSha = await dspSourceSha('mixer.dsp');`); vrt-exemptions.ts "1033-1035" -> the 421->398 sentence is :1033-1034. (By contrast the contract-signature.ts:76-88 / :91-129, contract-lock.txt:1797-1807, module-face-lint.test.ts:419/:618, Fader.svelte:337/437-439/453, KnobConic.svelte:205, level-meter.ts:24, attenumix.ts:137 and faces-parity.spec.ts:265 cites are exact.)

- **[MINOR]** The controlLoss ledger declares the one-source-of-truth audit closed while leaving the card's re-typed RANGES unmentioned — the exact divergence class the standard is written about.
  - *evidence:* The ledger flags only the labels map (MixerCard.svelte:21-24) as the def-disagreement risk, and the rationale asserts "the control ledger really is closed" off a grep pattern (`<button|<select|<input|...|Knob|XyPad`) that cannot see numeric literals. MixerCard.svelte:33-37 re-types `min={0} max={1} defaultValue={1} curve="linear"` on all five Faders, duplicating mixer.ts:72-76. The values AGREE today, so this is latent rather than a live bug — but it is the same single-source violation the spec argues must be fixed for labels, and the spec's own PF-19 (promoting Fader into the shell) is what would remove it.

## filter

**Verdict: CONTRADICTS_CODE** · archetype: PROCESSOR · single-engine VCF. One hero the hand SWEEPS (cutoff), one hand RIDES (resonance), one set-once switch that RE-FRAMES what the hero means (mode), and one two-knob modulation-depth stage that decides how hard a patched EG/LFO throws the sweep. Not a voice (no gate/pitch in), not a mixer (one audio in / one audio out), not a multi-engine effect (three parallel fi.reson sections but ba.selectn picks exactly one — filter.dsp:9).

**Rationale (as authored):** THE HONEST HEADLINE FIRST: filter's face is already BUILT and already CORRECT. The plan's §4 Batch-D prose says `order` unchanged, `pages` stay 2, rear 'finished — change nothing', glyph stays scope, new controls none — and its one open item, the PF-1 `options` loss remedy, ALREADY LANDED in A2/#1213 (filter.ts:112-117, with the Segmented/knob split pinned by faces-parity.spec.ts:644-704 and both platforms' dock baselines regenerated). So the buildable Batch-D delta for filter is: a COMMENT REWRITE, a ONE-LINE port label, and a card bug-fix. Reporting that is worth more than inventing a redesign — plan §7's anti-pattern (3) is 'proposing a ranking identical to the existing one and presenting it as a redesign', and I am declining to do that.

THREE CORRECTIONS TO THE PLAN, all against real lines:
(a) The plan's proposed option labels are lowercase `{value:0,label:'lp'}…`. The shipped code is UPPERCASE `'LP'/'HP'/'BP'` plus a `title` tooltip per option (filter.ts:113-117), and faces-parity.spec.ts:694 asserts `toHaveText(['LP','HP','BP'])` while :661 asserts the lane readout is `'LP'`. Do NOT paste the plan's snippet — it would red two assertions to change nothing a user can see.
(b) The plan omits filter from PF-4's dependent list ('mixer, lfo, adsr, vca, sixstrum'). It belongs there: the shell prints AUDIO on both the input hole and the output hole, where the legacy card said OUT (see controlLoss[3]).
(c) The plan says '⚠ Delete the fold justification' while also saying filter.ts's in-source claim was correct. Both are true and the resolution is: the CLAIM was honest (it cited the captured baseline, not phantom arithmetic — which is why §1 exonerates it), but §1's consequence 1 makes budget an ILLEGITIMATE JUSTIFICATION regardless of accuracy. So rewrite filter.ts:148-155 to defend the 2-band split on GROUPING SEMANTICS alone, and delete the 'pushed cv depth below the fold at the 720p dock height' sentence. Do not delete the measurement discipline it demonstrates.

WHY THIS ORDER (a priority ranking, defended so the argument would be WRONG for another module):
1 `cutoff` — the hero, and it survives the plan's Step-3 inertness check: it is live at spawn (1000 Hz, mid-travel on a log taper) and audible on ANY input regardless of every other setting. Nothing else on a VCF earns the single-knob mini tier.
2 `resonance` — the second hand, live at 0.1 and independently audible. Compact = cutoff + res + the live trace, which is the whole gesture.
3 `mode` ABOVE the two CV depths, deliberately: it re-frames what the hero MEANS (dark vs thin vs narrow), so a lane tile should answer it at a glance. It is not higher because it is a set-once switch, not a performance control.
4 `cutoff_cv_amt` OVER 5 `res_cv_amt`: EG → cutoff is the single most common patch in any rack, and the Faust mapping is ±5 OCTAVES per full-scale CV (filter.dsp:12), so a plain 0..1 envelope at the +1 default pins the corner at the 20 kHz ceiling. That trim is the knob you reach for immediately after patching; the res trim is a refinement. They stay ADJACENT because they read as one modulation stage and splitting the pair to promote one costs more than it buys.

WHY THESE PAGES (function order, allowed to disagree with order — and here they do NOT, which is itself worth stating so the next author does not read agreement as a rule): the module is two stages a player thinks about separately — WHAT THE FILTER DOES (`response · type`, 3 controls) and HOW HARD PATCHED CV DRIVES IT (`cv depth`, 2 controls). `mode` rides band 1 rather than earning a third band because it is not a third IDEA: it selects among the three sections `cutoff` and `resonance` already control, and the band header names it. Both bands clear the plan's ≥2-controls-or-identity bar. Two bands is also what the rear card wants — pages are its projection (plan §7 Step 5), and the `modulation` page id is what lets the curated `cv depth` group claim the page slot instead of appearing as a trailing extra.

GLYPH `scope` is the right call and the alternative is explicitly demoted: a `response` magnitude-curve glyph was rejected in §2 because the reuse argument fails on 5 of the 6 modules it named.
