The trace is the one glyph that shows a FILTER doing its job — harmonics shaved as cutoff drops, the resonant ring appearing as res comes up. A meter would report level, which a filter barely changes. PRICE IT HONESTLY: with 5 ranked cells, laneBodyPlan (module-shell-model.ts:340-342) computes rows = ceil(5/3) = 2, so `glyph = hasGlyph && rows <= 1` is FALSE and the scope DOES NOT RENDER at the `full` tier. It renders at mini and compact only. That is the cost of ranks 4 and 5, it is already paid today, and it is the one-line revert if anyone disagrees: cut the face to 3 ranked controls and the glyph returns at `full`. I do not recommend it — losing the CV trims from the plate to win a 40 px trace is a bad trade on the module whose headline hazard is an un-trimmed envelope pinning the ceiling.

REAR — change nothing but the output label. The `res` pin is the right non-obvious call and must not be 'simplified': `res` carries neither a paramTarget nor a `<param>_cv` id (its stem is `res`, the param is `resonance` — the tidyVco pwm_cv/pw class), so rearFieldPlan's :229-232 would classify it as NOT a per-param CV and file it into the leading voice band next to the audio input. Pinning both CV holes to the `modulation` group keeps the signal band the single audio hole it should be. NO `audioRate` ticks, and the citation is in the source: both derived values pass `si.smoo` (filter.dsp:12-13), the documented ~7 Hz one-pole, so neither jack is FM-able and a `~` would lie about the one thing the tick exists to say.

```ts
face: {
  order: ["cutoff", "resonance", "mode", "cutoff_cv_amt", "res_cv_amt"],
  pages: [
    { id: "response", label: "response \u00b7 type", controls: ["cutoff", "resonance", "mode"] },
    { id: "modulation", label: "cv depth", controls: ["cutoff_cv_amt", "res_cv_amt"] },
  ],
  glyph: "scope",
}
```

**New params:**
- NONE — and per plan §7 Step 8 that is a FINDING, not a shortfall. packages/dsp/src/filter.dsp declares exactly THREE UI elements (cutoffKnob :6, resKnob :7, modeKnob :8). The def's other two params (cutoff_cv_amt, res_cv_amt) are engine-graph GainNodes, not hsliders (filter.ts:229-236), so the def is a strict SUPERSET of the DSP: zero un-exposed DSP capability.
- REJECTED (stays demoted, plan §2): a `mode` MORPH param (crossfade instead of ba.selectn). It is real DSP + a contract line + an ART re-pin, AND it would break the Segmented render that #1213 just shipped — a crossfade has real in-between states, which is `landmarks` (PF-10) vocabulary, not `options` (PF-1). Never fold a DSP change into a face wave.
- REJECTED: a `slope` / 24 dB-per-oct param. filter.dsp is three 2-pole fi.reson sections; a 4-pole mode is a different DSP and a different module (moog904a/b are the ladders).

**Control-loss ledger:**
- ZERO front-face control loss, and it is verifiable in one line. The plan §7 Step-7 adversarial grep (`<button|<select|<input|oncontextmenu|manualTrigger|Toggle|Selector|format`) over FilterCard.svelte returns exactly ONE hit: the mode <button> at FilterCard.svelte:41. filter declares no controlFamilies and has no numbered legend file (only adsr/lfo/sequencer have one), so the grep IS the whole ledger.
- `mode` as NAMES (legacy: three labelled LP/HP/BP buttons, FilterCard.svelte:39-43) → NOT LOST, already closed. `mode` is face.order rank 3 on page `response`; paramCellKind (shell-control-kind.ts:133-136) returns 'segmented' at tier 'dock' (3 options ≤ SEGMENTED_MAX_OPTIONS=6) and 'knob' at every lane tier, where PF-1's persistent readout + 3 detent ticks name the state. Shipped in A2/#1213 (filter.ts:112-117) and pinned end-to-end by faces-parity.spec.ts:644-704. The plan's §5 ledger row for filter is therefore already RESOLVED — Batch D inherits it green.
- cutoff / resonance rendered as FADERS on the legacy card (FilterCard.svelte:35-36) → KnobConic on the face. A deliberate PRIMITIVE SWAP, not a loss: same param, same def range, same commit path. Named here only because it is the sole remaining visual difference between the two surfaces.
- REAL LOSS THE PLAN MISSES — the OUT jack's name. FilterCard.svelte:26 renames the output via `portsFromDef(filterDef.outputs, { audio: 'OUT' })`. ModuleShell.svelte:380 calls `portsFromDef(def.outputs ?? [])` with NO overrides map, and no PREFIX_TO_VERBOSE pattern matches a bare `audio`, so expandStem falls to ABBREV_TO_VERBOSE.audio = 'AUDIO' (patch-panel-labels.ts:106). The migrated rear card therefore prints AUDIO for the INPUT hole (band 'audio in') and AUDIO again for the OUTPUT hole on the same card — disambiguated only by rail side + the ←/→ glyph. REMEDY, 1 line in the def, in this PR: `{ id: 'audio', type: 'audio', label: 'out' }` on filterDef.outputs. PF-4 has already LANDED (PortDef.label exists at graph/types.ts:250-268) and both consumers honour it (card-kit.ts portsFromDef tier-2; rear-card-model.ts rearHoleLabel → resolveVerboseLabel :189-191, which uses an explicit label verbatim, uppercased). The plan's PF-4 dependent list is 'mixer, lfo, adsr, vca, sixstrum' — filter belongs on it.
- REVERSE loss, worth naming because it is the bigger user-facing hole: the LEGACY card exposes only 3 of the def's 5 params. `cutoff_cv_amt` / `res_cv_amt` (added by #1174) have NO control on FilterCard at all. laneRenderKind (legacy-fallback.ts:108) returns 'legacy' whenever workflow mode is off OR shell preview is off, so for a DAWLESS user the two attenuverters are unreachable except via MIDI-learn/automation. The FACE fixes this; the card is what is lossy. Do NOT 'fix' this by adding two faders to FilterCard — the card is the surface being retired.

**Contract delta (claimed):** EXACTLY ZERO LINES. contract-lock.txt:1112-1121 (1 meta + 3 in + 1 out + 5 param lines) stays byte-identical. Three independent reasons, each checked against the projector rather than assumed: (a) no new param, no new port, no new controlFamily — the face fields (order/pages/glyph/rear/paramCells) are not projected at all; (b) `options` on `mode` is already shipped and already cost 0 — serializeModuleContract emits `${t} param ${id} ${min}..${max} ${curve} default=${default}${unit}` and nothing else (contract-signature.ts:108-111), which is why line 1119 still reads `filter param mode 0..2 discrete default=0` with no options fragment; (c) the recommended `label: 'out'` on the output port is ALSO 0 — portLine (contract-signature.ts:78-88) has no label branch, and graph/types.ts:258-260 states PortDef.label is contract-transparent for exactly this reason. Consequence: `task docs:accept` should produce an EMPTY diff. A non-empty contract-lock diff on this PR means something unintended moved — stop and read it, do not re-pin.

**VRT impact (claimed):** ZERO baselines move for the spec as written, on BOTH platforms — and that is the deliverable, not a hedge. (1) The face fields are byte-identical to what ships today (filter.ts:146-184), so the compact tile and the dock faceplate are unchanged. (2) The comment rewrite is 0 pixels. (3) The recommended `label: 'out'` changes only the REAR CARD's outputs-rail text and the lane-rail drill-down — and there is NO rear baseline for filter: e2e/vrt/__screenshots__/workflow-rear-card.spec.ts/darwin/ holds only rear-{dx7,sixstrum,tidyVco,vca}.png. The dock scene captures `[data-testid="dock-full-view"]` (the FRONT faceplate; the rear is on TAB) and the compact tile shows no jack text, so neither face-filter baseline sees it. NO EXEMPTION DRAINS, and the vrt-meta linux-deficit ratchet does not move: `linux/face-filter-{compact,dock}` are NOT in EXEMPT_BASELINE_PAIRS — batch 3 drained them and the linux PNGs landed (both platforms' files dated Jul 28, regenerated by #1213). The plan's Batch D drain row lists only shimmershine; filter needs nothing. Structural page-count gate (workflow-shell-faces.spec.ts:57 FACES table): filter stays `{ type: 'filter', pages: 2 }` — do not edit the table. ⚠ If review overrides me and changes a page label or membership, exactly 2 files move (darwin + linux face-filter-dock.png) and the diff will almost certainly land under DOCK_MAX_DIFF=1500 → `git rm` both BEFORE dispatching vrt-update.yml, or the run comes back green having captured nothing (see risks[0]).

**CI delta (claimed):** ≈ 0 s. Δ faces-parity cells = 0 (5 params, 0 families, before and after) at the measured ~0.8 s/cell on CI. No new spec, no new scene, no new unit file. The VRT scene count is unchanged (2 per platform). The only way this PR adds CI time is if someone adds a test — and it should not: `mode` is already covered end-to-end by faces-parity.spec.ts:644-704 (both tiers + agreement), the face is covered by the registry-driven module-face-lint sweeps (filter auto-enrols via STRICT_FACES, strict-faces.ts:46), and the audio is pinned by art/scenarios/filter/profile.test.ts. This matches the plan's own Batch D row (Δ cells 0, Δ wall-time ~0). Well under the 2-minute sign-off threshold; state it in the PR body anyway.

**Risks (as authored):**
- ⚠ THE #1 LANDMINE — the A2/#1213 sub-tolerance VRT trap, and it was set on THIS module. DOCK_MAX_DIFF is 1500 px (workflow-shell-faces.spec.ts:76). #1213's knob→Segmented swap on filter.mode — a WHOLE PRIMITIVE SWAP — moved the dock face only 865 px, i.e. UNDER the gate. Playwright only rewrites a snapshot when the comparison FAILS, so `--update-snapshots` committed ZERO files, twice. If anyone touches a page label, a page membership or the rear group labels here: `git rm` e2e/vrt/__screenshots__/workflow-shell-faces.spec.ts/{darwin,linux}/face-filter-dock.png FIRST, then dispatch. A green dispatch that committed nothing is a RED FLAG, never 'nothing to do'. The same arithmetic means the ordinary VRT gate would not have caught the swap either.
- LIVE BUG IN THIS MODULE'S CARD — FilterCard.svelte:22: `const t = patch.nodes[id]; if (t) t.params.mode = m;`. SyncedStore's proxy transacts with NO origin, and the UndoManager is configured `trackedOrigins: new Set([LOCAL_ORIGIN])` (graph/mutate.ts:13-19), so changing the filter MODE is NOT undoable while cutoff/resonance (which route through cardParams `set()` → setNodeParam → mutateNode → LOCAL_ORIGIN, card-kit.ts:104-106) are. Cmd-Z silently skips the mode change. Fix: `setNodeParam(id, 'mode', m)` and delete the `patch` import. One line, boy-scout, in a file this PR touches anyway.
- THE GATE THAT SHOULD HAVE CAUGHT IT IS STRUCTURALLY BLIND — the CLAUDE.md 'what is this gate unable to see' class, found live. mutate.guard.test.ts:46 is `RAW_PARAM_WRITE = /\.params\[[^\]]+\]\s*=(?![=>])/` — BRACKET-INDEXED writes only. `t.params.mode = m` is DOT notation and cannot match. Its self-test (:78-84) only ever exercises the bracket form, so the negative control is blind in the same direction as the gate. A dot-form scan across packages/web/src/lib finds ~95 unguarded sites, of which a large subset are direct user-action card writes that should be undoable and are not (ShapegenCard:57, SlewSwitchCard:35,41, GatemaidenCard:28, MarblesCard:32,35, AcidwarpCard:86,90,95, LumakeyCard:33, ChromaCard:44-46, ChromakeyCard:43-45, RingsCard:39, ScopeCard:70, ShapesCard:33,38, TempestCard:43, JoystickCard:32-33, WarrenspectrumCard:79, SamsloopCard:544, Moog904a/904b/921/921a/921b/902…). Widen the regex to `/\.params(\[[^\]]+\]|\.[A-Za-z_$][\w$]*)\s*=(?![=>])/`, add the dot form to the self-test, and annotate the legitimate per-frame/programmatic sites. That is a SEPARATE PR (it will red the lane on ~95 lines) — do not fold it into the face wave, but file it from this PR.
- HARDCODED RANGES IN THE CARD — the backdraft ±0.2-vs-±1 class, currently benign but one edit from being a real bug. FilterCard.svelte:35-36 re-types min/max/defaultValue/curve/units for cutoff and resonance instead of reading filterDef.params. They AGREE with the def today, so this is not a live divergence — but nothing enforces it, and every def-reading gate (contract-lock, module-docs-lint, the range asserts) is blind to the card side. Two aggravators in the same file: :15-16 index params POSITIONALLY (`filterDef.params[0]!` / `[1]!`), so reordering the params array silently rebinds the defaults; and :20 `const MODES = ['LP','HP','BP']` is now a SECOND source of truth for the vocabulary the def declares at filter.ts:113-117 — edit the def's options and the legacy card keeps the old captions with nothing failing.
- PAGE-ID ↔ REAR-GROUP-ID COLLISION: filter has one (`modulation` is both a face.pages id and a face.rear.groups id) and it is SAFE — but only by one line, so it must be understood rather than copied. The double-render dx7 documents (dx7.ts:215-224) fires ONLY when the page id collides with the LEADING band group ('voice' or 'signal'), because rearFieldPlan consumes that group at :262-269 and the page loop at :288 re-finds it with no usedGroupIds check. For a NON-leading group the page loop at :288-291 is the INTENDED claim path ('a curated group with the page's id claims the slot, its label wins'), and usedGroupIds (:290) stops the extra-curated loop at :303 re-adding it. Traced by hand for filter: bands = [signal(audio), modulation/'cv depth'(cutoff,res)], holeCount 2+1 = 3 = declared port count. Totality gate green. ⚠ The live constraint is that filter's page ids must NEVER become `signal` or `voice` — its leading rear group is `signal` (filter.ts:180). Say that in the face comment; nothing enforces it.
- UNVERIFIED DOC CLAIM (flagging, not asserting). filter.ts:205 (docs.controls.mode) says highpass 'is built as input-minus-lowpass, so its deep stopband tapers at 6 dB/octave rather than 12'. The DSP uses `fi.resonhp` (filter.dsp:18), which in Faust's filters.lib is a tf2s biquad with an s² numerator — a true 2-pole highpass, 12 dB/oct. filters.lib is not vendored in this checkout so I could not read it; treat the sentence as suspect and settle it by measurement (the ART harness already captures filter — sweep a tone below cutoff at mode=1 and read the slope) before the docs ratchet cements it. The BP claim in the same sentence ('6 dB/oct skirts both sides') is correct for an s-numerator resonbp.
- NO VRT COVERAGE AT THE `full` TIER. Only face-filter-compact (2 cells + glyph) and face-filter-dock (all 5) are pinned. The 'full' 3×2 plate — the tier where filter renders 5 cells and DROPS the glyph — has no baseline on either platform, so a rank 4-6 reshuffle is pixel-invisible. The faces-parity spec exercises it functionally (:650, asserts data-shell-tier='full') but asserts nothing about layout. Do not treat 'VRT green' as proof the plate is right.

**Counts independently re-derived by the fact-checker:**

> INDEPENDENTLY DERIVED FROM packages/web/src/lib/audio/modules/filter.ts — PARAMS = 5: cutoff (log 20..20000, def 1000, unit Hz, :94), resonance (linear 0..0.99, def 0.1, :95), mode (discrete 0..2, def 0, options LP/HP/BP with titles, :112-117), cutoff_cv_amt (linear -1..1, def 1, :118), res_cv_amt (linear -1..1, def 1, :119). PORTS = 4: 3 inputs — audio/audio (:65), cutoff/cv paramTarget=cutoff (:89), res/cv no paramTarget (:90) — plus 1 output audio/audio (:92). face.order = 5 keys, every one a real param id, no invented ids, none of the def's 5 params missing (:147). face.pages = 2 (response: cutoff/resonance/mode; modulation: cutoff_cv_amt/res_cv_amt — :157-158) covering all 5 exactly once. controlFamilies = 0; face.paramCells absent; face.momentary absent. face.rear.groups = 2: signal[audio], modulation[cutoff,res] (:180-181). contract-lock.txt:1112-1121 = 10 lines (1 meta + 3 in + 1 out + 5 param) — matches the spec. LANE BUDGET: 5 ranked ≤ 6 (LANE_PLATE_MAX_CELLS = PLATE_COLS 3 × PLATE_MAX_ROWS 2, curated-face.ts:46) — respected, no ranks 7+, no panels. GLYPH: laneBodyPlan (module-shell-model.ts:328-343) at tier 'full' → rowMax 2, 5 > 2 → plate, cellCount 5, rows = ceil(5/3) = 2 → glyph FALSE; the spec prices this correctly. PAGE-ID vs REAR-GROUP-ID: 'modulation' collides, and the spec's safety analysis is CORRECT — I hand-traced rearFieldPlan: voiceGroup='signal' claims the leading slot (:262-269), the page loop (:287-300) finds curated 'modulation' and adds it to usedGroupIds (:290), the extra-curated loop (:303-307) skips both, 'response' has zero holes and is dropped (:299). No double band. FAMILY/STATIC KEYS: zero ranked, so nothing needs a shell-cells.ts spec. CONTRACT DELTA 0 is arithmetically right — portLine (contract-signature.ts:78-88) has no label branch, the param line (:108-111) emits id/min/max/curve/default/unit only, and face fields are never projected. REAR HOLECOUNT: 3 input holes + 1 output = 4, NOT the 3 the spec states.

**DEFECTS — fix these before building:**

- **[BLOCKER]** The spec's rear-card TOTALITY PROOF is arithmetically wrong: it states "bands = [signal(audio), modulation/'cv depth'(cutoff,res)], holeCount 2+1 = 3 = declared port count. Totality gate green." Both sides of that equals sign are false, and the error is exactly the class it cites elsewhere — a metric invariant to the dimension under test.
  - *evidence:* packages/web/src/lib/ui/workflow/rear-card-model.ts:328-330 — `const holeCount = bands.reduce(...) + outs.length;`. The field's own docstring at :105-107 reads "Total hole count (inputs + outputs) — always equals the declared port count (the no-orphan-holes guarantee, linted)." For filter: 1 (signal) + 2 (modulation) input holes + 1 output = holeCount 4. Declared port count is also 4 — 3 inputs (filter.ts:65, :89, :90) + 1 output (filter.ts:92). The spec used the code's own named field with the outputs term dropped, so its "proof" would have read 3=3 GREEN even if filterDef.outputs had been emptied. That is a false verification of a linted invariant, stated with the code's vocabulary.

- **[MAJOR]** The def CONTRADICTS ITSELF on the HP/BP slopes, and the spec mis-files this as unverifiable ("filters.lib is not vendored ... treat the sentence as suspect") while simultaneously endorsing a roster that says the opposite. No external library is needed to see it — the contradiction is inside filter.ts.
  - *evidence:* filter.ts:205 (docs.controls.mode): HIGHPASS "is built as input-minus-lowpass, so its deep stopband tapers at 6 dB/octave rather than 12" and BANDPASS has "6 dB/octave skirts on both sides". The SAME FILE asserts 12 dB/oct four times: filter.ts:6-7 ("three `fi.reson{lp,hp,bp}` 2-pole (12 dB/oct) sections"), filter.ts:115 (option title "Highpass — ... (12 dB/oct)"), filter.ts:116 (option title "Bandpass — ... (12 dB/oct)"), filter.ts:188 (docs.explanation "All three are two-pole (12 dB/octave)"). The spec's risks[6] asserts the BP-6dB reading "is correct" — which makes filter.ts:116 wrong — while its rationale correction (a) instructs "Do NOT paste the plan's snippet" and treats the shipped roster as untouchable. Those two spec claims cannot both stand. The stated reason for untouchability is also wrong in scope: faces-parity.spec.ts:692 pins only `toHaveText(['LP','HP','BP'])` (the LABELS); `title` is asserted nowhere, so correcting the titles costs zero test churn. The spec's own docs ratchet would cement the contradictory sentence.

- **[MAJOR]** The VRT impact ledger enumerates the WRONG scene set. The spec says "The VRT scene count is unchanged (2 per platform)" and "exactly 2 files move (darwin + linux face-filter-dock.png)", but filter has THREE baseline pairs, and the third one is in the REQUIRED vrt-strict lane and captures the very component the spec's PR edits.
  - *evidence:* e2e/vrt/__screenshots__/vrt.spec.ts/darwin/filter.png and .../linux/filter.png both exist (the legacy FilterCard scene). `filter` is a member of STRICT_VRT_MODULES at e2e/vrt/vrt-exemptions.ts:883 (`'filter', // filter knob card`), and e2e/vrt/vrt.spec.ts:35,50 restrict `task vrt:strict` — the required `vrt-strict (visual regression — strict subset)` context — to that set. The spec's own PR body edits FilterCard.svelte:22 (the setNodeParam bug-fix) and FilterCard.svelte:26/:35-36 discussion. The conclusion ("zero baselines move") happens to survive because setNodeParam is pixel-neutral, but the ledger and the `git rm`-before-dispatch contingency in risks[0] are both incomplete for the lane that gates the merge.

- **[MINOR]** Systematic off-by-N line citations in every file OUTSIDE packages/web/src/lib/{docs,graph,ui} — the e2e specs, the strict-faces registry, and the Faust source. The lib-side citations are exact; these are not.
  - *evidence:* strict-faces.ts:46 → `'filter'` is at packages/web/src/lib/ui/workflow/strict-faces.ts:51; line 46 is where `'filter'` sits in the DIFFERENT file packages/web/src/lib/docs/strict-docs.ts. e2e/vrt/workflow-shell-faces.spec.ts:76 for DOCK_MAX_DIFF → actual :74 (`const DOCK_MAX_DIFF = 1500;`); ":57 FACES table" → the table opens at :43 and the `{ type: 'filter', pages: 2 }` row is :60. e2e/tests/faces-parity.spec.ts:650 for `data-shell-tier','full'` → :653; :661 for `toHaveText('LP')` → :659; :694 for `toHaveText(['LP','HP','BP'])` → :692. packages/dsp/src/filter.dsp:9 for `ba.selectn` → :9 is BLANK, `ba.selectn(3, int(modeKnob), lp, hp, bp)` is :10; ":12" for the ±5-octave mapping → :12 is the comment, the code is :13; "si.smoo (filter.dsp:12-13)" → si.smoo is on :13 and :14; "fi.resonhp (filter.dsp:18)" → :18 is `fi.resonlp`, resonhp is :19.

- **[MINOR]** "filter.dsp declares exactly THREE UI elements (cutoffKnob :6, resKnob :7, modeKnob :8) ... are engine-graph GainNodes, not hsliders" — the framing is wrong for one of the three.
  - *evidence:* packages/dsp/src/filter.dsp:8 is `modeKnob = nentry("mode", 0, 0, 2, 1);` — an `nentry`, not an `hslider`. Only :6 and :7 are hsliders. The count of three UI elements and the strict-superset conclusion hold; the "not hsliders" contrast does not.

- **[MINOR]** "both platforms' files dated Jul 28, regenerated by #1213" is false for half the baselines it names.
  - *evidence:* `ls -la` on e2e/vrt/__screenshots__/workflow-shell-faces.spec.ts: darwin/face-filter-compact.png and linux/face-filter-compact.png are both dated **Jul 26 07:58**; only face-filter-dock.png (darwin + linux) is Jul 28 13:28. This is consistent with the spec's own model — faceTierCap('compact', true) = LANE_ROW_MAX_CELLS_WITH_GLYPH = 2 (curated-face.ts:77, module-shell-model.ts:286), so the compact tile holds only cutoff+resonance and a `mode` primitive swap could not move it — but the stated fact is still wrong.

- **[MINOR]** The `res` port is filed under the wrong classification branch: "its stem is `res`, the param is `resonance` — the tidyVco pwm_cv/pw class". `res` and `pwm_cv` reach the voice band by DIFFERENT branches; they are not the same class.
  - *evidence:* packages/web/src/lib/ui/workflow/rear-card-model.ts:125-129 — `rearTargetParamId` returns a target only for an explicit `paramTarget` or an id ending `_cv`. tidyVco's `pwm_cv` DOES yield target 'pwm' and is then rejected by the `paramIds.has(target)` half of the test at :229. filter's `res` (filter.ts:90) has no `_cv` suffix and no paramTarget, so rearTargetParamId returns `undefined` and `isPerParamCv` is false at the FIRST clause — it never reaches the paramIds test. Same destination (:231 voice.push), different mechanism. Note the def's own comment at filter.ts:83-88 states this correctly ("port id 'res' is intentionally short"), and I confirmed the label table it relies on: patch-panel-labels.ts:55 `res: 'RESONANCE'`.


---

## tomtom

**Verdict: NEEDS_WORK** · archetype: struck percussion voice (drum family: kickdrum · snaredrum · tomtom · clap) — one mono trigger-driven engine, a manual press-pad audition, a per-knob CV on every continuous control, live-audio 'scope' glyph. Already in STRICT_FACES + STRICT_DOCS (strict-faces.ts:48, strict-docs.ts:384).

**Rationale (as authored):** ══ WHY THIS ORDER: because it is already right, and the honest move is to say so rather than re-cut it (the discipline §4 applies to qbrt: 'the draft presented an identical array as a redesign — don't'). Ranks 1-6 are the Vermona DRM1 tom channel — tune / bend / decay / noise / drive plus the modal TONE tilt — an EXTERNALLY ANCHORED curation, not a taste call, and it is exactly the 6 cells the plate can paint (curated-face.ts:46). `tune` at 1 answers 'which drum' (60-90 floor, ~110 mid, 200+ timbale) and is the one control that survives to `mini`. `bend_amt` at 2 is the synth-tom identity itself — 0 = a plain resonator, 24 = the Simmons dive — read against the live trace at `compact`.

I SERIOUSLY CONSIDERED AND REJECTED one re-rank, and record it so it is not re-litigated: **bend_time 7 ↔ drive 6**. FOR: three of the module's own four named corner recipes specify a bend TIME (808 = 40 ms, 909 = 60 ms, Simmons = 200+ ms; docs.explanation), only one specifies drive; and BEND DEPTH is already rank 2, so the lane today ships HALF of a two-knob gesture — structurally the same defect as the program's best-evidenced finding (snaredrum `bounce` 7→5 renders nowhere in the lane). AGAINST, and decisive: `level` was demoted to rank 8 on the drum-family rule that 'tomtom sets loudness with drive'. Demote drive and the lane has NO loudness control at all, which retroactively invalidates the argument that put level at 8. Two demotions cannot both lean on each other. So drive stays at 6 — but the def's stated reason for bend_time at 7 is rewritten, because as written it is wrong (see risks: bend_amt defaults to 7 st, so bend_time is audible at spawn).

══ WHY THESE PAGES: `order` is PRIORITY, `pages` is FUNCTION — they disagree here on purpose and neither is to be 'fixed' to match. `strike` is rank 9 (last) yet the FIRST cell of band 2, because as a priority it is the least important thing on the module and as a function it is the first thing that happens.

Band 1 `membrane · sweep · ring` (tune, bend_amt, bend_time, decay, tone) is ONE IDEA with no residue: it is everything the struck head does — what pitch it settles to, how far and how fast it dives to get there, how long it rings, and how the 1.593× Bessel second mode tilts against the fundamental. Reading order is the physics order. It reunites `bend_amt` (rank 2) with `bend_time`, which the old 4-page cut already had right and which no reader ever needed split from `decay`/`tone` by an 88 px rule.

Band 2 `stick · breath · out` (strike, noise, drive, level) is the excitation and the path out, in signal order: the pad fires it, the stick's impact splashes broadband energy through the skin (NOISE = the def's own BREATH layer), DRIVE saturates the sum, LEVEL leaves through the DC block into the true-peak bound. It is the weakest claim in the spec and I flag it as such in risks — but the alternative 3-page cut costs 88 px and puts the face back over the 720p fold, undoing the change's entire practical benefit.

NO CLUSTERS, and this is a deliberate, measured rejection of the plan's stated PF-9 dependency for this module. types.ts:491-496 prices a page at ~81 px and a cluster at ~14 px — but that arithmetic only holds when the band's cells were going to WRAP anyway. Band 1's five knob columns are 5×46 + 4×10 = 270 px inside a ≥900 px `.faceplate-body` floor: they never wrap. A `ring` sub-header would therefore BUY a second row it did not need — ~14 px caption + ~60 px row ≈ 78 px, i.e. within 10 px of the page it was supposed to replace. Clustering here would spend almost the entire saving to say something the label already says.

══ CORRECTIONS TO §4's tomtom prose, against the real code ══

1. **The plan's stated failure mode for `bend_cv` is WRONG, and so is the def's own comment.** §4 says (and tomtom.ts:143-146 repeats) that without the pin `bend_cv` 'would fall out of the sweep band into the orphan "cv" tail'. It would not. rear-card-model.ts:229 computes `isPerParamCv = target !== undefined && (port.paramTarget !== undefined || paramIds.has(target))`; for bend_cv the stem is 'bend', tomtom declares no param 'bend' (the param is `bend_amt`, tomtom.ts:72) and no paramTarget — so isPerParamCv is FALSE and :231 pushes it into `voice`, not into the orphan band at :310-312 (which only ever catches ports that ARE per-param CVs whose target no page claims — see the synthetic negative control at rear-card-model.test.ts:225-238, which uses an explicit `paramTarget`). So the un-pinned failure is that BEND appears in the leading `strike · voice` performance band, next to TRIGGER / ACCENT / PITCH. That is a WORSE outcome than the plan describes (it corrupts the band a player reads while dragging a cable, rather than dumping it in a visibly-junk tail), and it is invisible to every gate. The pin stays MANDATORY; the reason in the code comment must be corrected.

2. **'must be re-pointed at the new page id' — no rename is needed, and renaming is the risk.** I keep `id: 'sweep'` for page 1 and leave the rear group id at `'sweep'`, so the pin keeps claiming the page slot with zero churn (rear-card-model.ts:288). Only the group's LABEL moves, 'membrane · sweep' → 'membrane · sweep · ring', so the rear band header matches the front band it mirrors now that DECAY and TONE derive into it.

3. **The page-id/rear-group-id collision rule needs stating precisely, because 'any collision' is the wrong rule.** A curated group id matching a NON-voice page id is the PINNING MECHANISM, not a pathology — tomtom ships that today (`sweep`) and the rear totality gate is green, which is my negative control. The pathology is specific to the RESERVED slots: rear-card-model.ts:262-269 claims the voice slot for a group whose id is `voice` or `signal`, and the page loop at :287-291 does not skip an already-used group, so a page ALSO named `voice`/`signal` renders that band twice. tomtom's page ids (`sweep`, `stick`) touch neither, nor `cv` (the orphan band id at :311). VERIFIED SAFE.

4. **The dependency list is wrong: tomtom needs none of PF-6f, PF-2 or PF-9** (see controlFamilies). It is buildable against main today, ahead of the rest of batch E.

5. **PF-11 does not apply to tomtom** — its ART pins hash packages/dsp sources only (contractDelta). §5's ART table already says 'no'; §4's batch header does not, so state it.

6. **§1's 'the fold is not tight' does not generalize to this face** — it was sampled on the two 2-band faces; tomtom is a 4-band face at 194 px overflow with its audition pad below the cut (vrtImpact). For tomtom the budget argument is not merely available, it is the strongest argument there is.

══ WHAT I DID NOT TOUCH ══ `glyph: 'scope'` stays. The dx7 objection to 'scope' (it flatlines whenever nothing is gated, which is most of the time you look at a rack) is TRUE of a drum too — but dx7 had a data-derived replacement and tomtom does not, and §2 explicitly demotes the `transient` glyph to a dedicated glyph wave. Churning it here would move both baselines for an unspecified renderer. `face.rear.audioRate` stays exactly as declared: VERIFIED against packages/dsp/src/tomtom.ts:158-180 — all nine continuous CVs are read `[s]` inside the per-sample loop with no smoothing, while `strike` is read ONCE per block at :156 (k-rate), and `trigger_in`/`accent_in` are rightly excluded because the trigger's meaning is its edge and accent is latched at that edge. `face.momentary: ['strike']` stays — required by module-face-lint.test.ts:411-413 (a momentary id must be ranked) and :419-439 (every 0..1-discrete-default-0 param on a STRICT_FACES module must be classified). NO CARD/DEF DIVERGENCE FOUND: TomtomCard.svelte hardcodes min/max on all 8 faders (:110-113, :119-121, :127) and every one of them AGREES with the def (tomtom.ts:71-78) — 60..400, 0..24, 10..300, 40..1500, 0..1 ×3, −24..12. So there is no live bug of the backdraft class here. It is still a latent hazard — the card single-sources `defaultValue` through cardParams but re-types the ranges — and the boy-scout fix (import the ranges, or derive them from the def like `defaultFor` does) is cheap if this PR is already touching the module. Flagging, not requiring.

```ts
face: {
  order: ["tune", "bend_amt", "decay", "tone", "noise", "drive", "bend_time", "level", "strike"],
  pages: [
    { id: "sweep", label: "membrane \u00b7 sweep \u00b7 ring", controls: ["tune", "bend_amt", "bend_time", "decay", "tone"] },
    { id: "stick", label: "stick \u00b7 breath \u00b7 out", controls: ["strike", "noise", "drive", "level"] },
  ],
  glyph: "scope",
}
```

**New params:**
- NONE. `TomtomParams` is a 1:1 mirror of the 9 ParamDefs and the Faust-free worklet exposes no tenth control (packages/dsp/src/tomtom.ts:87 parameterDescriptors). No PF-1 `options` (every continuous knob is a real continuum — a Segmented would lie exactly the way §2 says it would for qbrt's `mode`). No PF-3 `format` (all four that need units already declare them: Hz/st/ms/dB, tomtom.ts:71-78; tone/noise/drive are honest 0..1 balances, and PF-3's own warning is that an ungated persistent readout moves ~17 dock baselines for nothing). No PF-10 `landmarks`.

**Control families:**
- NONE — and this is a correction to the plan. §4 Batch E lists tomtom as depending on PF-6f (or PF-6). It does not. PF-6f exists to give karplus/sixstrum/qbrt/kickdrum/snaredrum an AUDITION affordance they cannot rank, because their strike is an engine.read() call or nothing at all. tomtom's audition IS a real ParamDef (`strike`, tomtom.ts:81), declared on face.momentary (tomtom.ts:130), rendered as a press-and-release <Button> by ModuleShell.svelte:430-445, and driven+asserted by faces-parity's 'momentary' branch (faces-parity.spec.ts:327-345, which asserts release RETURNS TO REST). The plan's own §5 loss table (line 1197) agrees: 'tomtom's STRIKE is fully covered by face.momentary'. Adding a family would be +1 contract line for nothing.
- NONE — PF-2 (param→Toggle) is a snaredrum/kickdrum `hard` concern; tomtom has no latching switch. PF-9 (page clusters) has ALREADY LANDED (types.ts:503, curated-face.ts:184-211, ModuleShell.svelte:797-806) and is deliberately NOT used here — see risks. NET: tomtom's face needs ZERO platform work and is buildable against main TODAY.

**Control-loss ledger:**
- ZERO control loss, verified line by line against TomtomCard.svelte. The card exposes exactly 9 interactive controls: 8 <Fader>s (tune :110, bend_amt :111, bend_time :112, decay :113, tone :119, noise :120, drive :121, level :127) and the STRIKE <button data-testid="tomtom-strike"> (:128-136). All 9 are ranked; all 9 land on a page; the dock plan renders each exactly once (module-face-lint.test.ts:260-323 pins that). The card's PatchPanel sections (:66-97) are replaced by the derived rear card, which renders all 11 inputs + 1 output (rear totality gate, module-face-lint.test.ts:749-778).
- TIER loss, UNCHANGED by this spec but stated so nobody rediscovers it as a bug: `bend_time` (rank 7), `level` (rank 8) and `strike` (rank 9) render in NO lane tier — LANE_PLATE_MAX_CELLS = PLATE_COLS × PLATE_MAX_ROWS = 6 (curated-face.ts:46). They are DOCK-ONLY. Today `strike` is worse than dock-only: it is BELOW THE DOCK FOLD too (see vrtImpact). This spec's whole practical payoff is putting it back on screen.
- GLYPH loss at the `full` lane tier, ALREADY TRUE today and unchanged: 6 selected cells → rows = 2 → laneBodyPlan returns `glyph: hasGlyph && rows <= 1` = false (module-shell-model.ts:340-342). The scope glyph survives at mini (1 cell), compact (2 cells) and the dock hero. Any promotion of a 7th control would change nothing here; any DEMOTION to 3 ranked controls would buy the glyph back — not proposed.
- LABEL-vocabulary loss on the rear (a migration-design consequence, not this PR's): the legacy card's hand-typed jack abbreviations BTIM / DEC / NSE / DRV / LVL / TRIG / ACC / V-OCT (TomtomCard.svelte:70-95) are replaced by the derived FUNCTION labels — B TIME / DECAY / NOISE / DRIVE / LEVEL from the ParamDef labels, and TRIGGER / ACCENT / PITCH from resolveVerboseLabel after PF-5's `_in`/`_out` strip (patch-panel-labels.ts:168-175). Longer, but correct and single-sourced. No action.
- NOTHING is deliberately dropped. There is no control on either surface that this face declines to rank.

**Contract delta (claimed):** EXACTLY 0 lines. tomtom owns contract-lock.txt:3336-3357 (22 lines: 1 meta + 11 in + 1 out + 9 param). `face` is not read by contract-signature.ts at all — serializeModuleContract (:92-127) emits only meta / in / out / param / stereo / expose / family lines, and there is no `face` field in ContractDefLike (:35-53). This spec adds no param, no family, no port, and changes no range/curve/default/unit/edge. `flox activate -- task docs:accept` must produce an EMPTY diff on contract-lock.txt — if it produces ANY line, something in the change touched the contract and is a bug, not an accept. ⚠ ART is also 0: art/scenarios/tomtom/profile.test.ts:86-92 and :160-167 pin `dspSourceSha(...)`, which hashes packages/dsp/src files only (art/setup/capture.ts:58-65) — it does NOT hash the web def, so tomtom is NOT a PF-11 case (that is delay/scaler/polarizer/illogic/depolarizer, which use docsStrippedRepoSourceSha on the def). Attest is 0: packages/web/src/lib/audio/modules/** is in neither the collab basis (collab-attest-lib.ts:57-117) nor the WebGL basis (audio def), so no docs-hash-ignore markers and no re-attest.

**VRT impact (claimed):** MOVES: `darwin/face-tomtom-dock` — hard, and by design. Both band headers change text and band 2's membership goes from 2 knobs (DECAY, TONE) to 4 cells (STRIKE pad, NOISE, DRIVE, LEVEL) while DECAY/TONE move up into band 1. Re-capture locally (`REPEAT=3 flox activate -- task vrt:one -- tomtom`) and INSPECT the PNG. UNCHANGED (assert, do not assume): `darwin/face-tomtom-compact` — the compact tile paints ranks 1-2 (tune, bend_amt) + the scope glyph and `order` is untouched, so it must be pixel-identical; a diff there means something else moved. ══ THE MEASURED FINDING, and a CORRECTION TO THE PLAN'S §1 ══ §1 concluded 'band merges must be justified by grouping semantics, not budget' from two samples — face-filter-dock and face-mixer-dock, both TWO-band faces. That generalization is FALSE for tomtom. `.dock-faceplate` caps at `min(60vh, 680px)` = 425 px in the 1280×720 VRT viewport (DockFullView.svelte:237), and the committed measurement table (vrt-exemptions.ts:1029-1031) records tomtom's post-PF-8 dock overflow at **194 px**. The committed baseline PNG (1220×425) shows it directly: hero + `membrane · sweep` + `membrane · ring`, and NOTHING ELSE. Bands 3 (`breath · heat`) and 4 (`output · play`) are entirely below the fold — which means the STRIKE pad, the module's only self-audition affordance and already dock-only at rank 9, is currently UNREACHABLE in the 720p dock without scrolling. At 2 pages the predicted overflow is 194 − 2×88 = **18 px**, exactly the figure the three other 2-page faces (filter, lfo, qbrt) already carry, of which filter is the like-for-like 'scope'-glyph comparator. 18 px is the bottom padding of `.faceplate-scroll`, not a control. So this merge is justified BOTH semantically and by a measured, cross-validated budget on THIS face. (At 1080p `min(60vh,680)` = 648 px and the fold never bit — this is strictly a 720p concern, and it is the size CI renders at.) ══ EXEMPTIONS / RATCHET ══ `linux/face-tomtom-compact` (vrt-exemptions.ts:1010) and `linux/face-tomtom-dock` (:1011) are pending pairs, so vrt.spec's linux run test.skip()s them UNCONDITIONALLY. Per CLAUDE.md, DRAIN BEFORE DISPATCH: delete both lines AND lower the vrt-meta linux-deficit ceiling by the same count in the SAME commit — 119 → **117** (vrt-meta.test.ts:469, `toBeLessThanOrEqual(119)`; today's actual linux-pending count is exactly 119). If batch E lands as one PR with snaredrum's two pairs (:1008-1009), it is 119 → **115** — the integrator must bump ONCE to the true final count, not twice. Push that commit, THEN `flox activate -- gh workflow run vrt-update.yml -f ref=<branch> -f platform=linux`, UNSCOPED (`-f grep=…` dies as startup_failure); the bot's push lands the follow-on runs in `action_required`, so approve them. Keep darwin local. ══ STRUCTURAL GATE ══ `e2e/vrt/workflow-shell-faces.spec.ts:55` `{ type: 'tomtom', pages: 4 }` → `pages: 2`. That drives the pre-pixel assert at :222 (`face-page` toHaveCount) and fails BEFORE the image compare if a page is dropped. It is the ONLY page-count pin on tomtom: e2e/tests/p1-batch2-faces.spec.ts covers dx7 + sixstrum only, e2e/tests/workflow-shell-faces.spec.ts covers adsr + kickdrum only, and no spec anywhere references a tomtom `data-face-page` id — so page ids are free, and I keep `sweep` by choice, not by constraint. No rear VRT scene exists for tomtom (workflow-rear-card.spec.ts pins tidyVco/vca/dx7/sixstrum), so the rear label change is pixel-free.

**CI delta (claimed):** ≈ 0 s (bounded −1 s to +1 s; well under the 2-minute sign-off threshold). faces-parity's budget is FACE_FIXED_MS + FACE_PER_CELL_MS × cells.length (faces-parity.spec.ts:597) and the cell count is UNCHANGED at 9 (9 params + 0 families) — the plan's own CI table (line 1328) attributes batch E's +2.4 s entirely to karplus +1 and snaredrum +2 cells, i.e. tomtom Δcells = 0, which I confirm. VRT scene count is unchanged (2 tomtom scenes; no new scenes). The dock faceplate renders 2 section bands instead of 4, so marginally less DOM per dock scene. The one real cost is OFF the per-PR path: one `vrt-update.yml -f platform=linux` dispatch to capture the two drained tomtom baselines. ⚠ Confirm ON CI, not locally — CI is SwiftShader.

**Risks (as authored):**
- THE ONE UNGATED COUPLING: page id `sweep` ↔ rear group id `sweep` is load-bearing and NOTHING enforces it. rearFieldPlan's page loop (rear-card-model.ts:287-291) matches a curated group by page id, which is how tune_cv/bend_cv/bend_time_cv land in page position with the group's label winning. Rename the page id without the group id and `bend_cv` silently misfiles — and the rear totality gate STAYS GREEN, because the port still renders exactly once. Mitigation now: keep `id: 'sweep'` (zero churn) and say so in the def comment. Mitigation properly: a new module-face-lint clause — for every STRICT_FACES module, every `<stem>_cv` INPUT whose stem is not a declared param id and which declares no paramTarget MUST appear in a `face.rear.groups` entry. That is precise (it fires on tomtom bend_cv, tidyVco pwm_cv) and vacuous-free. Recommended, ~15 LOC, optional to this PR.
- LATENT PLATFORM HOLE, found while checking the prompt's collision rule and worth filing regardless of tomtom: rearFieldPlan claims the voice slot at :262-269 and adds the group to `usedGroupIds`, but the page loop at :287-291 does NOT skip an already-used group id. So a module with a curated rear group `{id:'voice'}` AND a page `{id:'voice'}` renders that band TWICE and reddens the rear-totality gate. dx7 works around it by hand (`id:'patch', label:'voice'` — the workaround is documented in p1-batch2-faces.spec.ts:40-47). There is no lint rule; the next author hits it the same way. tomtom is SAFE (rear group ids `voice`+`sweep`; page ids `sweep`+`stick`; no page is named voice/signal/cv), but the hole should be closed with a 3-line `if (usedGroupIds.has(g.id)) g = undefined` or a lint clause.
- THE WEAKEST PART OF THIS SPEC, stated so a reviewer can attack it: page 2 `stick · breath · out` is a 3-token label, which by §1's own test is the tell of a residual band ('everything that is not the membrane'). My defence is that it is 4 cells (under the 6+ threshold §1 sets for rejection) and that they are SEQUENTIAL STAGES OF ONE PATH, not a grab-bag: strike fires it, the stick's impact splashes the BREATH layer through the skin (the def's own mechanism, docs.explanation), DRIVE saturates the sum, LEVEL leaves. If a reviewer rejects it, the fallback is 3 pages (`membrane · sweep · ring` / `stick · breath` / `heat · out`), which costs 88 px and puts the face back over the fold at 106 px — i.e. the fallback re-creates the defect this change exists to fix. I would take the 3-token label.
- THE 18 px PREDICTION IS DERIVED, NOT MEASURED — validate the instrument before believing it. It comes from the committed overflow table (vrt-exemptions.ts:1026-1032) plus a per-band constant of 88 px cross-checked six ways across nine faces (2-page qbrt/filter/lfo = 18; 3-page karplus = 106; 4-page tomtom = 194; 5-page snaredrum/tidyVco = 282; 6-page kickdrum/sixstrum = 370 — every step exactly 88). karplus is the like-for-like comparator (same 'scope' glyph) and 106 + 88 = 194 reproduces tomtom exactly. Still: RE-MEASURE the post-change overflow with the same probe that produced that table before writing '18' in the PR body. A wrong number here reads exactly like a finding.
- The `bend_time` demotion argument in the def is FACTUALLY SHAKY and must be rewritten in this same commit. tomtom.ts:102-104 says bend_time 'never outranks a knob that always does something' because it is 'inaudible while BEND sits at 0' — but `bend_amt` DEFAULTS TO 7 st (tomtom.ts:72), so bend_time is audible on a fresh spawn. The honest form: bend_time is the only continuous knob on this module with a genuine INERT REGION (bend_amt = 0), and it is the sculptor's half of a pair whose depth half is already rank 2. This is a doc-accuracy fix, not a re-rank — see rationale for the re-rank I considered and rejected.
- The rear-curation comment at tomtom.ts:143-146 is WRONG about the derivation and must be corrected in the same commit (see rationale). Leaving it means the next author 'fixes' a failure mode that cannot occur and misses the one that can.
- VRT regen ordering: the darwin dock diff will be far above DOCK_MAX_DIFF (1500 px) — two band headers change text and two whole bands of content move above the fold — so `--update-snapshots` will rewrite it. But per the A2/#1213 precedent, a sub-tolerance render change is invisible to BOTH the gate and the regen; if the local regen comes back having committed nothing for face-tomtom-dock, treat that as a RED FLAG and `git rm` the baseline first rather than concluding 'nothing to do'.
- Shared-file conflict surface: this PR edits e2e/vrt/workflow-shell-faces.spec.ts (the FACES table), e2e/vrt/vrt-exemptions.ts and packages/web/src/lib/audio/modules/vrt-meta.test.ts — all three are on CLAUDE.md's post-merge conflict-sweep list, and batch E's other two modules (karplus 3→2, snaredrum 5→4) touch the same lines. Land them together or run `task pr:conflict-sweep` after each merge; NEVER `gh pr update-branch` on these.

**Counts independently re-derived by the fact-checker:**

> 9 params — tune, bend_amt, bend_time, decay, tone, noise, drive, level, strike (tomtom.ts:70-82). 11 inputs — trigger_in, accent_in, pitch_cv, bend_cv, decay_cv, tone_cv, noise_cv, tune_cv, bend_time_cv, drive_cv, level_cv (tomtom.ts:47-66). 1 output — audio_out (tomtom.ts:67-69). contract-lock.txt:3336-3357 = 22 lines (1 meta + 11 in + 1 out + 9 param). DSP PARAM_SPECS = 9 entries (packages/dsp/src/tomtom.ts:79-88). ALL AGREE with the spec. Every id in the proposed order (9) and pages (5+4=9) exists in the def; no param is dropped; no id invented. Card ranges (TomtomCard.svelte:110-113,119-121,127) = 60..400 / 0..24 / 10..300 / 40..1500 / 0..1 x3 / -24..12, identical to the def — no card/def divergence. Lane budget respected (LANE_PLATE_MAX_CELLS = PLATE_COLS 3 x PLATE_MAX_ROWS 2 = 6, curated-face.ts:46 / module-shell-model.ts:288-289); no panels, no families, so nothing to resolve in shell-cells.ts. Glyph: 6 cells at 'full' -> rows 2 -> laneBodyPlan returns glyph false (module-shell-model.ts:342) — the spec states this correctly and does not claim an in-lane glyph. Page ids sweep/stick vs rear group ids voice/sweep: the sweep match is the PIN (rear-card-model.ts:288), not the double-render pathology (only 'voice'/'signal' hit that, :262-269 vs :287-291); verified rear totality holds at 11 in + 1 out = 12 holes across 3 bands. Contract delta 0 confirmed: serializeModuleContract (contract-signature.ts:92-129) never reads `face`; ContractDefLike (:35-54) has no face field. linuxPending in EXEMPT_BASELINE_PAIRS counted programmatically = 119, matching vrt-meta.test.ts's ceiling exactly; karplus has ZERO pending pairs, so batch E drains exactly 4 (119 -> 115) as the spec says.

**DEFECTS — fix these before building:**

- **[MAJOR]** "VRT scene count is unchanged (2 tomtom scenes; no new scenes)" — there are FIVE tomtom VRT scenes, not two, and the vrtImpact ledger never names the other three.
  - *evidence:* e2e/vrt/vrt-karplus-tomtom-states.spec.ts:74 ('tomtom-simmons-zap'), :82 ('tomtom-timbale-tight'), :91 ('tomtom-strike-held') — three committed scenes with baselines on BOTH platforms (e2e/vrt/__screenshots__/vrt-karplus-tomtom-states.spec.ts/{darwin,linux}/tomtom-*.png), registered in vrt.config.ts:52. The spec asserts the count twice (ciDelta and vrtImpact) and both are wrong. I verified the conclusion survives — those scenes capture the LEGACY card (`.svelte-flow__node-tomtom` at /rack, spec L129) and I opened tomtom-strike-held.png: it is the MEMBRANE/COLOR/OUT fader card with the orange .held STRIKE button, untouched by a `face` change. But the spec's own rule is "UNCHANGED (assert, do not assume)", and the one scene it omits is literally the STRIKE pin — the control this face relocates. The ledger must name all five and state why three do not move.

- **[MAJOR]** "a per-band constant of 88 px cross-checked six ways across nine faces ... every step exactly 88" — the SAME committed line the spec cites contains three rows that falsify the linear model, and the spec omits all three.
  - *evidence:* e2e/vrt/vrt-exemptions.ts:1030-1032 reads in full: "cloudseed 498, kickdrum/sixstrum 370, snaredrum/tidyVco 282, tomtom 194, dx7 166, karplus 106, shimmershine 58, filter/lfo/qbrt 18". Cross-referencing the page counts in e2e/vrt/workflow-shell-faces.spec.ts: shimmershine :56 = 3 pages measuring 58 while karplus :57 = 3 pages measuring 106 — two same-page-count faces 48 px apart, so the per-band cost is NOT a constant. dx7 :52 = 4 pages measuring 166 where the model demands 194 (tomtom's own figure). cloudseed :50 = 8 pages measuring 498 where 18 + 88x6 = 546. The spec's nine 'cross-checks' are exactly the nine rows that fit; the three that do not are on the same line and are never mentioned. The '18 px' post-merge prediction is therefore uncross-validated — overflow is a function of band CONTENT (wrap/rows/hero), not page count. (The first-principles case for tomtom specifically still holds: I read face-tomtom-dock.png and the band pitch is ~88-90 px with 3- and 2-knob bands, and 5 columns = 5x46 + 4x10 = 270 px cannot wrap inside the 900 px .faceplate-body floor at DockFullView.svelte:122/164/226. Say that instead of claiming a validated constant.)

- **[MINOR]** "packages/dsp/src/tomtom.ts:87 parameterDescriptors" — wrong line; :87 is a PARAM_SPECS table row.
  - *evidence:* packages/dsp/src/tomtom.ts:87 is `['strike', 0, 0, 1],`, the last entry of the PARAM_SPECS array which closes at :88. `static get parameterDescriptors()` is at :116, with `automationRate: (name === 'strike' ? 'k-rate' : 'a-rate')` at :124. The substantive claim (9 descriptors, no tenth control) is TRUE and independently verified.

- **[MINOR]** "vrt-meta.test.ts:469, `toBeLessThanOrEqual(119)`" — off by one.
  - *evidence:* packages/web/src/lib/audio/modules/vrt-meta.test.ts:468 is `).toBeLessThanOrEqual(119);` (the other ceiling, the stale-exemption ratchet, is at :508). The VALUE and the arithmetic are correct: I counted EXEMPT_BASELINE_PAIRS programmatically = 127 total, 119 starting 'linux/', and karplus has zero pending pairs, so 119->117 (tomtom alone) / 119->115 (with snaredrum's :1008-1009) is right.

- **[MINOR]** "`.dock-faceplate` caps at `min(60vh, 680px)` = 425 px in the 1280x720 VRT viewport" — 60vh of 720 px is 432 px; 425 is an observed screenshot height presented as a derivation.
  - *evidence:* e2e/vrt/vrt.config.ts:226 pins `viewport: { width: 1280, height: 720 }`; DockFullView.svelte:237 is `max-height: min(60vh, 680px)` with `padding-bottom: 4px` at :239. 0.60 x 720 = 432, not 425. The 425 figure is the committed baseline's actual height (face-tomtom-dock.png is 1220x425, verified) and is quoted verbatim from vrt-exemptions.ts:1027-1028 — so the number is fine, the stated arithmetic is not. Per CLAUDE.md's 'state the units / validate the instrument' rule this should read 'measured 425 px' not '= 425 px'.

- **[MINOR]** Citation drift on five references — all substantively correct, all off by 1-3 lines, in a spec whose whole value proposition is line-exact evidence.
  - *evidence:* serializeModuleContract cited :92-127, actually contract-signature.ts:92-129; ContractDefLike cited :35-53, actually :35-54; art/scenarios/tomtom/profile.test.ts cited :160-167, the second dspSourceSha is at :161; rear-card-model.test.ts cited :225-238, the negative-control `it(` opens at :224; TomtomCard.svelte:93 labels the jack 'V/OCT' not 'V-OCT'. (Counter-note: the load-bearing citations are EXACT — module-face-lint.test.ts:411-413 and :419-439, faces-parity.spec.ts:597, ModuleShell.svelte:430-445 and :797-806, DockFullView.svelte:237, types.ts:491-496/:503, p1-batch2-faces.spec.ts:40-47, plan line 1197, workflow-shell-faces.spec.ts:55, packages/dsp/src/tomtom.ts:156.)

- **[MINOR]** The spec silently renames the plan's page-2 label and omits it from its own numbered corrections list.
  - *evidence:* .myrobots/plans/dx7-and-faces-design-program-2026-07-27.md:955 specifies `stick · heat · out`; the spec ships `stick · breath · out` and discusses the label at length in risks/rationale without ever flagging it as a deviation, while listing six other numbered '§4 CORRECTIONS'. Given the spec's own standard ('a correction is more valuable than the spec itself'), the divergence ledger is incomplete. Substantively the change is defensible — the def's docs.explanation calls the layer BREATH, and NOISE is that layer — but it must be declared.


---

## karplus

**Verdict: NEEDS_WORK** · archetype: Externally-struck monophonic VOICE — a physical-model string with NO internal exciter. Unlike every other voice on the bar (dx7/sixstrum/tidyVco self-oscillate; kick/snare/tom ship a `strike` press-PARAM), karplus can only sound when something fires `trigger_in` (karplus.ts:55, edge:'trigger'). Its audition affordance is not a param and not a family — it is an engine `read()` seam (karplus.ts:288-295), which is why it is structurally unrankable today.

**Rationale (as authored):** WHAT IT IS (recipe Step 2): the one voice in the rack that cannot make a sound by itself. It has no oscillator and no envelope generator — a noise burst is fired into a recirculating delay-line string and everything you hear is that burst dying (karplus.ts:163). The verb a player performs on it is STRIKE, and the strike comes from somewhere else. Every ranking below descends from that.

WHY THIS ORDER. Ranks 1-2 (DECAY, BRIGHT) are already shipped and already right: on a struck string with no ADSR, the decay envelope IS the instrument and BRIGHT is the material — the two knobs that decide whether this is a koto, a nylon guitar or a steel harp. They own the compact tile beside the live scope, and the scope is the correct glyph precisely because a plucked voice's decay is its identity.

The plan's two changes to ranks 3-8 both hold up against the code, and I am adopting them: LEVEL 8 → 6 and POSITION 6 → 7. The def's own comment (:100-101) justified level-last with "as on every face" — generic reasoning, and wrong here. karplus's param surface carries NO output bound: no `ceiling`, no `hard`, no `drive` (contract-lock.txt:1441-1448), unlike kickdrum (`ceiling` :1487, `hard` :1493) and snaredrum (:3022, :3028). Its own `docs.controls.level` (karplus.ts:210-211) says outright "nothing normalizes the voice's loudness across settings — a bright, long, hard-picked note is far hotter than a dark mallet thump" — and the knob that causes that blow-up, BRIGHT, is rank 2 in the same tile. A trim whose failure mode is created two cells to its left belongs in the lane. (CORRECTION to the plan: it calls karplus "the only voice with no output bound". On the param surface SIXSTRUM — six karplus strings — is equally unbounded, `level` and nothing else, contract-lock.txt:2940-2958. The comparative claim overstates; drop it. The karplus-specific argument stands without it.)

WHY `order` AND `pages` DISAGREE, deliberately. `order` says the strike is rank 7 — below the lane cap. `pages` puts it FIRST in its band. Both are right, because they answer different questions: `order` is priority for tiers that show a subset, `pages` is function order for the tier that shows everything. Rank 7 says "the lane cannot usefully paint this"; page-first says "when the band IS painted, this is what you reach for". Anyone who 'fixes' one to match the other breaks the face. Say it in the comment.

WHY THE STRIKE IS DOCK-ONLY, and it is a judgement call. The strike is the module's defining affordance, so rank 1 is arguable. Two things decide it against. (a) RENDER: ModuleShell.svelte:581 paints a lane `action` cell as a bare `▸` with no caption — an unlabelled triangle in a 46px column, where the `selector` cell one branch up (:562) does fall back to `ctl.label`. Promoting the strike into the lane buys a mystery glyph and costs LEVEL its slot. (b) WORKFLOW: you audition while you dial, and you dial in the dock — the `full` plate is six 26px `sm` knobs. The dock is one click away at every tier (`offersFullView` returns true unconditionally, module-shell-model.ts:245-247), so "dock-only" here means one click, not unreachable, which is the whole difference between this and the SEVERE loss it fixes. Revert if you disagree: swap ranks 6 and 7 and nothing else moves.

WHY 2 PAGES, NOT 3. The old `out` page held ONE knob for a whole ~81px band — exactly the purchase ModuleFacePage.clusters (graph/types.ts:491-496) exists to refuse. But I am correcting the plan's stated reason: it merged on the argument that "the output trim exists BECAUSE the exciter settings change the loudness — COLOR/BURST/POS/LEVEL are one idea". Checked against the prose it cites: `docs.controls.level` blames a "bright, long, hard-picked" note — bright = BRIGHT (page 1), long = DECAY (page 1), hard-picked = COLOR/BURST (page 2). The causes are split ACROSS both pages, so that argument is half true at best. The honest framing is simpler: page 1 is THE STRING (what rings and how long), page 2 is EVERYTHING THAT IS NOT THE STRING — the thing that hits it, and what comes out. `pick · strike · out` names all three ideas and, usefully, describes the rear band it projects onto exactly (COLOR = pick tone, BURST = strike length, POS = pick position, LEVEL = out), so no `face.rear.groups` label override is needed.

REAR RE-DERIVED ON PAPER (recipe Step 5), because the pages moved. `pageOfParam` becomes tune/decay/brightness/stiffness → `string`; color/burst/position/LEVEL → `pick`. Every one of the eight knob CVs carries an explicit `paramTarget` (karplus.ts:62-69), so `rearTargetParamId` never has to guess a stem — karplus has NO `bend_cv`/`pwm_cv` orphan class. `level_cv` follows `level` out of the dead `out` band into `pick`. Result: band `voice` (label 'play') 4 holes + band `string` 4 + band `pick` 4 + outputs rail 1 = 13 = 12 declared inputs + 1 output. ZERO orphans, no `cv` tail band, totality gate (module-face-lint.test.ts:749-778) green. The rear is otherwise UNCHANGED, and correctly so: the two-hands split (striking hand = trigger+accent, which are one gesture because accent is latched AT the trigger edge, karplus.ts:170-171; fretting hand = pitch+damp) is the best-argued rear in the repo.

audioRate VERIFIED, NOT INHERITED (recipe Step 6). `audioRate: ['pitch']` is correct: packages/dsp/src/karplus.ts:150 reads `p.pitchCv = inPitch ? (inPitch[s] ?? 0) : 0` inside the per-sample loop, while every knob CV goes through a per-param `WtParamSmoother` at 80 Hz (:90-99) — a-rate on paper, control-rate in the ear. Add the missing line number to the def's citation (it currently cites the expression without one). `trigger_in`/`damp_in` stay un-ticked: their ▲/▬ edge glyphs already carry the meaning.

TIER LADDER, read back as a sentence: mini shows DECAY and a live scope trace; compact shows DECAY + BRIGHT and the scope; the full-in-lane plate shows DECAY, BRIGHT, TUNE, COLOR, BURST, LEVEL and NO scope (6 cells ⇒ 2 rows ⇒ `glyph = hasGlyph && rows <= 1` is false, module-shell-model.ts:340-342 — the def comment's "most informative pixel budget" claim must be scoped to mini/compact/dock-hero); the dock shows all nine in two bands. Selected === rendered at all three lane tiers, so the cap↔fit-plan lint (module-face-lint.test.ts:677-695) stays green.

```ts
face: {
  order: ["decay", "brightness", "tune", "color", "burst", "level", "karplus-strike-{n}", "position", "stiffness"],
  pages: [
    { id: "string", label: "string \u00b7 ring", controls: ["tune", "decay", "brightness", "stiffness"] },
    { id: "pick", label: "pick \u00b7 strike \u00b7 out", controls: ["karplus-strike-{n}", "color", "burst", "position", "level"] },
  ],
  glyph: "scope",
  paramCells: none — karplus declares no `face.paramCells`. All eight params are continuous (contract-lock.txt:1441-1448: every one is `log` or `linear`, none `discrete`), so `looksLikeSwitch` (shell-control-kind.ts:56-58) matches nothing: no `face.momentary` entry is needed, no `ACKNOWLEDGED_LATCHING` entry is needed, and `paramCellKind` resolves all eight to `'knob'`. The three module-face-lint momentary clauses (module-face-lint.test.ts:391-439) are vacuously green for karplus and stay that way.,
}
```

**New params:**
- NONE. This is a finding, not a shortfall (recipe Step 8). karplus.test.ts:93 asserts `Proc.parameterDescriptors.length === karplusDef.params.length` — the worklet exposes EXACTLY the eight def params, so there is ZERO un-exposed DSP capability to promote. The only un-ranked capability on the module is the PLUCK, and it is deliberately NOT a param: a `strike` press-param (the tomtom/kickdrum shape) would add a `parameterDescriptor`, break karplus.test.ts:93, and — because `art/scenarios/karplus/profile.test.ts:118-123` pins `dspSourceSha('karplus.ts', 'lib/karplus-dsp.ts', 'lib/analog-delay-core.ts', 'lib/dsp-utils.ts')` — force an ART re-pin of BOTH karplus baselines. The `controlFamily` + host-ConstantSource route (PF-6f) moves neither.

**Control families:**
- karplus-strike

**Control-loss ledger:**
- PLUCK / ⟋ STRIKE — RECOVERED (this is the whole point of the spec). `KarplusCard.svelte:123-129` fires `engine.read(node,'manualTrigger')` → `karplus.ts:288-295` → `fireTrigger(strikeCs, ctx.currentTime)` on a dedicated ConstantSource summed into worklet input 0 (karplus.ts:245-248). It is neither a param nor a declared family nor a legend static (karplus has NO `e2e/vrt/__annotated__/karplus.legend.json` — only adsr/lfo/sequencer exist), so `face.order` literally cannot address it and BOTH gates are blind: module-face-lint's completeness enumerates params + declared families + legend statics (module-face-lint.test.ts:223-233) and faces-parity counts `data-cell-kind="family"` against `spec.controlFamilies`. Today an unpatched KARPLUS under `?shell=1` is MUTE with no way to hear it. GOES TO: dock page `pick`, first cell, via the new `karplus-strike-{n}` family + a `shell-cells.ts` `action` spec (PF-6f).
- position (Pos) — LEAVES THE LANE, rank 6 → 8. GOES TO: dock page `pick`. Argument from its own prose, not from generic reasoning: `docs.inputs.position_cv` (karplus.ts:179) says "Sample-and-hold it per step for a different pluck point on every note", and `docs.controls.position` (karplus.ts:203) says "It shapes the EXCITER, not the loop, so it colours each hit rather than the ring-out" — a per-step CV target, not a knob a player rides. Its CV jack keeps its rear home under `pick`.
- stiffness (Stf) — STAYS OUT OF THE LANE, rank 7 → 9. GOES TO: dock page `string`. It ships at 0 (contract-lock.txt:1447 `karplus param stiffness 0..1 linear default=0`), so at spawn the knob is inert-by-default, and `docs.controls.stiffness` (karplus.ts:205) frames the travel as leaving the instrument ("detuned metallic clang and bell") — a destination you set, not a ride knob. Lowest rank is correct.
- NOTHING IS DELIBERATELY DROPPED. Adversarial card grep (recipe Step 7) over KarplusCard.svelte for `<button|<select|<input|oncontextmenu|manualTrigger|Toggle|Selector|format`: exactly ONE hit — the `<button class="strike">` at :123-129 (plus `manualTrigger` at :42). The 8 `<Fader>`s map 1:1 to the 8 params. `<ModuleTitle>` is platform chrome (the shell paints its own `.tile-name`) and `<PatchPanel>` is the jack surface (the shell's RearCard on TAB). Post-change the face carries 9 of 9 on-card controls.
- The PLUCK is NOT in the lane, and that is a decision, not an oversight. ModuleShell.svelte:581 paints an `action` cell as `label={view === 'dock-full' ? cell.label : '▸'}` with NO `cell-cap` outside the dock — i.e. an unlabelled triangle in a 46px column, named only by a `title` tooltip. (Contrast the `selector` cell at :562, which falls back to `ctl.label` in the lane.) Ranking it 1-6 would buy a mystery glyph; rank 7 buys a properly-captioned `pluck` <Button> under a band header that names it. If ModuleShell ever captions lane action cells, promote it to rank 6 and demote `level` — that is the one-line revert.

**Contract delta (claimed):** EXACTLY +1 line. `contract-signature.ts:123-127` emits one `family` line per `controlFamilies` entry, sorted by id, AFTER the param/stereo/expose lines. karplus's block is contract-lock.txt:1427-1448 (22 lines, ending `karplus param tune 55..1760 log default=220 unit=Hz`), so the new line inserts at 1449, immediately before `kickdrum meta domain=audio`:

    karplus family karplus-strike kind=other prefix=karplus-strike

Shape verified against the two live precedents: `sixstrum family sixstrum-preset kind=other prefix=sixstrum-preset` (contract-lock.txt:2959) and `dx7 family dx7-preset-select kind=other prefix=dx7-preset-select` (:996). No `countParam` (one member), so no `count=` suffix.

NOTHING ELSE MOVES. `face` is explicitly OUT of contract-signature (graph/types.ts:512-516), so the re-rank, the page merge and the label edits are contract-transparent — the entire contract cost of this face is the family line. Accept with `flox activate -- task docs:accept`, then diff and confirm the ONLY changed line is +1449.

ATTEST: NIL. `packages/web/src/lib/audio/modules/karplus.ts` is not in `COLLAB_DIR_ROOTS`/`COLLAB_STANDALONE_SOURCE` (collab-attest-lib.ts:54-89), audio defs are not in the WebGL basis, and `art/scenarios/karplus/profile.test.ts:118` pins `dspSourceSha` over `packages/dsp/**` — NOT `docsStrippedRepoSourceSha` over the def. So PF-11 does NOT apply to karplus and no `docs-hash-ignore` markers are needed. (Correction to the plan's §2 generalisation: karplus is not one of the five pattern-3 defs.)

**VRT impact (claimed):** TWO scenes exist for karplus, both platforms, all four PNGs already committed (`e2e/vrt/__screenshots__/workflow-shell-faces.spec.ts/{darwin,linux}/face-karplus-{compact,dock}.png`).

• `face-karplus-compact` — DOES NOT MOVE, on either platform. The scene captures the lane tile at zoom 0.45 (LOD 'compact'), which selects the top TWO ranked controls. Today's order already leads `decay, brightness` (karplus.ts:112-113), and this spec does not touch ranks 1-2. Nothing in the compact tile changes. Do not regenerate it. (Note the `full` plate DOES change — POSITION is replaced by LEVEL — but no VRT scene captures the `full` tier, so that change ships pixel-unverified. That is a pre-existing coverage gap in workflow-shell-faces.spec.ts, worth naming, not worth fixing here.)

• `face-karplus-dock` — MOVES on BOTH platforms. Three superimposed changes: one section band removed (3 → 2), band 2's header text changes, and band 2 gains the PLUCK <Button> as its first cell, shifting four knob columns right by ~56 px each. The whole visible region below the hero glyph is repainted; expected diff is well past `DOCK_MAX_DIFF = 1500`.

EXEMPTION DRAINS: NONE, and this is a CORRECTION to the plan. The plan says "karplus's linux pairs are already drained — no dispatch", conflating two different things. Drained is TRUE (grep confirms no `linux/face-karplus-*` in `EXEMPT_BASELINE_PAIRS`; batch 3 drained them at vrt-exemptions.ts and the linux PNGs landed via the batch-3 dispatch). "No dispatch" is FALSE: the linux dock baseline now EXISTS and is STALE, and a stale linux baseline fails CI exactly like a missing one. A `vrt-update.yml -f ref=<branch> -f platform=linux` dispatch IS required.

Procedure, in this order:
1. `git rm e2e/vrt/__screenshots__/workflow-shell-faces.spec.ts/darwin/face-karplus-dock.png` and the linux sibling. Do this even though the diff "should" exceed tolerance — `--update-snapshots` only rewrites a snapshot when the comparison FAILS, and a sub-tolerance change comes back as a green dispatch that committed nothing (#1213/A2). Playwright always writes a MISSING snapshot.
2. Regenerate darwin locally (`flox activate -- task vrt:one -- karplus` with `--update-snapshots`), eyeball the PNG, flake-check 3×.
3. `flox activate -- gh workflow run vrt-update.yml -f ref=<branch> -f platform=linux`. Unscoped — never pass `-f grep=…` (startup_failure). Expect the follow-on run in `action_required`, not `queued`; approve it.
4. Same commit: `e2e/vrt/workflow-shell-faces.spec.ts:59` `{ type: 'karplus', pages: 3 }` → `pages: 2`.

vrt-meta linux-deficit RATCHET: UNCHANGED. It counts `EXEMPT_BASELINE_PAIRS` membership, and this spec adds and removes zero pairs. Do not touch the ceiling in `packages/web/src/lib/audio/modules/vrt-meta.test.ts`.

UNAFFECTED: `e2e/vrt/vrt-karplus-tomtom-states.spec.ts` (the LEGACY card scenes) — the card's rendered markup does not change. If the boy-scout range-derivation follow-up is folded in, re-check those scenes before pushing.

**CI delta (claimed):** +~20 s on CI — which BLOWS the plan's "+~3 s" estimate for Batch E, and the overrun is one deliberate spec.

Breakdown:
• faces-parity `karplus` row: 8 → 9 cells. The added cell is an `action` (one `toBeEnabled` + one `click`, faces-parity.spec.ts:506-511) rather than a knob drag (~14 CDP round-trips), so ≈ +0.2 s, not the ~0.8 s/cell the spec's own CI model uses for knobs. The derived per-face ceiling (`FACE_FIXED_MS + FACE_PER_CELL_MS * cells.length`) grows automatically — no constant to bump.
• The bespoke `?shell=1` audition spec: +~18-25 s. That is the whole overrun. It is a fresh boot (the spec's own measurement: 13.2 s cold under SwiftShader for the alphabetically-first face) + spawn + dock open + click + a ~1.5 s RMS window.
• VRT: +0 s — same two scenes, re-captured, none added.
• Unit lane (contract-lock +1 line, module-docs-lint, module-face-lint, shell-cells coverage): < 50 ms total, all pure-unit.

Recommendation: pay it. It is the ONLY assertion in the whole gate set that can distinguish a live PLUCK from a dead one, on the one module that is silent without it. Still far under the ~2 min owner-signoff threshold, but state it in the PR body rather than letting the batch estimate carry it.

Local pre-flight (CLAUDE.md, non-negotiable): `REPEAT=3 flox activate -- task e2e:one -- <the new audition spec>`, `flox activate -- npx --workspace e2e playwright test faces-parity --grep karplus`, `flox activate -- task test:one -- module-face-lint`, `… -- shell-cells`, `… -- module-docs-lint`, `… -- contract-lock`, `flox activate -- task typecheck`, and `flox activate -- task vrt:one -- karplus` to inspect the dock diff by eye.

**Risks (as authored):**
- faces-parity CANNOT prove the PLUCK works. The `action` branch (faces-parity.spec.ts:506-511) asserts only `toBeEnabled()` then `click()` — no observable effect. A PLUCK wired to a dead `onFire` passes the whole face green. This is the exact 'a rev-only probe passes on a dead button' class shell-cells.ts:123-127 warns about for PANELS — except `action` cells have NO probe mechanism at all. MITIGATION (required, not optional): one bespoke `?shell=1` spec that spawns karplus ALONE, opens the dock, clicks `shell-cell-karplus-strike`, and asserts audible RMS with the windowed MAX-HOLD already in e2e/tests/karplus.spec.ts:113-141 (`rms > 0.001`). This is CLAUDE.md's poly-module rule applied: a presence assertion does not count as coverage for the one thing the module cannot do without.
- The shell PLUCK is a SILENT no-op before the audio gate boots. `getActiveEngine()` (engine-ref.ts:23) returns null until Canvas.svelte:6991 registers the engine, and `engine.read` returns undefined for an un-instantiated node. The legacy card has the same hole but at least withholds its `strikePulse` flash (KarplusCard.svelte:39-48); the shell's <Button> runs its own press animation unconditionally, so it LOOKS like it fired. Consider a disabled state or a status caption; at minimum note it in the cell `title`.
- `e2e/vrt/workflow-shell-faces.spec.ts:59` pins `{ type: 'karplus', pages: 3 }` — a STRUCTURAL gate that fires BEFORE the pixel pin. It must become `pages: 2` in the SAME commit or both karplus VRT scenes fail with a page-count error, not a diff.
- VRT: `--update-snapshots` CANNOT rewrite a PASSING-but-stale baseline (the A2/#1213 hole). The dock diff SHOULD be large (a band removed + a new button cell + four knobs shifting ~56px), but 'should' is exactly the assumption that burned two dispatches on filter. `git rm` BOTH `e2e/vrt/__screenshots__/workflow-shell-faces.spec.ts/{darwin,linux}/face-karplus-dock.png` FIRST — Playwright always writes a MISSING snapshot — then regenerate darwin locally and dispatch linux. Treat a green dispatch that committed nothing as a red flag.
- The `karplus-strike` family is a FAMILY OF ONE whose card member does not follow the family convention. `ControlFamily` (graph/types.ts:429-430) documents members as `${testidPrefix}-${nodeId}-${i}`; KarplusCard.svelte:127 emits a BARE `data-testid="karplus-strike"`. It passes module-docs-lint.test.ts:241 only because that grep is substring PRESENCE-ONLY (its own comment says so). This is a deliberate, documented abuse of the family mechanism, chosen because the STATIC route needs a legend file karplus does not have. Say so in the def comment, and retire the family (contract-lock −1) when PF-6 `face.actions` lands.
- The authored `docs.controls['karplus-strike-{n}']` blob renders on the doc page as the row heading "Karplus strike {n}" — `+page.svelte:222` falls back to a raw controls table when `!data.face` (no legend), and `controlName` (:47-53) only turns `-{n}` into ` {n}`. Cosmetic and already precedented (sixstrum shows "Sixstrum preset {n}" today), but it is a real user-visible string.
- NOTHING keeps an `action` cell out of the LANE. module-face-lint.test.ts:528-563 gates PANELS dock-only by name and deliberately refuses to lean on the plate cap; there is no sibling rule for actions. Rank 7 here is a convention held by this spec's prose, not by CI — a future re-rank could silently put the unlabelled `▸` in the lane. Worth a follow-up lint clause.
- COMMENT ROT (recipe anti-pattern 6 — prose contradicting the code beneath it). Three blocks in karplus.ts must be rewritten in the SAME diff: :99-101 ("STIFF is rank 7 … LEVEL is last, as on every face" — both ranks change, and 'as on every face' is precisely the generic reasoning this program exists to kill); :103-105 ("the single most informative pixel budget on the tile" — TRUE at mini/compact/dock-hero, FALSE at `full`, where 6 cells ⇒ `rows=2` ⇒ `glyph = hasGlyph && rows <= 1` = false, module-shell-model.ts:340-342); :107-109 ("the string, the thing that hits it, the output" — now two pages, not three).
- The legacy card RE-TYPES every range as a literal. KarplusCard.svelte:101-104 (tune 55/1760, decay 0.1/10, brightness 0/1, stiffness 0/1), :110-112 (color 0/1, burst 0.1/4, position 0.02/0.5) and :134 (level -24/12) hardcode min/max/curve/units while only `defaultValue` comes from `defaultFor()`. I checked all eight value-for-value against the def (karplus.ts:75-82) — they AGREE today, so this is a latent bug, not a live one. But it is the exact backdraft class (CLAUDE.md: a card silently disagreeing with its def is invisible to contract-lock, module-docs-lint AND the range assertions, because all three read the DEF). Since this PR already touches the card to share `karplusStrike`, boy-scout it: derive the ranges from the def, or add a source-level grep guard.
- Page-id ↔ rear-group collision: CLEARED, but do not rename. `face.rear.groups` declares exactly one group, `id: 'voice'` (karplus.ts:152); the page ids are `string` and `pick`. No overlap. The hazard is real and unguarded-by-construction: rear-card-model.ts:262-269 lets a `voice`/`signal` group claim the leading slot AND :288 re-finds curated groups by page id WITHOUT consulting `usedGroupIds`, so a page id of `voice` (or `signal`) would build the same band twice and redden module-face-lint.test.ts:767 ("rear derivation rendered a port TWICE"). This is the dx7 `patch`-not-`voice` trap; karplus is safe only because neither page wants the word.

**Counts independently re-derived by the fact-checker:**

> Derived independently from packages/web/src/lib/audio/modules/karplus.ts: 8 params (tune/decay/brightness/position/stiffness/color/burst/level, :75-82 — every one log or linear, zero discrete); 12 inputs (trigger_in, pitch, accent_in, damp_in + 8 per-param CVs, :52-70); 1 output (out, :71-73) = 13 holes total; 0 controlFamilies today; face.order = 8 keys (:111-123); face.pages = 3 (:125-127); face.rear.groups = 1, id 'voice' (:152); face.rear.clusters = 2 (:155-156); audioRate = ['pitch'] (:158). contract-lock karplus block = 22 lines, 1427-1448; a family line sorts after params (contract-signature.ts serializeModuleContract order: meta, in, out, param, stereo, expose, family) so it inserts at 1449, immediately before `kickdrum meta domain=audio`. ART baselines = 2 (art/baselines/karplus/{out,melody}.f32). ALL of these agree with the spec's numbers — the +1 contract delta, the 4+4+4+1=13 rear count, the 8→9 faces-parity cell count, the ranks-1-6 lane budget, the 6-cells⇒no-glyph consequence (module-shell-model.ts:340-342), the no-page-id-collision check against rear group 'voice', and the pages:3→2 gate at workflow-shell-faces.spec.ts:59/:222 are all correct. No invented param ids, no silently dropped params.

**DEFECTS — fix these before building:**

- **[MAJOR]** The archetype's central premise — that karplus is the ONE voice with no internal exciter, that 'dx7/sixstrum/tidyVco self-oscillate', and that its `read('manualTrigger')` audition seam is 'why it is structurally unrankable today' — is contradicted by SIXSTRUM, which is an externally-struck string voice with the identical seam and the identical unranked hole, already shipped in STRICT_FACES.
