  - *evidence:* packages/web/src/lib/audio/modules/sixstrum.ts:377-384 declares `read(key){ if (key === 'manualTrigger') return () => { fireTrigger(strumCs, ctx.currentTime); } }` — the same shape as karplus.ts:288-295, with the comment 'On-card STRUM audition — fires one canonical trigger pulse at strum #1'. packages/web/src/lib/ui/modules/SixstrumCard.svelte:73-78 calls `e.read(node,'manualTrigger')` and :188-192 renders the audition `<button data-testid="sixstrum-strum">`. sixstrum does NOT self-oscillate: contract-lock.txt:2932-2937 declares `sixstrum in strum1..strum6 gate edge=trigger` plus `sixstrum in poly polyPitchGate` (:2929) — struck from outside exactly like karplus. sixstrum is listed in packages/web/src/lib/ui/workflow/strict-faces.ts with a shipped face whose order (sixstrum.ts:137-161) likewise never ranks its strum audition. karplus is therefore not the unique case the spec's whole rationale ('this is the whole point of the spec', 'the one voice in the rack that cannot make a sound by itself') rests on, and the correct framing is a PATTERN with a live unfixed sibling — which promotes the spec's own risk #5 (retire the family when PF-6 `face.actions` lands) from a footnote to the actual recommendation.

- **[MAJOR]** 'kick/snare/tom ship a `strike` press-PARAM' is false for two of the three named modules. Only tomtom declares one.
  - *evidence:* packages/web/src/lib/audio/modules/tomtom.ts:81 `{ id: 'strike', label: 'Strike', defaultValue: 0, min: 0, max: 1, curve: 'discrete' }` and :130 `momentary: ['strike']` → contract-lock.txt:3355 `tomtom param strike 0..1 discrete default=0`. kickdrum's FULL param list is contract-lock.txt:1481-1505 and snaredrum's is :3020-3041 — neither contains a `strike` param, and grep for 'strike|momentary' over kickdrum.ts returns ZERO hits and over snaredrum.ts only prose hits (no `momentary:` block on either def). The contrast the archetype draws — karplus is unrankable BECAUSE the drums solved this with a press-param — is supported by exactly one module, not three.

- **[MINOR]** The spec calls the bare `data-testid="karplus-strike"` a necessary 'deliberate, documented abuse of the family mechanism' and cites `sixstrum-preset` as its precedent — but the cited precedent actually FOLLOWS the documented convention, so nothing forces the deviation.
  - *evidence:* graph/types.ts:429-430 documents members as `${testidPrefix}-${nodeId}-${i}`. SixstrumCard.svelte:155 emits `data-testid={`sixstrum-preset-${id}-1`}` — the conventional form. KarplusCard.svelte:127 emits the bare `data-testid="karplus-strike"` only because no family existed when it was written, and the spec proposes to declare a family and KEEP the bare testid. Since the PR already edits that line to share `karplusStrike`, renaming it to `karplus-strike-${id}-1` costs one line and removes the 'abuse'. The spec's supporting claim is itself correct — I confirmed the grep is presence-only at module-docs-lint.test.ts:240 (`if (!cards.includes(f.testidPrefix))`) — but a weak gate is not a reason to deviate.

- **[MINOR]** The VRT ledger says 'TWO scenes exist for karplus' and names only vrt-karplus-tomtom-states.spec.ts as the unaffected legacy set. A THIRD committed baseline pair covers the legacy card and is omitted.
  - *evidence:* e2e/vrt/__screenshots__/vrt.spec.ts/darwin/karplus.png and .../linux/karplus.png exist on disk. e2e/vrt/vrt.spec.ts:55-57 builds `COVERED_MODULES = REGISTRY.filter(...)` — registry-driven, so karplus is enrolled without appearing by name (grep for 'karplus' in vrt.spec.ts returns nothing, which is exactly why it is easy to miss). The spec proposes touching KarplusCard.svelte twice (share `karplusStrike`; boy-scout the range derivation), so that pair belongs in the ledger.

- **[MINOR]** The 'the legacy card RE-TYPES every range as a literal' finding enumerates min/max/curve/units but omits `label`, which is the def-declared field that actually diverges TODAY (the ranges do not).
  - *evidence:* Def labels (karplus.ts:76,77,79,80,81,82): 'Decay','Bright','Stiff','Color','Burst','Level'. Card labels (KarplusCard.svelte:102,103,104,110,111,134): label="Dec", "Brt", "Stf", "Col", "Brst", "Lvl". Same one-source-of-truth class the spec correctly identifies for ranges, except this one is a LIVE divergence rather than a latent one — and it is the field that moves pixels, so a naive 'derive everything from the def' fix would move the vrt.spec.ts/karplus.png baseline the ledger omits. I did verify the eight RANGES agree value-for-value with karplus.ts:75-82, so that half of the spec's claim is correct.

- **[MINOR]** Two citation drifts.
  - *evidence:* (a) 'the three module-face-lint momentary clauses (module-face-lint.test.ts:391-439)' — the three clauses are at module-face-lint.test.ts:350, :391 and :419; the cited range covers only the last two. (b) 'Today's order already leads `decay, brightness` (karplus.ts:112-113)' — :112 is the `// hero ladder` comment; the keys are at :113 and :114.


---

## snaredrum

**Verdict: NEEDS_WORK** · archetype: Struck percussion VOICE with an internal MECHANISM (drum family — sibling to kickdrum/tomtom). No internal clock, no preset roster, no node.data: 22 ParamDefs, two strike inputs with DIFFERENT semantics (trigger_in edge:'trigger' → one hit; gate_in edge:'gate' → the two-hand roll engine), stereo audio_l/audio_r, glyph 'scope' (live analyser on audio_l). The archetype's distinguishing feature vs kickdrum/tomtom: it is the only drum with an ENGINE (roll_speed/bounce/humanize/spread), so the face must rank a mechanism, not just a timbre.

**Rationale (as authored):** WHY THIS ORDER (ranks 1-6 = the ENTIRE lane; faceTierCap('full') = LANE_PLATE_MAX_CELLS = PLATE_COLS*PLATE_MAX_ROWS = 6, curated-face.ts:46,65,76). The lane reads as two triads: WHICH DRUM (tune, wire, tone) then HOW IT ROLLS (roll_speed, bounce, damp). tune sets the size and everything tracks it (docs, snaredrum.ts:270). wire is the one knob that makes it a snare rather than a tom AND the master of a roll's sustain, because bed += wire x velocity (:277). tone tilts the whole instrument, voice gain 0.6->1.4 against the bed's 1.4->0.6 (:271).

THE PLAN'S BEST-EVIDENCED FINDING IS CONFIRMED BY THE CODE, exactly. Today's order (snaredrum.ts:155-167) is tune, wire, tone, head_decay, crack, roll_speed, bounce, level... — so slice(0,6) puts roll_speed in the LAST plate cell and leaves `bounce` at rank 7, i.e. rendered NOWHERE in the lane. You can set a roll's RATE but not its CHARACTER on the only module in the repo with a roll engine. bounce 7->5 and roll_speed 6->4 are correct and I adopt them unchanged. damp 13->6 is also correct: it scales head, body AND the wire bed together, x(1 - 0.6*damp) (:282) — one lane cell doing the work of three, and it is the natural counterpart to a buzz roll, whose tails are what turn a press roll into mud.

I REJECT the alternative of keeping `crack` at rank 5 in place of `damp`. Not because crack is unimportant, but because ranks 4-5 just made the ROLL the lane's headline, and damp is the only remaining knob that interacts with it (wire_decay is what makes a roll continuous, :279, and damp scales it); crack does not. Six coherent cells beat six important ones.

ONE COUNTER-ARGUMENT I CONSIDERED AND DECLINED: ranking `snaredrum-strike-{n}` inside the lane. It is the only way to hear the module without patching a sequencer, and at compact/full there is no dock. But compact's cap is 2 (LANE_ROW_MAX_CELLS_WITH_GLYPH, module-shell-model.ts:286) so it would have to displace tune or wire, and at full it would displace damp. The dock is one `shell-open-dock` click away, and the distinction that decides it is the same one that condemned bounce-at-7: a knob you RIDE during performance must be in the lane; a pad you press while dialling in belongs where the rest of the sound-design surface lives. Rank 7 — first of the dock-only ranks — records that priority without spending a lane cell.

WHY THESE PAGES (function order, and it is ALLOWED to disagree with rank — plan section 1 point 2). I adopt the plan's 5->4 cut and its page 1/2/3, and I REJECT its page 4 as specified. The plan's own section-1 rule says "Any merge that produces a 6+ control band whose members are three different ideas is rejected" — and its page 4 is 7 controls spanning three ideas (whole-drum scalers / bus saturation / output matrix). Fixed with PF-9 clusters instead of a fifth band, which is what PF-9 is for (~14 px caption vs ~81 px band, graph/types.ts:491-497). ModuleFacePage.clusters is ALREADY MERGED (types.ts:503, curated-face.ts resolvePage :184-211, ModuleShell.svelte:792-806 with data-face-cluster) — PF-9 is not pending work.

THE SIX CLUSTERS, verbatim, and note that resolvePage renders UN-CLUSTERED cells FIRST and clusters after, each cluster on its own .page-controls row (ModuleShell.svelte:786-806), so I authored every page.controls array in that same render order:
  page 'drum'  clusters: [{ label: 'pitch drop', controls: ['pitch_amt','pitch_time'] }]
      -> row [Tune, Damp, Head, Body] then PITCH DROP [P Amt, P Time]. One two-knob envelope (depth + settle time, :275-276) is the textbook "one idea, two knobs"; without the caption they read as orphans among four decay knobs.
  page 'snap'  clusters: [{ label: 'wires', controls: ['wire','wire_tone','wire_decay'] }, { label: 'crack', controls: ['crack','crack_tone'] }]
      -> no flat row; WIRES [3] then CRACK [2]. The band header already announces two things; the clusters let it keep that promise for 28 px instead of buying an 81 px band.
  page 'roll'  clusters: [{ label: 'one hit / hold to roll', controls: ['snaredrum-strike-{n}'] }]
      -> row [Roll, Bounce, Human, Spread] then the pads under their caption. Deliberately LAST, not first: because clusters always render after the flat row, listing the pad first in `controls` would not put it first, and last is right anyway — the four knobs are the roll's parameters, the pads are the instrument, and the caption is where a player learns on the FRONT what the rear cluster teaches at patch time.
  page 'bus'   clusters: [{ label: 'whole drum', controls: ['tone','damp'] }, { label: 'bus · out', controls: ['drive','hard','ceiling','width','level'] }]
      -> no flat row; WHOLE DRUM [2] then BUS · OUT [5]. tone and damp are the two scalers that touch all four layers; drive -> level -> per-channel ceiling with the side path carrying width is literally one signal chain (:287, :289-291). That is two ideas, cleanly, instead of one seven-knob dumping ground — and it is what actually dissolves the old 8-control 'tone · body' band the plan set out to kill.

Arithmetic: 6 + 5 + 5 + 7 = 23 = |order|. Every ranked key claimed exactly once, so dockFacePlan's '__unpaged' band never appears and module-face-lint's render-parity assert (every ParamDef exactly once, every family exactly once, nothing unbacked, :260-323) passes by construction. Height ledger: -81 px for the dropped band, +6x14 = +84 px of captions, net +3 px — the fold is NOT the argument here (plan section 1), grouping is.

REAR — verified hole-by-hole, zero orphans. Keep the curated `voice` group and its audioRate list unchanged; change ONLY the strike cluster label from 'strike' (snaredrum.ts:197) to 'strike — one hit / hold to roll'. Derivation under the new page ids: voice band 5 (trigger_in, gate_in | accent_in, pitch_cv, choke_in); 'drum' 6 (tune_cv, damping_cv, head_decay_cv, body_decay_cv, pitch_amt_cv, pitch_time_cv, in page.controls order); 'snap' 5; 'roll' 4 (roll_speed_cv derives in via its `_cv` stem, rear-card-model.ts:126-128 — the def comment at :177-179 is right); 'bus' 7 (tone_cv, damp_cv, drive_cv, hard_cv, ceiling_cv, width_cv, level_cv). 5+6+5+4+7 = 27 = the 27 declared inputs; +2 outputs = holeCount 29, well under REAR_COLLAPSE_THRESHOLD 60. Rear bands go 6 -> 5. NOTE the tomtom-style trap does NOT bite here: `pitch_cv`'s stem is 'pitch', which is not a param, so it would derive to the voice band anyway — and it is explicitly curated there regardless.

CORRECTIONS TO THE PLAN, file:line, most valuable first.
1. PF-6f AS SPECIFIED CANNOT SHIP — see risks 1 and 2. Its cost is a NEW shell-cell kind + a new faces-parity driveCell branch + a legacy-card edit, not "+1 contract line and a shell-cells action spec".
2. PF-2 HAS ALREADY LANDED. The plan lists it as a pending Batch A item ("param -> Toggle", plan line 86) and the snaredrum bullet says "hard -> PF-2 Toggle" (line 1021). `paramCellKind(p: ParamDef, momentary, tier, grid)` already takes the whole ParamDef and already returns 'toggle' for `looksLikeToggle(p)` (shell-control-kind.ts:125-139), and ModuleShell already paints `data-cell-control="toggle"` for it (:446-452). That bullet is a NO-OP. The live improvement is `hard.options` (PF-1, also landed) — see newParams.
3. THE PLAN'S PAGE 4 VIOLATES THE PLAN'S OWN SECTION-1 RULE. 7 controls, three ideas. Fixed above with clusters.
4. "New controls: none (PARAM_TABLE is exactly 22 rows)" (line 1026) is true of PARAMS only and sits in the same bullet that mandates a new controlFamily. The def has exactly 22 params (snaredrum.ts:95-119) — the count is right, the framing is not.
5. TWO OFF-BY-ONE CITATIONS, both harmless but both wrong: `gate_in` is worklet input 1 at snaredrum.ts:332, not :331 (:331 is trigger_in -> input 0) — the CLAIM is correct; and the VRT pairs are vrt-exemptions.ts:1008-1009, not :1007-1008.
6. THE DEF'S OWN COMMENT IS STALE and must be rewritten with the face: snaredrum.ts:134-139 says "Ranks 4-8 complete the full-in-lane face" and ":138-139 DRIVE deliberately ranks BELOW the identity set". Under PF-7's cap of 6 there are no ranks 7-8 in the lane at all. Also snaredrum.ts:128-131's "a glyph-bearing face fits two whole knob columns beside the trace" is true at COMPACT only — at `full` snaredrum renders 6 cells, rows = ceil(6/3) = 2, and `glyph = hasGlyph && rows <= 1` is FALSE (module-shell-model.ts:340-342). The scope glyph is ALREADY dead at the full tier today; this face costs nothing further. It stays live at mini (1 cell), compact (2 cells) and the dock hero.
7. The `spread` sign bug is REAL and confirmed: docs.controls.spread (snaredrum.ts:286) documents that the bed's pan term is summed with the OPPOSITE sign to the voices' constant-power pan, so a left-hand stroke throws its sizzle right. packages/dsp/src/lib/ fix + ART re-pin; out of scope; file it, and note that promoting the roll band to ranks 4-5 raises the odds a user reports it first.

WHAT NO GATE HERE CAN SEE (ask it of every gate). (a) The `full` lane tier — the six cells this spec re-cuts entirely — has NO VRT scene; only face-<type>-compact and face-<type>-dock are pinned (workflow-shell-faces.spec.ts:8-17). The single most visible change in this PR is pixel-uncovered. Do not add a scene in this PR: a new scene needs baselines on both platforms and the linux pair would RAISE the vrt-meta deficit ratchet, which only shrinks. File it as a program-level gap. (b) The dock capture is 1220x425 (the `.dock-faceplate` min(60vh,680px) cap) while snaredrum's dock content overflows it by a MEASURED 282 px (vrt-exemptions.ts:1030) — so roughly the bottom band is below the fold and invisible to the pixel gate. `hard`'s Segmented swap lands there. Its coverage is faces-parity's 'segmented' driveCell branch, not VRT. (c) Every gate in this system reads the DEF; SnaredrumCard.svelte's re-typed ranges are unseeable by all of them (risk 8).

```ts
face: {
  order: ["tune", "wire", "tone", "roll_speed", "bounce", "damp", "snaredrum-strike-{n}", "head_decay", "crack", "pitch_amt", "pitch_time", "damping", "body_decay", "wire_tone", "wire_decay", "crack_tone", "humanize", "spread", "drive", "hard", "ceiling", "width", "level"],
  pages: [
    { id: "drum", label: "drum \u00b7 head + body", controls: ["tune", "damping", "head_decay", "body_decay", "pitch_amt", "pitch_time"] },
    { id: "snap", label: "snap \u00b7 wire + crack", controls: ["wire", "wire_tone", "wire_decay", "crack", "crack_tone"] },
    { id: "roll", label: "roll \u00b7 two hands", controls: ["roll_speed", "bounce", "humanize", "spread", "snaredrum-strike-{n}"] },
    { id: "bus", label: "whole drum \u00b7 bus \u00b7 out", controls: ["tone", "damp", "drive", "hard", "ceiling", "width", "level"] },
  ],
  glyph: "scope",
  paramCells: none — do NOT declare face.paramCells. The only discrete param is `hard` (0..1 discrete default 0, snaredrum.ts:116); `paramCellKind` already resolves it to 'toggle' from its shape (shell-control-kind.ts:137) and a 2-cell picture grid would be absurd. See newParams for the `hard.options` recommendation, which is a ParamDef field, NOT a paramCells entry.,
}
```

**New params:**
- NO new ParamDefs. The def has EXACTLY 22 params (snaredrum.ts:95-119) and the legacy card exposes exactly those 22 (SnaredrumCard.svelte:137-215, 21 Faders + the HARD button). The plan's 'New controls: none (PARAM_TABLE is exactly 22 rows)' is correct about params — but it is misleading in the same bullet that mandates PF-6f, which is a controlFamily, not a param. Two different things; the face adds one family and zero params.
- RECOMMENDED, SEPARABLE, contract-transparent — `hard.options = [{value:0,label:'clean'},{value:1,label:'hard'}]` (PF-1, ParamDef.options exists at graph/types.ts:396). Range/default UNCHANGED (0..1 discrete, default 0). WHY: `hard` is not a boolean, it is a CHARACTER choice — docs.controls.hard (snaredrum.ts:288) says OFF = 'clean-warm tanh saturation, 2x oversampled' and ON = 'a wavefolder + bounded asymmetric shaper, 4x oversampled'. Today it paints as an anonymous <Toggle> and the legacy card prints 'HARD: OFF', which names neither state. With `options` it renders <Segmented> `clean | hard` at the dock (<=SEGMENTED_MAX_OPTIONS=6) and a KnobConic with a persistent readout in the lane (shell-control-kind.ts:133-136). Contract cost ZERO: contract-signature.ts:110 emits `param <id> <min>..<max> <curve> default=<v> unit=<u>` and never reads `options`. Gate-safe: `looksLikeSwitch` still true, `ACKNOWLEDGED_LATCHING['snaredrum:hard']` (module-face-lint.test.ts:341) STAYS and still satisfies the switch-classification ratchet — the render kind is independent of the momentary/latching classification (shell-control-kind.ts:120-123). faces-parity already has a 'segmented' driveCell branch. Ship it or drop it independently of the rest of this face.
- NO `face.momentary` entry for the audition. module-face-lint.test.ts:402 requires every `face.momentary` id to be a DECLARED PARAM; the strike pads are not params. `face.momentary` stays absent on snaredrum.

**Control-loss ledger:**
- ZERO ParamDef loss. All 22 params render in the dock exactly once — verified against the legacy card one-for-one: tune/head_decay/damping/damp/pitch_amt/pitch_time (card HEAD group, :137-144), tone/body_decay (BODY, :150-151), wire/wire_tone/wire_decay (WIRE, :157-159), crack/crack_tone (CRACK, :171-172), roll_speed/bounce/humanize (ROLL, :178-180), drive/hard/ceiling (DRIVE, :186-196), spread/width (STEREO, :208-209), level (OUT, :215). face.order ranks 22 params + 1 family = 23 keys; the 4 pages claim 6+5+5+7 = 23, so dockFacePlan's '__unpaged' tail is EMPTY and module-face-lint's render-parity assert (every ParamDef exactly once) holds.
- LANE DEMOTION, not loss — `head_decay` rank 4 -> 8 and `crack` rank 5 -> 9 leave the full-in-lane plate. Both keep a dock cell on page 1 / page 2. Justification: `damp` (new rank 6) scales head, body AND wire-bed decays together, x(1 - 0.6*damp) (snaredrum.ts:282), so ONE lane cell now moves all three tails where `head_decay` moved one. `crack` is a fixed ~6 ms tick level (snaredrum.ts:280) — an attack you set once, not one you ride.
- ALREADY LOST BEFORE THIS SPEC — `level` is at rank 8 today (snaredrum.ts:160) and faceTierCap('full') returns LANE_PLATE_MAX_CELLS = PLATE_COLS*PLATE_MAX_ROWS = 6 (curated-face.ts:46,65). So the def's own comment 'ranks 4-8 -> the full-in-lane face' (snaredrum.ts:134-139) is STALE by two ranks: `bounce` (7) and `level` (8) render NOWHERE in the lane today. Rewrite that comment to 'ranks 4-6'.
- CARD GROUP HEADERS dropped and replaced. The card's 8 headers over 3 bands (HEAD/BODY/WIRE | CRACK/ROLL/DRIVE | STEREO/OUT, SnaredrumCard.svelte:135-213) become 4 pages + 6 PF-9 clusters. Nothing is orphaned; the grouping is re-cut by producer intent, not by the card's flex layout.
- CARD LABEL ABBREVIATIONS dropped — the shell paints ParamDef.label, the card painted its own: GDmp->'G Damp', Dec->'W Dec', Amt->'Crack', Drv->'Drive', Sprd->'Spread', Wid->'Width', Lvl->'Level', Ceil->'Ceiling', PAmt->'P Amt', PTim->'P Time'. NET GAIN, and it fixes a real ambiguity: the card prints the literal string 'Tone' THREE times (tone :150, wire_tone :158, crack_tone :172) with only the group header to disambiguate; the def labels are 'Tone' / 'W Tone' / 'Ck Tone'. It is still a visible text change on every dock/compact baseline.
- CARD REAR SECTIONS re-grouped. The card's 9 PatchPanel sections (HEAD/BODY/WIRE/CRACK/ROLL/DRIVE/STEREO/OUT/PATCH, SnaredrumCard.svelte:113-123) become 5 RearCard bands + 2 clusters. All 29 holes present (27 inputs + 2 outputs) — derivation verified hole-by-hole, zero orphans, see rationale.
- NOTHING IS DELIBERATELY DROPPED. The face ADDS a control the card never had: there is NO audition button anywhere in SnaredrumCard.svelte — the only <button> is the HARD toggle at :187-193. Today the module cannot be heard without patching a sequencer into trigger_in or gate_in. The plan's own loss table (line 1190) says 'audition x2 (hit + roll) | none' and is CORRECT: this is an addition, not a remedy for a loss.

**Contract delta (claimed):** +1 line, exactly one, and I can name it: `snaredrum family snaredrum-strike kind=other prefix=snaredrum-strike`. It is inserted immediately AFTER `snaredrum stereo audio_l+audio_r` (contract-lock.txt:3042) and BEFORE `sourcery meta domain=video` (3043) — i.e. the new line 3043. That placement is forced by serializeModuleContract's fixed emission order meta -> in -> out -> param -> stereo -> expose -> family (contract-signature.ts:104-128); snaredrum declares no `exposableControls`, so `family` follows `stereo` directly. The format is verbatim from contract-signature.ts:126, and the existing precedent is `sixstrum family sixstrum-preset kind=other prefix=sixstrum-preset` (contract-lock.txt:2959). NOTHING ELSE MOVES: (a) `face` is deliberately out of contract-signature (graph/types.ts:512-516, module-face-lint.test.ts:20-21), so the entire order/pages/clusters/rear re-cut is contract-free; (b) `docs.controls` is not read by contract-signature at all; (c) PF-1 `options` is NOT in the param line (contract-signature.ts:110 emits only id/min/max/curve/default/unit), so the recommended `hard.options` is contract-transparent — record that decision in the PR body, as PF-1 requires. Accept with `flox activate -- task docs:accept` and review the ONE-line diff. IT IS +1, NOT +2 — the plan's Batch E header says '+2 family lines (karplus, snaredrum)', meaning one each across two modules; snaredrum's share is one, achieved by ONE family carrying TWO pads rather than two families (`snaredrum-hit` + `snaredrum-roll` would be +2 and would split a single idea across two dock cells). NO other attest moves: snaredrum's ART profile pins `dspSourceSha('snaredrum.ts','lib/snaredrum-dsp.ts','lib/snare-roll-dsp.ts','lib/dsp-utils.ts','lib/oversample.ts','lib/rbj-biquad.ts')` (art/scenarios/snaredrum/profile.test.ts:128-135), and dspSourceSha reads DSP_SRC_DIR = packages/dsp/src (art/setup/capture.ts:58-65) — the web def/factory is NOT in that basis, so PF-11 does not apply and the .sha does not move even though the factory gains two ConstantSources. The collab basis (scripts/collab-attest-lib.ts:54-104) contains neither audio module defs, nor shell-cells.ts, nor ModuleShell.svelte — no re-attest. snaredrum is an AUDIO def, so it is not in the WebGL attest basis and needs no docs-hash-ignore markers.

**VRT impact (claimed):** TWO scenes, one platform, and the two behave OPPOSITELY — read this before dispatching anything.

face-snaredrum-dock (darwin/face-snaredrum-dock.png, 1220x425): MOVES, massively. Page count 5 -> 4, page 1 loses `tone` and `damp` and re-orders, page 2 gains two cluster captions, page 3 gains a cluster caption plus a strike cell, page 4 is rebuilt. The diff is far over DOCK_MAX_DIFF = 1500 px, so the gate fires and `--update-snapshots` regenerates it normally. No `git rm` needed here — the CLAUDE.md git-rm rule is for SUB-tolerance changes, and this is not one.

face-snaredrum-compact (darwin/face-snaredrum-compact.png, 88x82): DOES NOT MOVE, and that is a prediction, not a hope. Compact's cap is LANE_ROW_MAX_CELLS_WITH_GLYPH = 2 (module-shell-model.ts:286, faceTierCap :77) and order[0..1] is `tune, wire` both before and after this re-cut. If the darwin compact scene DIFFS AT ALL, something unintended changed (chrome, glyph binding, a label) — treat a compact diff as a finding, not as churn to accept. COMPACT_MAX_DIFF is 150 px.

LINUX DRAIN — drain first, dispatch second, in this exact order. Both `linux/face-snaredrum-compact` and `linux/face-snaredrum-dock` are currently in EXEMPT_BASELINE_PAIRS (vrt-exemptions.ts:1008-1009 — the plan cites :1007-1008, off by one) and there is NO linux directory entry for either file, so both are MISSING, not stale. (1) In ONE commit: delete those two lines AND lower the vrt-meta linux-deficit ratchet (packages/web/src/lib/audio/modules/vrt-meta.test.ts:314-321) by exactly 2, with a ledger comment — that ceiling only shrinks. (2) Then `flox activate -- gh workflow run vrt-update.yml -f ref=<branch> -f platform=linux`, UNSCOPED (never `-f grep=`, it dies as startup_failure). Playwright ALWAYS writes a missing snapshot, so the compact pair lands even though its content is unchanged. (3) The bot's push lands the follow-on run in `action_required` — approve it, do not wait for a queue.

NO OTHER BASELINES MOVE. There is no rear-snaredrum VRT scene at all (workflow-rear-card.spec.ts has only dx7, sixstrum, tidyVco, vca on darwin; tidyVco, vca on linux) — so the rear band 6 -> 5 and the strike-cluster relabel are pixel-uncovered. The plain card scene `linux/snaredrum` (vrt-exemptions.ts:1577) is a separate, still-pending exemption for the LEGACY card; adding the two strike pads to SnaredrumCard.svelte moves the DARWIN card baseline for it, so regenerate that one too and leave the linux exemption alone (do NOT drain it in this PR — that would need its own capture and its own ratchet move).

STRUCTURAL GATE, same commit: `{ type: 'snaredrum', pages: 5 }` -> `pages: 4` at workflow-shell-faces.spec.ts:54. That assert fires BEFORE the pixel pin, so forgetting it produces a confusing structural failure rather than a diff.

BLIND SPOT, stated because it is exactly the A2/filter trap: the dock capture is capped at 425 px while snaredrum's dock content overflows by a MEASURED 282 px (vrt-exemptions.ts:1030), so roughly the last band renders BELOW the frame and is invisible to VRT. The `hard` -> Segmented swap lives there. Its only real coverage is faces-parity's 'segmented' driveCell branch. A green dock VRT proves nothing about page 4.

**CI delta (claimed):** +12 to +16 s, dominated by ONE new e2e case. Breakdown: (1) unit lane ~0 s — contract-lock gains one line, module-face-lint / module-docs-lint / curated-face are registry-driven sweeps that already iterate snaredrum. (2) faces-parity: snaredrum's row goes 22 -> 23 cells; the derived ceiling grows by FACE_PER_CELL_MS = 1800 ms under SLOW_RENDER / 600 ms locally (faces-parity.spec.ts:97-98,597), real cost <1 s for two pad presses. (3) workflow-shell-faces VRT: same two scenes, one fewer band to render — call it 0, possibly slightly negative. (4) THE COST: the third case in e2e/tests/snaredrum-roll.spec.ts (boot ?shell=1, spawn snaredrum + scope + AUDIOOUT, open dock, click HIT and poll RMS, then press-and-hold ROLL over a bounded window) ~= +10 to +14 s on one of ten e2e shards, matching the two existing real-chain cases at :107 and :149. Well under the ~2 min sign-off threshold. NOTE the audio poll window must be expressed the way the existing spec expresses it (windowed RMS over a bounded wall-clock poll of a real AudioContext, which is renderer-INDEPENDENT because it is audio-clock-driven, not rAF-driven) — this is not a WebGL frame-count case, so the frame-count rule does not apply, but do not invent a new ms budget: reuse the file's own poller at :39-70.

**Risks (as authored):**
- THE PLAN'S AUDITION SEAM IS NOT BUILDABLE AS WRITTEN — this is the single biggest correction. PF-6f says 'a one-member controlFamily + a shell-cells `action` spec', and the plan's snaredrum bullet says 'one family with two action cells'. Neither is expressible today. (a) ONE face key resolves to exactly ONE ShellCell (`shellCellFor(moduleType, ctl)` returns `SHELL_CELLS[type]?.[ctl.key] ?? null`, shell-cells.ts:240-243) rendered into ONE `<div data-cell-key={ctl.key}>` (ModuleShell.svelte:579) — there is no 'two cells from one family' path. (b) `ShellActionCell` is `{kind:'action', label, title?, onFire(nodeId)}` (shell-cells.ts:61-67) painted as `<Button onTrigger={() => cell.onFire(id)}>` (ModuleShell.svelte:578-587) — a CLICK. There is NO press/release, so `mode:'gate'` (hold-to-roll) has no representation. A ROLL you cannot hold is not the module's reason to exist. REQUIRED: a new `ShellStrikeCell` kind rendering 1-2 momentary pads, with `pads: [{suffix,label,glyph,title,mode:'trigger'|'gate',readKey}]`. Pads emit `shell-cell-snaredrum-strike-hit` / `-roll` (cellTestId is `shell-cell-${familyId}`, ModuleShell.svelte:376-378) — NEVER a `control-*` testid, exactly the PF-14 panel rule, because faces-parity asserts exact multiset equality of `control-*` against the 22 def param ids (faces-parity.spec.ts:555-562).
- A `panel` CELL IS STRUCTURALLY UNAVAILABLE HERE — worth stating so nobody reaches for it. `ShellPanelProbe.effect` is `{kind:'data'|'data-rev'}` and faces-parity reads it through `readData(page,nodeId,path)` off `w.__patch.nodes[nodeId].data` (faces-parity.spec.ts:212-224). snaredrum writes NOTHING to node.data — an audition fires a host-side ConstantSource on the ENGINE handle (the karplus `read('manualTrigger')` seam, karplus.ts:285-295). No declarable panel probe can therefore be satisfied. Do not route the audition through a panel to dodge the new cell kind.
- THE NEW `data-cell-control` VALUE KILLS THE WHOLE faces-parity SPEC IF IT LANDS ALONE. `driveCell` ends in `throw new Error('unknown cell control kind')` (faces-parity.spec.ts:533) and the CellControl union at :121-131 is closed — an unrecognised value fails the entire spec, not one row. The `'strike'` branch MUST land in the SAME PR as the cell kind and BEFORE snaredrum's face references the family. Same PR also updates `cells.length === params + families` accounting: snaredrum goes 22 -> 23 (faces-parity.spec.ts:584-588).
- faces-parity's EXISTING `action` PROBE IS THE DEAD-BUTTON CLASS THE PANEL RULE EXISTS TO KILL. Its whole body is `await expect(btn).toBeEnabled(); await btn.click();` (faces-parity.spec.ts:505-510) — it cannot distinguish a live audition from a button wired to nothing. Do not model `'strike'` on it. The honest split, and I am declaring it deliberately: the `'strike'` driveCell branch proves DOM press/release semantics (mouse.down -> `aria-pressed="true"`, mouse.up -> `aria-pressed="false"`, the momentary branch's shape at :325-348 minus the readParam calls, since there is no param to read), and the AUDIBLE proof lives in a third case appended to e2e/tests/snaredrum-roll.spec.ts, which already owns the exact machinery (`rmsOf` :33, the max/min-hold scope poller :39-70, and the two existing real-chain tests at :107 and :149). That third case: boot `?shell=1`, spawn snaredrum -> AUDIOOUT + a scope, open the dock, click HIT -> `l.max > 0.005` on both channels; press-and-HOLD ROLL for a bounded window -> sustained RMS with no silent window. Without it the strike cell has no operability gate at all.
- A HELD GATE PAD CAN LEAK AND THE DRUM ROLLS FOREVER. `mode:'gate'` opens a ConstantSource at GATE_HI ($lib/audio/gate-trigger exports GATE_HI=0.5, openGate:72, closeGate:77) into worklet input 1. If the release edge is missed — pointer leaves the button, pointercancel, tab hidden, node deleted mid-hold, dock closed mid-hold — the roll engine never stops. Close on pointerup AND pointercancel AND pointerleave AND blur AND visibilitychange, and unconditionally in the handle's `dispose()` (snaredrum.ts:375-380). This is the tidyVco `hold` stuck-value class relocated from the Y.Doc into the audio graph, where nothing reverts it.
- module-docs-lint FORCES A LEGACY-CARD EDIT. 'every declared controlFamily.testidPrefix actually appears in the card source' greps `allCardSource()` for the literal prefix (module-docs-lint.test.ts:231-247). `snaredrum-strike` appears NOWHERE in SnaredrumCard.svelte today. So the family cannot be declared without also giving the LEGACY card its two pads — which is welcome (the card cannot make a sound today) but it is real work the plan does not budget: the card needs the `engineCtx.get()` / `e.read(node, ...)` wiring the KarplusCard already carries (KarplusCard.svelte:38-48, testid `karplus-strike` at :127). Also required in the same commit: `docs.controls['snaredrum-strike-{n}']`, since snaredrum is in STRICT_DOCS (strict-docs.ts:380) and module-docs-lint.test.ts:175-179 fails an undocumented family. Keep the prose to identifiers that resolve (trigger_in, gate_in, roll_speed all do) — the no-unknown-identifier fact-check reads it.
- PAGE-ID / REAR-GROUP-ID COLLISION IS ONE TYPO AWAY. snaredrum's only curated rear group id is `voice` (snaredrum.ts:191). If any page were given `id: 'voice'`, rearFieldPlan would emit that band TWICE — once from the voice-slot branch (`curatedGroups.find(g => g.id==='voice'||g.id==='signal')`, rear-card-model.ts:262) and again from the page-slot loop (`curatedGroups.find(gr => gr.id === page.id)`, :288) — both times with the same holes, reddening the STRICT_FACES rear-derivation totality gate (module-face-lint.test.ts:749). The ids above (`drum`,`snap`,`roll`,`bus`) are all collision-free. In particular do NOT name page 3 'strike' if the rear cluster is ever promoted to a group.
- THE LEGACY CARD RE-TYPES EVERY RANGE. SnaredrumCard.svelte:137-215 hardcodes min/max/curve/units as literals for all 21 continuous params rather than reading the def (it uses cardParams only for `defaultFor`/`set`/`live`, :28). I checked all 21 against snaredrum.ts:95-119 and they AGREE today, so this is NOT a live bug and NOT a spec input — but it is precisely the backdraft divergence shape (a card silently disagreeing with its def), and no gate can see it: contract-lock, module-docs-lint and module-face-lint all read the DEF only. Fix while the card is open for the strike pads: source min/max/curve/units from `snaredrumDef.params` the way `defaultFor` already does. This is the boy-scout half of the PR.
- SIBLING TRAP IN THE SAME BATCH — the plan's karplus `order` is `['decay','brightness','tune','color','burst','level','position','stiffness']` (line 984 of the plan) with NO `karplus-strike-{n}` key, while its next bullet adds the family. module-face-lint.test.ts:226-229 fails that combination outright: "control family 'karplus-strike' not in face.order (need 'karplus-strike-{n}')". Batch E ships karplus and snaredrum together; rank BOTH families or both faces go red.
- RANKS 7+ HAVE NO RENDER CONSEQUENCE ON A FULLY-PAGED FACE, so do not spend review time on them. The only consumers of `face.order` are `curatedFace` (curated-face.ts:226-227) and `dockFacePlan` via it (:284); the sole call sites are ModuleShell.svelte:138 and :301. At the dock the cap is Infinity and the PAGES decide the layout; the `__unpaged` tail (:303-306) is empty because all 23 keys are paged. So `level` at 23 vs the plan's implied re-rank vs today's 8 is documentation, not behaviour. I rank the tail in dock reading order and say so in the comment.

**Counts independently re-derived by the fact-checker:**

> Independently derived from packages/web/src/lib/audio/modules/snaredrum.ts: 22 ParamDefs (:95–119 — tune, tone, damping, head_decay, body_decay, pitch_amt, pitch_time, wire, wire_tone, wire_decay, crack, crack_tone, damp, roll_speed, bounce, humanize, spread, drive, hard, ceiling, width, level). 27 inputs (:50–86 — 6 structural: trigger_in, gate_in, roll_speed_cv, accent_in, pitch_cv, choke_in; + 21 per-param `_cv`). 2 outputs (:89–90) → 29 rear holes, 1 stereoPair. 0 controlFamilies today. face.order = 22 keys (:155–167); face.pages = 5 (:169–173); face.rear.groups = 1 (id 'voice', :191); face.rear.clusters = 2 (:197–198); face.momentary absent; face.paramCells absent. Only discrete param = `hard` (:116). contract-lock.txt snaredrum block = lines 2990–3042 (1 meta + 27 in + 2 out + 22 param + 1 stereo = 53 lines); next line 3043 is `sourcery meta domain=video`. All of these AGREE with the spec's stated counts (22 params, 27+2 ports, +1 family line inserted as the new 3043). Card: 21 Faders + 1 HARD button (SnaredrumCard.svelte:137–215); all 21 hardcoded min/max/curve/units match the def exactly — the spec's "not a live bug" call is correct. Lane budget: LANE_PLATE_MAX_CELLS = PLATE_COLS(3) × PLATE_MAX_ROWS(2) = 6 (module-shell-model.ts:288–289) — ranks 1–6 only, spec respects it; rank 7 (`snaredrum-strike-{n}`) is dock-only. Glyph: laneBodyPlan(6, true, 'full') → rows 2 → glyph FALSE (module-shell-model.ts:340–342) — already dead today, spec's accounting correct. Page ids drum/snap/roll/bus vs the sole rear group id 'voice' → no collision. `snaredrum-strike-{n}` does NOT resolve in shell-cells.ts (SHELL_CELLS has only dx7 + sixstrum, :161–232) — the spec explicitly says so.

**DEFECTS — fix these before building:**

- **[MAJOR]** The delivered `pages` field omits the PF-9 `clusters` that the spec's own central argument depends on — a builder copying `pages` verbatim ships exactly the flat 7-control band the spec claims to reject.
  - *evidence:* Spec `pages[3]` = {id:'bus', controls:['tone','damp','drive','hard','ceiling','width','level']} with no clusters, which is byte-identical in membership+order to the plan's page 4 the spec says it 'REJECTS ... as specified' (plan .myrobots/plans/dx7-and-faces-design-program-2026-07-27.md:1008-1010). The six clusters exist only in the `rationale` prose. `ModuleFacePage.clusters` is a real merged field — graph/types.ts:503, resolved by curated-face.ts:186-204, rendered at ModuleShell.svelte:796-805 (`data-face-cluster`). Without them page 'bus' is a 7-control band spanning three ideas, which the plan's §1 rule at :63-64 ('Any merge that produces a 6+ control band whose members are three different ideas is rejected') rejects — the exact defect the spec charges the plan with.

- **[MINOR]** The vrt-meta linux-deficit ratchet citation points at the `it()` header, not the number to lower.
  - *evidence:* Spec: 'lower the vrt-meta linux-deficit ratchet (packages/web/src/lib/audio/modules/vrt-meta.test.ts:314-321) by exactly 2'. vrt-meta.test.ts:314 is `it('the linux-baseline deficit only shrinks toward zero', () => {`, :315 computes `linuxPending`, :316-321 is the assertion message. The literal to edit is `).toBeLessThanOrEqual(119);` at vrt-meta.test.ts:468. (I counted EXEMPT_BASELINE_PAIRS: exactly 119 `linux/` entries, so the '-2 → 117' arithmetic itself is right.)

- **[MINOR]** "Same PR also updates `cells.length === params + families` accounting: snaredrum goes 22 -> 23 (faces-parity.spec.ts:584-588)" is false — that assertion is registry-derived and needs no edit.
  - *evidence:* e2e/tests/faces-parity.spec.ts:588 asserts `.toBe(defIds.length + (spec.controlFamilies?.length ?? 0))`, where `spec` comes from `readSpec()` (:162) reading `__moduleSpecs`, which projects the live def — packages/web/src/lib/dev/module-specs.ts:154-168 builds `controlFamilies` from `def.controlFamilies`. Adding the family auto-updates both sides. Contrast the genuinely manual `{ type: 'snaredrum', pages: 5 }` → `4` at e2e/vrt/workflow-shell-faces.spec.ts:54, which the spec correctly flags.

- **[MINOR]** "There is NO press/release" overstates the platform gap — the momentary press/release primitive already exists and is already wired in ModuleShell.
  - *evidence:* packages/web/src/lib/ui/controls/Button.svelte declares `momentary?: boolean` (:18-19,:40), fires `onGate(high)` (:66), handles pointerdown/up/cancel/leave (:71-100) and emits `aria-pressed` (:127). ModuleShell.svelte:435-444 already renders `<Button momentary onGate={(high) => firePressParam(pd, high)}>` for `face.momentary` params. What is actually missing is a `mode`/`onGate` field on `ShellActionCell` (shell-cells.ts:62-67); the new-cell-kind cost is justified only by wanting TWO pads in ONE cell, not by the absence of press/release.

- **[MINOR]** The proposed `'strike'` driveCell probe contradicts the primitive it names: a `mode:'trigger'` pad has no `aria-pressed` to assert.
  - *evidence:* Spec risk 4: the branch should prove 'mouse.down -> aria-pressed="true", mouse.up -> aria-pressed="false"' for the pads. Button.svelte:127 is `aria-pressed={momentary ? pressed : undefined}` — a one-shot Button (the `onTrigger` path the spec itself cites at ModuleShell.svelte:584) emits no `aria-pressed` at all. The HIT pad is declared `mode:'trigger'`, so the probe cannot pass on it unless the new cell renders trigger pads as `momentary` Buttons — which the spec never states.

- **[MINOR]** The card-label-abbreviation ledger is enumerated but incomplete — it misses `wire`.
  - *evidence:* Spec controlLoss lists GDmp/Dec/Amt/Drv/Sprd/Wid/Lvl/Ceil/PAmt/PTim. SnaredrumCard.svelte:157 paints `label="Wire"` while snaredrum.ts:103 declares `label: 'Wires'` — a further visible text change on every dock/compact baseline that the ledger does not name. (The `wire_tone` 'Tone'→'W Tone' case is separately covered by the 'Tone three times' note.)

- **[MINOR]** Two plan citations are wrong, and one plan-vs-spec divergence is left unflagged in a section that claims to enumerate the corrections.
  - *evidence:* (a) Spec risk 10 cites karplus's `order` array at 'line 984 of the plan'; the array is at .myrobots/plans/dx7-and-faces-design-program-2026-07-27.md:970 — :984 is the PF-6f remedy bullet. The substance of the trap (no `karplus-strike-{n}` in that order + a mandated family → module-face-lint.test.ts:228 'control family not in face.order') is correct. (b) The spec's ciDelta of '+12 to +16 s' silently contradicts the plan's budgeted Batch E CI delta (plan:1328 '+2.4 s' for karplus+snaredrum+tomtom; plan:942 'CI: +~3 s') by ~5×; the gap is entirely the new e2e case the spec adds, but it is absent from the spec's 'CORRECTIONS TO THE PLAN' list.

- **[MINOR]** "snaredrum writes NOTHING to node.data" is literally false; the conclusion survives but the premise as stated does not.
  - *evidence:* SnaredrumCard.svelte:128 mounts `<ModuleTitle {id} {data} …>`, and ModuleTitle.svelte:20-28 documents that the rename field persists through the `node.data.name` channel, so a snaredrum node does carry `data`. The operative point (no snaredrum-SPECIFIC data key an audition could flip, so no satisfiable `ShellPanelProbe.effect` per shell-cells.ts:128-130) is still correct.

## sixstrum

**Verdict: CONTRADICTS_CODE** · archetype: INSTRUMENT (voice + strummer + chord-voicer in one), not a voice. 19 params, 22 in / 1 out, 1 existing control family. STRICT_FACES + STRICT_DOCS member. glyph 'scope'.

**Rationale (as authored):** WHY THIS ORDER. `order` stays a genuine PRIORITY ranking end to end, and I deliberately DROP the def's current 'the dock tail runs page by page' convention (sixstrum.ts:128-131) rather than extend it: a priority list that stops being one halfway is a trap, and PF-7 has already moved the lane cap once — whoever moves it next promotes ranks 7-9 sight unseen, and they should get the next-best controls, not whatever page order happened to put there. Ranks 1-6 are the plan's, with one argument the plan does not make and the code does: of the 19 params, exactly SEVEN carry a dedicated per-knob CV modulator (sixstrum.ts:71-80) — strumSpread, pickTone, pickGrain, spread, body, strumDir, quality. That list is the module's OWN declaration of what it expects to be ridden, and three of my six (strumSpread #1, pickTone #5, body #6) come straight off it; ring and material earn theirs as the two largest timbral levers (0.1-10 s sustain; the primary bright↔dark, whose damping filter tracks the note so it voices identically on every string). body#6 over register#7 follows from the same test: `body_cv` exists and its own doc advertises 'a wah-like body swell under an envelope', while REGISTER is a ±24-semitone transpose — changing which instrument you have, not playing it. The tail then ranks by liveness: strumDir #9 is inert at strumSpread 0 (docs.controls.strumDir: 'with STRUM at 0 all three sound identical'), muteDepth #10 is inert until a MUTE cable lands, quality #15 is inert until CHORD is patched ('It does nothing while CHORD is unpatched'), the audition #16 is design-time not performance, and envDecay is DEAD LAST because docs.controls.envDecay states it 'does nothing until you lower SUSTAIN' — which ships at 1.0. PRESET AT RANK 3, NOT 1 — and the plan's three reasons all check out against the source: (a) `sixstrumPresetName` reads back off `tuning` ALONE (sixstrum-preset-actions.ts:67-70, whose own doc comment says it reports 'the TUNING the instrument is in… not the preset is still pristine'), so it is a 3-state projection of one param, not a high-information readout; (b) `faceTierCap('mini')` = 1 and `laneBodyPlan` mini caps at 1 (module-shell-model.ts:330-332), so a rank-1 preset makes the ONLY control at the coarsest zoom a one-click destructive 14-param stamp — the dx7 precedent genuinely does not transfer, dx7's selector writes one `node.data.preset` slot; (c) it would evict `ring` from the 2-cell compact tile. At rank 3 it lands in the 6-cell plate and leads the first dock band, which is exactly what a recall wants. WHY THESE PAGES, AND WHY THE IDS. Function order, and it disagrees with `order` on purpose — the faceplate reads instrument → strings → hand → pick → envelope/body/out, i.e. the signal chain, while the ranking reads what your hands ride. Six pages become five by folding 'envelope' into 'output': on this instrument the amp ADSR and the box ARE one stage (strings → per-string ADSR → sum → LEVEL → then BODY, per docs.outputs.out), so that merge has a grouping argument, not a budget argument. PAGE IDS ARE DELIBERATELY UNCHANGED FROM TODAY (tuning, string, strum, pick, output) — only labels move, and 'envelope' is retired. That is the dx7 collision check done: sixstrum's ONLY curated rear group is `{ id: 'voice' }` (sixstrum.ts:195), no page id is 'voice' or 'signal', and `rearFieldPlan`'s page-slot loop (rear-card-model.ts:285-289) does NOT re-check `usedGroupIds` — a page id equal to a curated group id genuinely does build that band twice. Reusing the existing ids also keeps the rear churn to labels + order rather than ids, and note `id: 'tuning'` colliding with the PARAM `tuning` is harmless: `pageOfParam` is keyed by control key and `byPage` by page id, two separate maps. THE STRUM ACTION AT RANK 16, DOCK-ONLY, ON PAGE 3. It sits with the roll controls it demonstrates. It stays out of the plate on the same precedent the plan invokes for tidyVco's `hold` (tomtom ranks `strike` 9th) and for the same geometric reason: `laneBodyPlan`'s no-clip guarantee is derived from knob-column geometry, and in the lane an action cell degrades to a bare `▸` (ModuleShell.svelte:575) that says nothing. NO PANEL, NO GRID, NO NEW GLYPH. `paramCells` stays absent — 'grid' is for states that are PICTURES, and guitar/bass/harp are words. The `strings` glyph is demoted in §2 for good reason (it needs a worklet→main postMessage plus a read() seam), and 'scope' is honest here: SIX STRUM is gate-struck, so the trace shows a real plucked attack with the string's exponential decay. Note the plate carries 6 cells → `laneBodyPlan` gives rows=2 → `glyph = hasGlyph && rows <= 1` is FALSE, so there is no in-lane glyph at 'full' — that is already true today and this face does not change it; the glyph survives at mini, compact and the dock hero.

```ts
face: {
  order: ["strumSpread", "ring", "sixstrum-preset-{n}", "material", "pickTone", "body", "register", "tuning", "strumDir", "muteDepth", "spread", "stiffness", "pickGrain", "pickPos", "quality", "sixstrum-strum-{n}", "attack", "release", "sustain", "level", "envDecay"],
  pages: [
    { id: "tuning", label: "instrument \u00b7 tuning \u00b7 chord", controls: ["sixstrum-preset-{n}", "tuning", "register", "quality"] },
    { id: "string", label: "strings", controls: ["ring", "material", "stiffness", "spread"] },
    { id: "strum", label: "strum hand", controls: ["strumSpread", "strumDir", "muteDepth", "sixstrum-strum-{n}"] },
    { id: "pick", label: "pick", controls: ["pickTone", "pickGrain", "pickPos"] },
    { id: "output", label: "envelope \u00b7 body \u00b7 out", controls: ["attack", "envDecay", "sustain", "release", "body", "level"] },
  ],
  glyph: "scope",
}
```

**New params:**
- NONE. All 19 params already exist and the plan is right that there is nothing un-exposed. What IS added is contract-transparent PF-1 VOCABULARY on three existing discrete params, sourced from the card's own name tables so the def becomes the single source: strumDir (0..2 discrete, default 0) → 3 options down/up/alt (SixstrumCard.svelte:50 DIR_NAMES); tuning (0..2 discrete, default 0) → 3 options guitar/bass/harp (sixstrum-preset-actions.ts:34 SIXSTRUM_MODE_NAMES); quality (0..7 discrete, default 0) → 8 options maj/min/dom7/maj7/min7/sus4/pow5/oct (SixstrumCard.svelte:51 QUALITY_NAMES, cross-checked against docs.controls.quality). param-vocabulary.test.ts:120-133 requires options.length === round(max-min)+1 → 3/3/8 exactly; :136-141 requires defaultValue to hit a real option → 0 in all three. Renders: strumDir + tuning → <Segmented> at the dock (≤ SEGMENTED_MAX_OPTIONS=6), quality → <Selector> at the dock (8 > 6), all three keep the KnobConic + persistent option readout at every LANE tier (shell-control-kind.ts:126-130).
- ONE PF-4 PortDef.label, and it is NOT the plan's: `chord` gets `label: 'chord root'`. Reason from the code, not taste — `rearHoleLabel` (rear-card-model.ts:135-150) labels `chord_cv` from its paramTarget's ParamDef.label ('Chord' → 'CHORD') and labels `chord` from id derivation ('CHORD'). TWO REAR HOLES READ 'CHORD' TODAY. The def comment at sixstrum.ts:187-189 states the ambiguity in prose and nothing fixes it in the UI. portLine (contract-signature.ts:78-88) has no label branch → contract-transparent. (Equivalent alternative: rename the `quality` ParamDef.label 'Chord' → 'Chord qual', also contract-transparent, but it changes the knob caption too.)

**Control families:**
- sixstrum-preset — EXISTING (sixstrum.ts:310). Shorten `label` 'Preset — guitar / bass / harp' → 'Preset — guitar/bass/harp'. CONTRACT-TRANSPARENT (contract-signature.ts:124-127 emits id/kind/prefix/count only, never label). ⚠ The plan's REASON for shortening is WRONG — see risks[3]; the label does NOT render in the lane column, it renders as the dock `.cell-cap` (ModuleShell.svelte:558, max-width 220px, wrapping) and as the lane chip's title/aria text.
- sixstrum-strum — NEW, one member, `{ id: 'sixstrum-strum', label: 'Strum', kind: 'other', testidPrefix: 'sixstrum-strum' }`. The PF-6f audition fallback. testidPrefix greps clean: `data-testid="sixstrum-strum"` at SixstrumCard.svelte:192 (module-docs-lint.test.ts:236-244 does a whole-corpus `cards.includes(prefix)`). Requires a `docs.controls['sixstrum-strum-{n}']` blurb (module-docs-lint.test.ts:173-176, sixstrum is in STRICT_DOCS at strict-docs.ts:397) and a shell-cells `{ kind: 'action' }` spec.

**Control-loss ledger:**
- STRUM audition (SixstrumCard.svelte:188-194 `⟋` → the factory's `read('manualTrigger')` seam at sixstrum.ts:378-385) — TODAY UNREACHABLE under ?shell=1. RECOVERED as the `sixstrum-strum-{n}` action cell, dock page 3 'strum hand', rank 16 (dock-only, the tomtom-strike precedent). ⚠ ZERO factory change needed and NO new ConstantSource: the plan's 'verify strumCs does not defeat strum-1 normalling' item is ALREADY ANSWERED in-tree — sixstrum.ts:330-333 states the permanent `strumCs.connect(worklet, 0, 2)` IS the barre default ('an unpatched string normals to strum #1'), so input 2 reading as permanently patched is the shipped, intended behaviour and adding a second source would be the mistake.
- strumDir / tuning / quality NAME readouts (SixstrumCard.svelte:52-54 tuningName/dirName/qualityName) — today the shell prints `5` where the card printed `sus4`. RECOVERED via PF-1 `options` on the def (Segmented ×2 + Selector ×1 at the dock; persistent name readout on the KnobConic at every lane tier). Side effect worth having: the names stop living in three places (card :50, card :51, preset-actions :34) and become def-sourced — the card should then read `sixstrumDef.params.find(p=>p.id==='strumDir')!.options` instead of its own table.
- REGISTER drops OUT of the lane plate (rank 6 → 7). Goes to dock page 1 'instrument · tuning · chord', where it belongs next to TUNING and the PRESET that stamps it (0 / −12 / +7).
- MUTE DEPTH drops OUT of the lane plate (rank 5 → 10). Goes to dock page 3 'strum hand'. The plan's argument holds against the code: docs.controls.muteDepth says it is 'how much EXTRA amplitude choke the MUTE gates apply', i.e. it is inert until a MUTE cable lands — an always-dead lane cell. BODY takes its plate slot and always does something (default 0.35, docs.controls.body: '0 = dry, an exact passthrough').
- Card per-string rear SECTIONS (SixstrumCard.svelte:88-94, six `Str n` sections each holding STRM+MUTE) — DELIBERATELY NOT REPRODUCED. The rear keeps FUNCTION grouping (all six strums, then all six mutes) because the normalling rule is a property of the strum SET ('an unpatched string follows the nearest PATCHED strum at or below it', docs.inputs.strum1) and the choke is a property of the mute SET; splitting into six pairs makes both invisible. Zero jacks lost — 23 holes either way (workflow-rear-card.spec.ts:52).
- Card MODE fader's 14-param STAMP semantics on a control declaring `paramId="tuning"` — DELIBERATELY SPLIT, not carried over. See risks[7]: that conflation is a live bug, and the face fixes it by giving the recall its own Selector cell (rank 3) and leaving `tuning` a plain Segmented that swaps only the string set, exactly as docs.controls.tuning already promises.
- NOTHING ELSE. Every other card control is a Fader over a declared param and every one is ranked.

**Contract delta (claimed):** EXACTLY +1 line in packages/web/src/lib/docs/contract-lock.txt. The sixstrum block is currently lines 2916-2959 (44 lines: 1 meta + 22 in + 1 out + 19 param + 1 family). The new line is `sixstrum family sixstrum-strum kind=other prefix=sixstrum-strum`, inserted IMMEDIATELY AFTER line 2959 (`sixstrum family sixstrum-preset kind=other prefix=sixstrum-preset`) because contract-signature.ts:124 sorts families by id and 'sixstrum-preset' < 'sixstrum-strum'. Block becomes 45 lines. NOTHING ELSE MOVES: ParamDef.options is not in the param projection (contract-signature.ts:108-111 emits `id min..max curve default=X unit=Y` only), PortDef.label is not in portLine (:78-88), ControlFamily.label is not in the family line (:124-127), and all of `face` is out of the signature by construction. Accept with `flox activate -- task docs:accept`, then review the diff: exactly one added line, or something is wrong.

**VRT impact (claimed):** THREE darwin baselines, of which TWO move. (a) `darwin/face-sixstrum-compact.png` — EXPECTED UNCHANGED: compact = 2 cells + glyph, and ranks 1-2 (strumSpread, ring) plus glyph 'scope' are all untouched. Do not regen; run it and confirm green (see risks[8]). (b) `darwin/face-sixstrum-dock.png` — MOVES, substantially: 5 section bands instead of 6, new labels, reordered pages, two <Segmented> rows and one hero <Selector> replacing three knobs, and a new STRUM action <Button>. Regen locally with `REPEAT=3 flox activate -- task vrt:one -- sixstrum`. (c) `darwin/rear-sixstrum.png` — MOVES: the rear's five page-derived CV bands take their ids/labels/ORDER from `face.pages` (rear-card-model.ts:285-300), so the sequence goes strum→string→pick→tuning→output ⟶ tuning→string→strum→pick→output with three relabels, plus the `chord` hole reading CHORD ROOT. holeCount stays 23, so `workflow-rear-card.spec.ts:52 { holes: 23 }` is unchanged. The 'envelope' page is deleted but contributed NO rear band (it targets no CV), so the band count stays 6 (voice + 5). No 'full'-tier lane VRT scene exists, so the plate change (preset entering at rank 3, body replacing muteDepth/register) is pixel-uncovered — the faces-parity DOM sweep is its only gate. EXEMPTION DRAINS available: `linux/face-sixstrum-compact` (:1006), `linux/face-sixstrum-dock` (:1007), `linux/rear-sixstrum` (:1017); draining all three lowers the vrt-meta linux-deficit ceiling 119 → 116 (vrt-meta.test.ts:468) in the same commit. sixstrum is the only module in the whole 16-face program that needs a REAR re-capture. Unrelated and untouched: the legacy-card `EXEMPT_FROM_VRT['sixstrum']` entry at vrt-exemptions.ts:336.

**CI delta (claimed):** ≈ +0.5 s to +1.0 s wall-time, single-digit seconds worst case. Well under the ~2 min sign-off threshold. Breakdown: (1) faces-parity's sixstrum row goes 20 → 21 driven cells, ≈ +0.8 s at the plan's own measured ~0.8 s/cell on the SwiftShader CI runner — note FACE_PER_CELL_MS (faces-parity.spec.ts:98) is a derived TIMEOUT, not runtime, so the ceiling self-adjusts and needs no edit; (2) three params move from `dragKnob` to a Segmented/Selector click, which is CHEAPER, ≈ −0.3 s; (3) unit lane (contract-lock, module-docs-lint, param-vocabulary, module-face-lint, shell-cells) ≈ 0 — all pure, no new files beyond the two proposed assertions; (4) VRT scene COUNT is unchanged (compact + dock + rear already exist), so no new Playwright rows; (5) if the linux drain lands in the same PR, three previously-skipped VRT scenes start actually running on CI — that is ≈ +25-30 s of REAL work on the linux lane and is the honest price of the drain (it is coverage you are buying, not overhead).

**Risks (as authored):**
- ⚠ THE BUILDABILITY BLOCKER — PF-6f AS WRITTEN CANNOT SHIP. `ShellActionCell.onFire: (nodeId: string) => void` (shell-cells.ts:62-67) receives ONLY a nodeId, but every audition in the program (sixstrum, karplus, qbrt, kickdrum, snaredrum) must call `engine.read(node, 'manualTrigger')`, and `useEngine()` (engine-context.ts:22) is a Svelte CONTEXT accessor legal only during component init — a plain spec function cannot reach it. The plan put the engine seam in PF-6 (`readKey?: string`) and then told Batches B/E/F to ship on PF-6f, which has no seam. FIX (~15 LOC, platform, ships with Batch F): widen ShellActionCell to `onFire?: (nodeId) => void` XOR `readKey?: string`, and give ModuleShell's action branch (:572-581) a `fireCellAction(cell)` that resolves `params.engineCtx.get()!.read(node, cell.readKey)` — the handle already exists at ModuleShell.svelte:134 and is already used this way at :191 and :351. Size PF-6f S+platform, not 'S per module'.
- ⚠ faces-parity's `action` branch is DECORATION — it cannot fail on a dead button. e2e/tests/faces-parity.spec.ts:506-511 asserts `toBeEnabled()` then `click()` and returns; it never asserts anything HAPPENED. That is precisely the rev-only-probe pathology the plan outlaws for PANEL cells (shell-cells.ts:121-127). Do not lean on it. Close it with two PURE gates instead: (a) in packages/web/src/lib/audio/modules/sixstrum.test.ts (which already builds the real handle, see its 'factory — CV inputsMap' block) assert `typeof handle.read!('manualTrigger') === 'function'`; (b) in shell-cells.test.ts assert every `readKey`-bearing action cell names a key the module's factory actually serves, and that an action declares exactly one of onFire/readKey. Both browser-free, zero-flake, and negative-controllable.
- ⚠ THE PLAN'S PF-4 PROPOSAL FOR SIXSTRUM IS A REGRESSION — REJECT IT. §4-F says to label the twelve per-string jacks `1 2 3 4 5 6` twice and 'buy back per-string findability' from the rear cluster headers. But `PortDef.label` is ONE string consumed by TWO surfaces, and the second has no clusters: the lane drill-down (PatchPanel.svelte:1165-1183 INPUT drill) groups by CABLE TYPE only, so all twelve gate ports land under a single 'Gates' header and would render `1 2 3 4 5 6 1 2 3 4 5 6` — strictly worse than today's derived STRUM1…MUTE6. Ship NO per-string labels; spend the one PF-4 label on `chord` instead (see newParams[1]).
- ⚠ PLATFORM DEFECT FOUND WHILE CHECKING THAT: `rearHoleLabel` (rear-card-model.ts:135-150) returns the target ParamDef's label and NEVER consults `port.label` whenever `rearTargetParamId` resolves. So a PortDef.label on any of sixstrum's seven `*_cv` ports is SILENTLY IGNORED on the rear card while still taking effect in the lane drill-down (which calls `resolveVerboseLabel(port)` raw at PatchPanel.svelte:847) — one authored string, two different rendered labels. Do not author labels on paramTarget'd CV ports until that branch honours `port.label`.
- e2e/tests/p1-batch2-faces.spec.ts:60 PINS THE SIX PAGE LABELS as an exact array (`['strum · damp','string','pick','tuning · chord','envelope','body · out']`) and asserts `face-page` count against `pages.length`. The plan never mentions this file. It must be updated to the new five labels IN THE SAME COMMIT or the e2e lane goes red.
- e2e/vrt/workflow-shell-faces.spec.ts:52 `{ type: 'sixstrum', pages: 6 }` → 5. That is a STRUCTURAL gate (`face-page` count) asserted before the pixel pin, and it also catches the `__unpaged` failure mode: `dockFacePlan` appends a '__unpaged' band for any ranked key no page claims (curated-face.ts:303-306) and ModuleShell renders it as another `[data-testid="face-page"]` (:781), so a forgotten key shows up as 6 bands, not as a missing control. All 21 keys are claimed here; keep it that way.
- ⚠ LIVE CARD BUG, report it (boy-scout fix optional, but do not ship the face without recording it). SixstrumCard.svelte:157 renders `<Fader … paramId="tuning" readLive={live('tuning')} onchange={setMode} />`. Fader emits `data-testid="control-tuning"` (Fader.svelte:439) and is MIDI-assignable on `tuning`, but `setMode` → `applySixstrumPreset` writes FOURTEEN params (sixstrum-preset-actions.ts:87-90). So (a) a control whose declared identity is `tuning` writes 13 other params, (b) its motorized readback reads back only `tuning`, and (c) a learned CC swept across this fader fires up to 3 full 14-param `setNodeParam` bursts per detent crossing — the cloudseed MIDI-write-storm class PF-13 exists to guard. This is the card↔def divergence class in the repo standard, in its semantic rather than its numeric form. (The numeric form is CLEAN here: I checked all 19 ranges/curves in SixstrumCard against the def line by line and every one matches — they are re-typed, which is the hazard, but they currently agree.)
- VRT SUB-TOLERANCE BLINDNESS on the compact tile. `face-sixstrum-compact` should be PIXEL-IDENTICAL (ranks 1-2 stay strumSpread + ring, glyph stays 'scope', neither label changes) — but COMPACT_MAX_DIFF is 150 px on a ~7 k px image, so a real change could also hide under tolerance, and `--update-snapshots` writes nothing for a passing baseline (the A2/#1213 hole). Verify by RUNNING `flox activate -- task vrt:one -- sixstrum` and reading the result, never by dispatching and assuming.
- LINUX DRAIN ORDERING. `linux/face-sixstrum-compact` (vrt-exemptions.ts:1006), `linux/face-sixstrum-dock` (:1007) and `linux/rear-sixstrum` (:1017) are in EXEMPT_BASELINE_PAIRS → `test.skip()`-ed unconditionally, so CI (which runs linux) diffs NONE of sixstrum's three scenes today. The plan's cited line numbers (1005-1006, 1016) are off by one against the current tree. If Batch F drains them: remove the 3 pairs AND lower the vrt-meta ceiling 119 → 116 (vrt-meta.test.ts:468) in the SAME commit, push, THEN dispatch `vrt-update.yml -f ref=<branch> -f platform=linux` UNSCOPED (never `-f grep=`). The three linux PNGs do not exist on disk, so `--update-snapshots` will write them.
- DOCK BAND WIDTH — measured, not assumed. Page 1 carries a hero <Selector> (preset, min-width 168px, Selector.svelte:256), a 3-caption <Segmented> (tuning), a knob (register, 46px) and a second hero <Selector> (quality, 168px): ≈ 568 px + gaps against the ~856 px usable width at the 900 px `.faceplate-body` floor. One row. Page 3 ≈ 294 px, page 5 ≈ 326 px. No band wraps. Dock content shrinks ~80 px net (6 bands → 5); measured post-PF-8 overflow was 370 px, so it stays inside the 425 px faceplate cap either way and the dock screenshot still moves — the change is at the TOP of the faceplate.

**Counts independently re-derived by the fact-checker:**

> 19 params (sixstrum.ts:85-103); 22 inputs (:44-80 — poly, chord, strum1-6, mute1-6, accent, tone_cv/grain_cv/spread_cv/body_cv/strum_cv/dir_cv/chord_cv); 1 output (:82); 1 declared controlFamily 'sixstrum-preset' (:309-311). contract-lock.txt sixstrum block = 44 lines, 2916-2959 (1 meta + 22 in + 1 out + 19 param + 1 family); the new 'sixstrum family sixstrum-strum kind=other prefix=sixstrum-strum' line sorts after 2959 → 45. Spec's order = 21 keys (19 params + 2 families), no invented ids, no duplicates; spec's pages claim all 21 (4+4+4+3+6) so no '__unpaged' band. Page ids {tuning,string,strum,pick,output} do NOT collide with the sole curated rear group id 'voice' (:195). Ranks 1-6 contain no panel cell (panelCellKeys('sixstrum') is empty; the new action cell is rank 16, dock-only). 6 plate cells → laneBodyPlan rows=2 → glyph false at 'full' (already true today). ALL SPEC COUNTS AGREE WITH MINE.

**DEFECTS — fix these before building:**

- **[BLOCKER]** risks[0] — the spec's headline "⚠ THE BUILDABILITY BLOCKER — PF-6f AS WRITTEN CANNOT SHIP": it asserts that because `ShellActionCell.onFire: (nodeId: string) => void` receives only a nodeId and `useEngine()` is a Svelte context accessor, "a plain spec function cannot reach" the engine, and therefore demands a ~15 LOC platform widening (`readKey?: string` XOR `onFire`, plus a `fireCellAction` branch in ModuleShell) and a re-sizing of PF-6f from 'S per module' to 'S + platform'. The code already provides exactly this seam.
  - *evidence:* packages/web/src/lib/audio/engine-ref.ts:23 — `export function getActiveEngine(): PatchEngine | null`, whose file header (:3-11) says verbatim it is "A tiny process-wide accessor for the live PatchEngine, for code that runs OUTSIDE the Svelte context tree where provideEngineContext / useEngine apply" and that "callers poll it at the moment of use (a button click, a feedback-pump tick)". It is registered by Canvas.svelte:6991 (`setActiveEngine(e)`) / cleared at :7556, and is ALREADY driven from plain non-component functions on user interactions: PatchPanel.svelte:424 `getActiveEngine()?.setGateInput(nodeId, portId, high)`, clipplayer.ts:794/810/2097/2239, push2-control.svelte.ts:279, ElectraConnectButton.svelte:43. The audition target is reachable from a bare id two ways: PatchEngine.read(node,key) is public at engine.ts:2147 and merely delegates to the domain-level `read(nodeId: string, key: string)` declared at engine.ts:152 / implemented at :762; and a shell-cells-imported actions module already resolves node-from-id — dx7-patch-actions.ts:57 imports `patch` from '$lib/graph/store' and reads `patch.nodes[nodeId]` at :269. So the sixstrum audition ships today as `onFire: (nodeId) => { const t = getActiveEngine()?.read(patch.nodes[nodeId], 'manualTrigger'); if (typeof t === 'function') t(); }` against the UNCHANGED ShellActionCell at shell-cells.ts:62-67. Knock-on: risks[1] clause (b) ("in shell-cells.test.ts assert every `readKey`-bearing action cell names a key the module's factory actually serves, and that an action declares exactly one of onFire/readKey") gates on a field that should not be added, and the ciDelta/effort sizing inherits the same false premise.

- **[MINOR]** controlFamilies[0] cites the wrong evidence for its own correction: "⚠ The plan's REASON for shortening is WRONG — see risks[3]". risks[3] is about `rearHoleLabel` ignoring `port.label` on paramTarget'd CV ports and says nothing about where a ControlFamily label renders. No risk entry supports the claim. (The claim's SUBSTANCE is correct — I verified it independently — but the pointer is dead, so a reviewer following it lands on an unrelated finding.)
  - *evidence:* ModuleShell.svelte:562 `label={view === 'dock-full' ? cell.tag : ctl.label}` + :563 `compact={view !== 'dock-full'}`, and Selector.svelte:184 `{#if label && !compact}<span class="lab">{label}</span>{/if}` — at lane tiers `compact` is true so the family label is suppressed to title/aria (Selector.svelte:176,178). That is the real proof; risks[3] (rear-card-model.ts:138-152) is a different subject.

- **[MINOR]** Six line citations are drifted against the current tree. Each is individually harmless but the spec is sold on file:line precision and these are the refs an implementer will open first.
  - *evidence:* (a) risks[5] "e2e/vrt/workflow-shell-faces.spec.ts:52 { type: 'sixstrum', pages: 6 }" — actual line is :53. (b) controlFamilies[0] "the dock `.cell-cap` (ModuleShell.svelte:558, max-width 220px)" — :558 is `<Selector`; the cell-cap render is :567 and the `max-width: 220px` rule is at :972. (c) risks[0] "ModuleShell's action branch (:572-581)" and rationale "a bare `▸` (ModuleShell.svelte:575)" — the action branch is :578-587 and the `▸` literal is at :581. (d) newParams[0] "KnobConic + persistent option readout at every LANE tier (shell-control-kind.ts:126-130)" — :126-130 is the `paramCellKind` signature; the rule is :133-136 (`if (p.options?.length) { if (tier !== 'dock') return 'knob'; return p.options.length <= SEGMENTED_MAX_OPTIONS ? 'segmented' : 'selector'; }`). (e) risks[0] "`useEngine()` (engine-context.ts:22)" — declared at :21. (f) newParams[1] "rearHoleLabel (rear-card-model.ts:135-150)" — the function spans :138-152.

- **[MINOR]** newParams[0]'s render claim "all three keep the KnobConic + persistent option readout at every LANE tier" is vacuous for this face — none of the three params can ever reach a lane tier under the spec's own ranking, so the sentence describes a code path the face never exercises and could mislead a reviewer into expecting a lane-tier visual change (and into expecting the compact/plate VRT to move).
  - *evidence:* The spec ranks tuning #8, strumDir #9, quality #15. curated-face.ts:46 `LANE_PLATE_MAX_CELLS = PLATE_COLS * PLATE_MAX_ROWS` = 6 (module-shell-model.ts:288-289) and :65 `full: LANE_PLATE_MAX_CELLS`, with curatedFace slicing `ranked.slice(0, cap)` at :227 — so ranks 7+ are dock-only and shell-control-kind.ts:134's `if (tier !== 'dock') return 'knob'` branch is unreachable for all three on this face.
